We further provide a theoretical formulation of layer selection as an optimal stopping problem, showing that under bounded projection noise and dominant late-stage alignment perturbation, our search rule filters perturbation while bounding the loss relative to the oracle refinement layer. Experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts LLMs demonstrate consistent gains on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including GPQA-Diamond, Omni-MATH, and HLE, with zero memory overhead and less than 2% latency increase. These results suggest dynamically bypassing final-layer perturbations can unlock stronger reasoning behavior from aligned LLMs.

</details>


### [75] [Pre-Generation Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models via Soft-Target Attention Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21917)
*Amina Miftakhova,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在生成前通过模型内部表示检测幻觉风险，将传统二元分类问题重构为风险估计问题，并引入基于随机采样输出的经验答案错误率作为软目标监督，结合注意力探针方法，在多个QA基准和模型上显著优于线性探针和二元标签方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法通常对解码后的单个输出进行二元分类，无法在生成前评估风险，导致解码开销浪费。本文旨在提出一种在生成前就能准确估计幻觉风险的方法，支持提前拒绝、检索增强或路由决策。

Method: 1) 将幻觉检测重构为风险估计问题，而非二元分类；2) 基于模型随机采样输出的经验答案错误率构造软目标监督信号，证明该估计量是模型逐提示错误概率的唯一无偏最小方差估计量；3) 将注意力探针（attention probing）适配到生成前设置，使检测器能选择性聚合与幻觉相关的提示表示。

Result: 在三个问答基准和五个模型上，注意力探针在短答案任务中优于线性探针；用软目标监督替换二元标签进一步且持续地提升了检测质量。

Conclusion: 生成前幻觉风险检测应以风险估计而非二元分类为框架，软目标监督和注意力探针的结合能有效提升检测性能，为生成前的拒绝、检索增强和路由决策提供了更可靠的依据。

Abstract: Detecting hallucination risk before generation enables abstention, retrieval augmentation, and routing decisions without incurring the cost of decoding.
  While prior work has shown that such risk can be estimated from a model's internal representations, existing approaches treat this as binary classification over a single decoded output. We instead formulate it as a risk-estimation problem. Under this formulation, we introduce soft-target supervision based on the empirical answer error rate over stochastically sampled outputs - an estimator we prove to be the unique unbiased minimum-variance estimator of the model's per-prompt error probability under its sampling distribution.
  We further adapt attention probing to the pre-generation setting, enabling the detector to selectively aggregate hallucination-relevant prompt representations. Across three question-answering benchmarks and five models, attention probing outperforms linear probing on short-answer tasks. Replacing binary labels with soft-target supervision further and consistently improves detection quality.

</details>


### [76] [MindTailor: Personalized Emotional Support via Post History-Grounded Case Formulation and Collaborative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21930)
*Suhyun Han,Kyunghyun Cho,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MindTailor是一个通过分析用户在社交媒体上的历史发帖，构建案例表述（case formulation），并利用基于不同咨询策略的咨询师智能体协作批评机制来迭代优化回应，从而生成个性化情感支持回复的框架。在ReddiSupp数据集上，通过LLM评估、专家评估和用户研究，MindTailor在共情、个性化、理解度和整体偏好上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化情感支持方法主要捕捉用户当前状态（情绪、角色、情境），但忽略了塑造当前问题的形成性经历（formative experiences），即缺乏对用户历史背景的深入理解。

Method: 提出MindTailor框架：(1) 从用户历史发帖中构建案例表述；(2) 基于不同咨询策略的咨询师智能体协作批评机制迭代优化情感支持回应。同时构建了ReddiSupp数据集（798个Reddit帖子及其历史发帖记录）。

Result: 通过LLM-as-a-Judge评估、专家人工评估和用户研究，MindTailor在共情、个性化、理解度和整体偏好上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 融入用户历史背景信息进行案例表述，并结合多策略协作批评机制，能有效提升个性化情感支持回应的质量。

Abstract: As mental health concerns continue to rise globally, social media has emerged as a vital space where individuals seek emotional support. While prior work on personalized emotional support has leveraged seekers' emotional states, personas, and situational context, these approaches primarily capture the seeker's current state, overlooking the formative experiences that shape present concerns. In this work, we propose MindTailor, a framework that generates personalized emotional support responses by constructing a case formulation from the seeker's post history and iteratively refining responses through collaborative critique among counselor agents grounded in distinct counseling strategies. To enable research on this history-aware task, we construct ReddiSupp, a dataset of 798 Reddit posts paired with seekers' prior post histories. Through LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation, expert human evaluation, and a user study with seekers, we demonstrate that MindTailor outperforms baselines across these evaluations, improving empathy, personalization, understanding, and achieving the highest overall preference.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Value Benchmarks: Measuring Value-Structure Alignment in Large Language Models via Symmetric Q-Sorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21939)
*Jingting Zheng,Yuqi Ren,Linhao Yu,Yongqi Leng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Q方法论的对称人机评估框架，通过140项道德陈述排序任务测量LLM与人类在**价值结构**（而非逐项行为）上的对齐程度。对12个LLM进行240次复现Q分类实验，发现模型间存在显著异质性、生成随机性敏感性和局部错位，揭示了全局分数可能掩盖区域扭曲的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德评估依赖逐项行为指标，无法捕捉模型在多个竞争性价值之间进行**结构性权衡**的方式。需要一种能测量价值系统层面对齐的评估方法。

Method: 基于Q方法论构建对称人机评估框架：人类和LLM对相同的140项道德陈述进行九列强制分布排序；对LLM采用严格排序并确定性映射到Q分类桶；使用Procrustes相似度（φ）和RSA-based Spearman相关（ρ）量化结构对齐；在4个模型家族的12个LLM上以两种温度设置进行240次复现Q分类。

Result: 发现了显著的跨模型家族异质性、模型对生成随机性的特定敏感性以及局部错位现象；全局高分可能掩盖区域扭曲；基于排序和基于分桶的分析高度一致，但提示措辞引入显著方差。

Conclusion: 评估价值结构对齐为传统的逐项道德基准提供了关键的结构性补充，揭示全局指标无法反映的局部价值关系扭曲。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in contexts requiring complex moral reasoning and value trade-offs. However, existing evaluations typically rely on item-level behavioral metrics, which fail to capture how models structurally prioritize competing values as a cohesive system. To address this, we propose a symmetric human-LLM evaluation framework, grounded in Q methodology, to measure value-structure alignment. Under our protocol, humans and models sort an identical 140-item moral statement set into a shared nine-column forced distribution; for LLMs, we elicit strict rankings and deterministically map them to Q-sort buckets. Using a human reference sample ($N=35$), we establish a stable three-factor reference geometry specific to this instrument and sample. We evaluate 12 LLMs across four model families via 240 replicated Q-sorts at two temperature settings, quantifying structural alignment via Procrustes similarity ($φ$) and RSA-based Spearman correlation ($ρ$). Our results reveal significant cross-family heterogeneity, model-specific sensitivity to generation stochasticity and localized misalignment, which demonstrate that favorable global scores can obscure underlying regional distortions. While rank- and bucket-based analyses remain highly consistent, prompt phrasing introduces notable variance. Ultimately, assessing value-structure alignment provides a crucial structural complement to traditional itemwise moral benchmarks.

</details>


### [78] [Are Multilingual Models Actually Improving? Isolating True Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21954)
*Prasoon Bajpai,Eleftheria Briakou,Colin Cherry,Preethi Jyothi,Vihari Piratla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score的新指标，用于更可靠地衡量跨语言迁移强度，并通过对20个多语言模型和3个基准数据集的分析，揭示了关于迁移强度的几个关键见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨语言迁移强度度量指标将迁移改进与源语言准确性的一般性改进混为一谈，因此需要一种能够可靠捕捉迁移强度的替代指标。

Method: 提出Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score指标，在20个不同语言模型和3个主流多语言基准上进行分析。

Result: 1) 小型模型的迁移能力并未失效；2) 随着模型规模增大，跨语言迁移的进展速度慢于预期；3) 跨语言迁移能力随时间推移有明显的进步。

Conclusion: HAT Score能够更准确地评估跨语言迁移强度，并揭示了模型规模、时间进展与迁移能力之间的关系。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is a model's ability to generalize capabilities from well-represented source languages to under-represented target languages. Existing measures of a model's transfer strength conflate improvements in transfer with general improvements to accuracy in the source language. We advocate for an alternate metric that reliably captures transfer strength called Hardness Adjusted Transfer (HAT) Score, and use it to derive multiple insights on factors influencing transfer strength. Our analysis across twenty diverse language models and three popular mainstream multilingual benchmarks argues that 1) transfer in small models is not broken, 2) we are making slower than expected progress in cross-lingual transfer with model size, and 3) we have made clear progress over time.

</details>


### [79] [OpenBioRQ: Unsolved Biomedical Research Questions for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21959)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenBioRQ，一个包含12,553个未解决生物医学研究问题的检索增强型智能体基准。现有基准忽略了一个关键失败模式：模型会引用正确论文但该论文并不支持其主张（约15.9%）。该基准通过未解决问题（无答案键）作为忠实性与弃权探针，在前沿智能体模型上准确率仅17%-60%，并发现最困难问题上出现"智能体崩溃"（停止使用工具）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在根本性缺陷：当问题有固定答案键时，模型可以机械复现答案中的来源，而非独立验证来源是否真正支持论点。当前智能体模型虽极少编造引用（99%以上可解析），但约15.9%的引用指向错误论文，而现有基准完全忽略这一失败模式。因此需要一个无法通过"背答案"来作弊的评估方法。

Method: 构建OpenBioRQ基准：(1)收集12,553个未解决的生物医学研究问题（无答案键），覆盖12个领域；(2)开放性通过真实后续证据而非模型参数知识验证；(3)难度锚定于三个开放权重参考模型均无法回答的问题；(4)采用检索增强的智能体设置，模型需执行多次工具调用；(5)引入冻结的逐问题核对表以提高评判者间一致性（Spearman 0.35→0.82）。

Result: (1)与难度锚定模型同源的模型仅解决约17%；(2)独立前沿智能体（Gemini-3-Pro, Opus-4.7, GPT-5.5）准确率范围29-60%；(3)最佳智能体仍有33-40%未解决；(4)在困难问题上观察到"智能体崩溃"——模型停止使用工具，对最易崩溃的模型，完全阻断工具访问后得分几乎不变。

Conclusion: OpenBioRQ是一个困难、非饱和、能区分能力层级的基准，揭示了现有基准忽略的引用错误失败模式，并发现智能体在最需要工具的困难问题上反而停止使用的"智能体崩溃"现象，为评估和改进检索增强型智能体提供了新视角。

Abstract: A working citation looks like proof -- but the fact that a link resolves does not mean the cited paper supports the claim. I find that current agentic models rarely fabricate citations (over $99\%$ resolve), yet roughly $15.9\%$ link to the wrong paper. Existing benchmarks miss this failure mode: when a question has a fixed answer key, a model can reproduce the expected source from that key rather than independently verifying that the source supports the claim. I introduce \textbf{\openbiorq{}}, a retrieval-grounded agentic benchmark of $12{,}553$ unsolved biomedical research questions across $12$ domains that treats open questions as a faithfulness-and-abstention probe. To my knowledge, this is the first biomedical benchmark to combine an agentic setting -- where the model must issue multiple tool calls -- with unsolved questions that have no answer key. Openness is verified against real follow-up evidence rather than a model's parametric knowledge. Difficulty is empirical: I anchor it on questions that three open-weight reference models fail to answer, rather than on subjective hardness labels. On this hardest subset, held-out models from the same lineage as the difficulty anchors solve only ~17%, while three independent frontier agents (Gemini-3-Pro, Opus-4.7, GPT-5.5) span a wide 29-60% range. The benchmark is thus hard, non-saturating (the best agent still leaves ~33-40\% unsolved), and discriminating across capability tiers. Beyond difficulty, I observe agentic collapse on the hardest questions, where agents stop using their tools. For the most collapse-prone model, blocking tool access entirely barely changes its score -- so tools stop paying off exactly where they are needed most. A frozen per-question checklist raises inter-judge agreement from Spearman 0.35 to 0.82.

</details>


### [80] [Can LLMs Control Readability? A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation Framework for CEFR-Controlled Arabic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21981)
*Nour Rabih,Chatrine Qwaider,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多维度评估框架，用于检验指令遵循型LLM在生成符合CEFR标准的阿拉伯语文本时的能力。结果表明，结构化的CEFR引导提示结合词汇约束能显著提升文本的可读性控制，余弦相似度达0.91，可读性预测一致性达0.99，为非约束提示下的弱控制提供了对比基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能生成流利的阿拉伯语文本，但其在可靠地控制文本可读性等级方面的能力尚不明确。作者旨在探究指令遵循型LLM能否作为自适应语言学习中可靠的、符合CEFR标准的文本生成器。

Method: 构建了一个多维度评估框架，包含：(1) 受控提示策略（CEFR引导提示、词汇约束提示等）；(2) 基于Taha-19模型的可读性自动预测；(3) 词汇约束验证；(4) 句法复杂度分析。通过对比不同提示策略下生成文本与参考语言特征的匹配程度来评估效果。

Result: 结构化提示显著提升了CEFR对齐度。其中，CEFR引导提示结合词汇约束策略实现了最高的参考语言特征余弦相似度（0.91）和近乎完美的可读性预测一致性（0.99）；而非约束提示的控制能力较弱。

Conclusion: 研究为将可读性感知的阿拉伯语文本生成整合到自适应教育系统中建立了实证基础，证明通过合适的提示策略，LLM可以可靠地控制阿拉伯语文本的CEFR可读性等级。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent Arabic text, their ability to reliably control readability levels remains unclear. We propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework for Common European Framework of Reference for Language (CEFR)-controlled Arabic text generation, assessing whether instruction-following LLMs can serve as reliable generators for adaptive language learning. Our framework integrates controlled prompting, automatic readability prediction using a validated Taha-19 model, lexical constraint validation, and syntactic complexity profiling. Results show that structured prompting substantially improves CEFR alignment. In particular, CEFR-guided prompting with lexical constraints achieves the highest conformity to reference linguistic profiles (0.91 cosine similarity) and near-perfect agreement with predicted readability levels (0.99), while unconstrained prompting exhibits weak control. These findings establish an empirical foundation for integrating readability-aware Arabic text generation into adaptive educational systems.

</details>


### [81] [Adding Robust Code-Switching Capabilities to High Performance Multilingual ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21990)
*Enes Yavuz Ugan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯因子化适配方法，在不破坏单语基线能力的前提下，通过少量合成数据高效地将语种切换相关知识整合到预训练多语种ASR模型中，在语种切换词上降低32.87%的词错误率，整体WER改善5.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中大规模多语种ASR系统在语种切换任务上面临挑战，直接微调合成CSW数据会退化强单语基线的性能，需要在保持原有单语能力的同时扩展模型对复杂语种切换（包括跨语言形态变化）的处理能力。

Method: 提出贝叶斯因子化适配（Bayesian factorized adaptation），通过学习高效整合语种切换相关知识到强预训练模型中，避免覆盖已有能力，仅需少量合成数据即可实现。

Result: 在语种切换词上减少32.87%的转录错误，整体WER降低5.31%，同时保持单语性能不退化。

Conclusion: 有效的语种切换适配更依赖于知识整合策略而非数据复杂度，贝叶斯因子化方法能在不牺牲单语能力的前提下显著提升CSW识别性能。

Abstract: Code-switching (CSW) remains challenging for large multi-lingual ASR systems in real-world deployment. While fine-tuning on synthetic CSW data is possible, it generally degrades strong monolingual baselines. Our goal is to preserve these capabilities while extending models to handle complex code-switching, including morphological variations across languages. We propose Bayesian factorized adaptation, which learns to efficiently integrate switching-relevant knowledge into strong pretrained models without overwriting existing capabilities. Requiring only a small amount of synthetic data, our approach reduces transcription errors by 32.87% on code-switched words while improving overall WER by 5.31%, all while maintaining mono-lingual performance. Our results demonstrate that effective CSW adaptation depends more on knowledge integration than data complexity.

</details>


### [82] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion: A Japanese Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22009)
*Tomoki Koriyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对30多种大语言模型（LLMs）在日本语字形到音素（G2P）转换任务上进行了基准测试，发现最佳LLM的假名字符错误率低至0.52%，远超传统形态分析器（1.03%），并提出"解析模式"（先形态分析再规则转换）优于"直接模式"（直接预测假名），同时验证了LLM预测的假名用于kana-input TTS可获得更好发音效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G2P转换对于可控且鲁棒的文本转语音（TTS）至关重要，而大语言模型具备广泛的语言知识，提供了一种有前景的解决方案。作者希望在日语G2P任务上系统评估LLM的能力，并与传统方法进行对比。

Method: 在3000条人工标注句子上，对30多种LLM进行基准测试，与传统的形态分析器对比。设计了两种提示策略：（1）解析模式：LLM先进行形态分析，再通过规则进行假名转换；（2）直接模式：LLM直接预测假名读音。

Result: 最佳LLM的假名字符错误率低于0.52%，而最佳传统工具为1.03%。解析模式在大多数模型上优于直接模式。模型大小、版本和日语专门训练是关键因素。

Conclusion: LLM在日语G2P任务上显著优于传统方法；解析模式通过规则后处理减轻了LLM处理复杂发音规则的负担；将LLM预测的假名输入kana-input TTS比端到端TTS产生更好的发音效果。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion is essential for controllable and robust text-to-speech, and large language models (LLMs), with broad linguistic knowledge, offer a promising approach. We benchmarked over 30 LLMs on Japanese G2P, comparing them with conventional morphological analyzers on 3000 manually annotated sentences. We evaluated two prompting strategies: a parse mode, where the LLM performs morphological analysis followed by rule-based kana conversion, and a direct mode, where the LLM directly predicts kana readings. The results show that model size, version, and Japanese-specialized training are key factors, with the best LLMs achieving kana character error rate below 0.52\% vs. the best conventional tool (1.03\%). Parse mode outperforms direct mode for most models, as rule-based post-processing relieves the LLM of handling complex pronunciation rules. We also show that feeding LLM-predicted kana into a kana-input TTS yields better pronunciation than end-to-end TTS.

</details>


### [83] [NL2Scratch: An Executable Benchmark and Evaluation for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22061)
*Heejin Do,Alexandre Ballenghien,Yang Wu,April Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向Scratch代码生成的NL2Scratch基准，包含31万余个经过解析验证的自然语言-程序对，并设计了语义对齐一致性（SAC）指标用于评估语义匹配。实验表明，传统词法相似度指标高估了模型性能，模型在操作语义槽（动作、条件、数值参数）上存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2Code研究主要聚焦于文本编程语言，忽视了Scratch等基于块、事件驱动、视觉组合式编程环境的特性及其评估需求，缺乏相应的基准和合适评估指标。

Method: 从真实Scratch项目中提取程序并配对语义对齐的自然语言描述，构建311,648个解析有效的数据对；提出SAC（语义对齐一致性）指标，用于解释性的槽级语义匹配度量；基于SAC筛选出23,594个语义验证样本和800个槽平衡诊断基准。

Result: 在指令微调和微调LLM上的实验显示，即使token级F1超过0.93，模型也常未能达到完美的SAC分数，尤其在长样本上；错误主要集中在操作语义槽（动作、条件、数值参数），这些错误在传统词法相似度指标下难以被发现。

Conclusion: 传统词法相似度指标在Scratch代码生成评估中存在严重局限，SAC能更有效地揭示模型在语义对齐方面的真实缺陷，尤其是在操作语义槽上的问题。

Abstract: Block-based programming environments such as Scratch are widely used in early programming education, yet natural-language-to-code (NL2Code) research has focused primarily on text-based languages. Scratch programs are event-driven, visually compositional, and distributed across concurrent scripts, making conventional NL2Code assumptions and evaluation insufficient. We introduce NL2Scratch, an executable benchmark for natural-language-to-Scratch generation comprising 311,648 parser-valid NL--program pairs, whose program side is extracted from real Scratch projects and paired with semantically aligned NL descriptions. For reliable evaluation beyond surface overlap, we propose Semantic Alignment Consistency (SAC), an interpretable slot-level metric for measuring semantic agreement between descriptions and programs. With SAC, we construct a semantically validated pool of 23,594 examples, and a slot-balanced 800 diagnostic benchmark. Experiments across instruction-tuned and fine-tuned LLMs reveal a notable gap between lexical similarity and semantic alignment: models achieving token-level F1 above 0.93 often fail to attain perfect SAC, particularly on longer examples. Errors concentrate on operational slots like actions, conditions, and numeric arguments, exposing failure modes largely invisible under conventional metrics.

</details>


### [84] [Where Does the Signal Live? A Web Data Recipe for Medical Encoder Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22079)
*Bofeng Huang,Jacques Sun,Diane Bouchacourt,Nicolas Barascud,Fajwel Fogel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对密集术语领域（如医学）的编码器模型预训练，提出两种互补策略——医学术语密度过滤和信号增强改写，在法语医学NLP上验证了其有效性，构建了FineMed语料库和SOTA模型DoctoBERT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学等密集术语领域的编码器模型仍依赖小规模人工策划语料，可扩展性差且写作风格多样性不足，非英语场景下尤为严重。Web规模数据筛选是否有利于此类领域的掩码语言建模（MLM）尚不明确。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：1) 医学术语密度过滤：筛选富含医学术语的文档；2) 信号增强改写：利用LLM将文档重写为术语更密集、实体上下文更丰富的变体。在法语医学NLP上实例化，构建FineMed语料库并预训练DoctoBERT模型。

Result: 医学术语密度过滤在医学下游任务上优于广泛使用的教育质量过滤方法，且二者互补。信号增强改写本身相较于原始网页数据有提升，与过滤后的网页数据混合使用增益最大。DoctoBERT在DrBenchmark和临床NER任务上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: Web级数据筛选策略可有效提升密集术语领域编码器的MLM预训练效果，所提两种互补方法具有协同效应，为非英语临床场景的预训练数据构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Web data curation has been widely studied for decoder Large Language Model (LLM) pretraining. Encoders for dense-terminology domains such as medicine, by contrast, are pretrained on small, manually-curated corpora that limit scalability and writing style diversity, a bottleneck even more severe in non-English clinical settings. Whether web-scale data curation also benefits encoder Masked Language Modeling (MLM) in a dense-terminology domain remains an open question. To address this, we introduce two complementary levers. Medical-term density filtering selects documents rich in medical terms. Signal-amplifying rephrasing uses an LLM to rewrite documents into denser variants with broader entity contexts. We instantiate the recipe on French medical NLP. The medical-term density filter outperforms the widely-used educational quality filter on downstream medical tasks, and the two complement each other. Signal-amplifying rephrasing alone improves on raw web data, and mixing it with filtered web data produces the largest gain. The recipe yields FineMed, a French medical pretraining corpus, and DoctoBERT, a state-of-the-art French medical encoder family evaluated on both the public benchmark DrBenchmark and a proprietary clinical Named Entity Recognition (NER) task.

</details>


### [85] [Plurification in/of language technology -- The integration of culture in next-generation AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22097)
*Gertraud Koch,Fausto Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何在NLP中操作化"文化"概念，提出文化对齐不能仅通过添加更多"其他文化"的示例实现，而需要多元认识论——允许多种本地化的认知方式。通过五层技术活动模型分析，发现当前NLP研究虽在文化敏感性方面取得进展，但大多仍停留在输出或表征层面，未能解决权力、治理和社会语境等深层问题。论文结论是文化操作化需要超越技术适应，采用反思性的多元社会技术方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP系统在跨文化场景下表现不足，但如何将"文化"有效纳入NLP设计仍缺乏系统性框架。本文旨在厘清文化在NLP中的操作化路径，并揭示当前方法在认识论层面的局限性。

Method: 采用社会技术模型——五层技术活动模型，收集并系统化NLP中处理"文化"的各种方法，从认识论层面分析这些方法的完整性与局限性。

Result: NLP研究在文化敏感性方面确有进步，但多数方法仅停留在输出或表征层面对文化进行处理，未能触及权力结构、治理机制和社会语境等深层社会技术问题。

Conclusion: 操作化文化需要超越单纯的技术适应，应当采用反思性的、多元的社会技术方法，在计算形式化的潜力与局限之间寻找平衡，以容纳多重的语言和社会文化背景。

Abstract: The paper explores how "culture" can be operationalised in Natural Language Processing (NLP) and what this reveals about the possibilities and limits of considering a plurality of cultural backgrounds in technological design. It proposes that cultural alignment cannot be achieved only by adding more examples of "other cultures", rather it requires plural epistemologies: allowing multiple, locally grounded ways of knowing. To analyze how this plurality of knowing can be addressed in NLP, the paper uses a socio-technical model of language technology (LT) design, the five layers of technological activity model, for collecting and systematizing approaches to culture in NLP. The analysis shows that while NLP research has made progress toward more culturally sensitive systems, many approaches remain partial, addressing "culture" primarily at the level of output or representation while leaving deeper questions of power, governance, and social context unresolved. The paper concludes that operationalising culture requires much more than technical adaptation; it suggests a reflexive and plural socio-technical approach that navigates potentials and limits of computational formalisation for accounting multiple linguistic and socio-cultural backgrounds.

</details>


### [86] [From Recognition to Understanding: Unlocking Cognitive Time Series Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22126)
*Xin Qiu,Junlong Tong,Yao Zhang,Yunpu Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TSCognition多模态时序推理基准和TSAlign对齐框架，通过将时序数据编码为补丁级表征并与LLM语义空间对齐，在多项时序推理任务上超越现有基线，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分析与LLM结合的效果有限，根本原因在于任务形式化与LLM能力之间存在根本性错配：大多数设置将时间序列理解简化为曲线拟合，聚焦于低层次预测，忽略了真实世界时序决策中的语义、上下文和推理密集型特性。

Method: 构建TSCognition多模态基准（15个公开源、约41K QA样本、5类认知推理任务）；提出TSAlign统一框架，将时间序列编码为紧凑的补丁级表征，通过门控残差注入和多变量融合与LLM嵌入空间的语义方向对齐。

Result: TSAlign在TSCognition和TimerBed基准上优于现有LLM、VLM和时间序列QA基线，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过将时间序列理解从曲线拟合提升为认知推理任务，并设计有效的跨模态对齐框架，可以显著提升LLM在时序推理上的表现，且效率更高。

Abstract: Time series analysis has recently been coupled with Large Language Models (LLMs) to leverage their reasoning and world knowledge capabilities, yet gains remain limited. We attribute this to a fundamental mismatch between existing task formulations and LLM strengths: most settings reduce time series understanding to curve-fitting systems, focusing on low-level prediction while ignoring the semantic, contextual, and reasoning-intensive nature of real-world temporal decision-making.To address these limitations, we introduce TSCognition, a multimodal benchmark for multi-dimensional time series reasoning. It collects real-world time series and textual information from 15 public sources and constructs approximately 41K QA samples around five cognitive reasoning tasks: Decoding, Grounding, Inferring, Extrapolating, and Acting. Building on this, we further propose TSAlign, a unified framework that encodes time series into compact patch-level representations and aligns them with semantic directions in the LLM embedding space via gated residual injection and multivariate fusion.Experiments show that TSAlign outperforms existing LLM, VLM, and time series QA baselines on TSCognition and the publicly available TimerBed benchmark while substantially reducing computational cost.Code is available at: [https://github.com/EIT-NLP/CognitiveTSR](https://github.com/EIT-NLP/CognitiveTSR)

</details>


### [87] [BioMatrix: Towards a Comprehensive Biological Foundation Model Spanning the Modality Matrix of Sequences, Structures, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22138)
*Qizhi Pei,Zhimeng Zhou,Yi Duan,Yiyang Zhao,Wei Li,Han Guo,Liang He,Chengping Li,Chang-Yu Hsieh,Conghui He,Rui Yan,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BioMatrix 是首个在单一解码器架构中原生地整合分子序列（SMILES/SELFIES）、分子结构、蛋白质序列、蛋白质结构和自然语言的多模态基础模型，通过统一 Tokenization 将所有模态映射到共享离散 token 空间，仅用下一个 token 预测目标即可统一消费和生成所有模态，无需外部编码器或适配器。基于 Qwen3 模型，在 3044 亿 token 上持续预训练，在 80 个下游任务中的 77 个上达到 SOTA 或竞争力水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物基础模型无法同时实现原生多模态与广泛实体覆盖：多模态融合模型局限于单一实体类型，跨实体模型则要么缺失显式结构建模，要么依赖适配器设计导致模型不能原生生成其能读取的模态。BioMatrix 旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用统一 Tokenization 方案将所有模态（分子序列、分子结构、蛋白质序列、蛋白质结构、自然语言）映射到共享离散 token 空间；基于 Qwen3 语言模型（1.7B 和 4B）的纯解码器架构；仅用下一个 token 预测目标统一训练，无需外部编码器、投影适配器或模态特定输出头；在 3044 亿 token（涵盖通用与领域特定文本、分子/蛋白质的序列与结构视图、跨模态语料）上持续预训练。

Result: 在涵盖 6 个类别、80 个下游任务的全面评测中，BioMatrix 在 77 个任务上实现了 SOTA 或具有竞争力的性能，表明单一的多模态通用模型能有效匹配甚至超越跨广泛生物任务的专业方法。

Conclusion: BioMatrix 验证了在单一解码器架构中原生统一多种生物模态与自然语言的可行性，为开发真正的生物多模态通用基础模型提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We present BioMatrix, the first multimodal foundation model that natively integrates sequences, structures, and natural language for both molecules and proteins within a single decoder-only architecture. Existing biological foundation models pursue native multimodality and broad entity coverage separately: those that fuse multiple modalities under a shared objective remain confined to a single entity type, while those spanning multiple entity types either omit explicit structural modeling or rely on adapter-based designs in which the model cannot natively generate the very modalities it can read. BioMatrix closes this gap by mapping molecular sequences (supporting both SMILES and SELFIES notations), molecular structures, protein sequences, protein structures, and natural language into a shared discrete token space through a unified tokenization scheme, so that all modalities are consumed and produced uniformly under a single next-token prediction objective -- without external encoders, projection adapters, or modality-specific output heads. Built upon the Qwen3 language model (1.7B and 4B), BioMatrix is continually pretrained on 304.4 billion tokens spanning general and domain-specific text, sequence and structure views of molecules and proteins, and cross-modal corpora that interleave biomolecular entities with scientific text and link distinct entities through molecule-protein and protein-protein interaction data. After tuning on a comprehensive suite of downstream applications covering 80 tasks across 6 categories -- encompassing single-entity and multi-entity understanding and generation tasks across and within modalities -- BioMatrix achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on 77 out of 80 tasks, demonstrating that a single, natively multimodal generalist model can effectively match or surpass specialized approaches across a wide range of biological tasks.

</details>


### [88] [The Score Granularity Gap in Black-Box LLM Classification: A Comparative Study of Confidence Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22179)
*Ao Sun,Tian Sun,Jiaxing Geng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“得分粒度差距”概念，研究LLM作为黑盒分类器时，置信度得分可被阈值化的分辨率问题。通过比较7种置信度构建方法（从单次口头化数字到多次查询聚合）在25个模型-数据集对上的表现，发现单次口头化置信度虽然排序性能良好，但只取少量离散值，导致操作者只能使用少数粗略阈值。多次查询聚合能帮助弱模型但可能降低强模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM置信度是否校准良好或排序良好，但忽视了在实际部署中置信度得分能以何种分辨率被阈值化这一关键问题。作者提出“得分粒度差距”概念来回答这个问题。

Method: 通过控制实验比较7种置信度构建方法（从单次口头化数字到token概率再到多次查询聚合），在25个模型-数据集对（9个LLM，3个基准测试）上进行系统评估。

Result: 单次口头化置信度在正确转换为类别概率后排序性能良好，但只取少量离散值，导致粒度粗糙。多次查询聚合可帮助弱模型但会降低强模型性能。

Conclusion: 不同置信度构建方法在排序性能和阈值粒度之间存在权衡，论文将这些权衡转化为具体的部署指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as black-box classifiers in pipelines that automate confident decisions and route uncertain ones to human review. Such selective prediction needs a confidence score that an operator can threshold at a chosen risk level. Prior work asks whether LLM confidence is well calibrated or well ranked; we ask a complementary, deployment-oriented question that has been largely overlooked: at what resolution can the score be thresholded? We call the answer the score granularity gap. Through a controlled comparison of seven ways to build a confidence score, from a single verbalized number, to token probabilities, to querying the model many times and combining the answers, across 25 model-dataset pairs (9 LLMs, 3 benchmarks), we find that single-shot verbalized confidence, once correctly converted to a class probability, ranks cases surprisingly well, yet takes only a handful of distinct values. It therefore offers an operator only a few coarse thresholds, no matter how well it ranks. We show which constructions widen this gap, at what inference cost, and with what effect on ranking, notably that multi-query aggregation helps weak models but can degrade already-strong ones. We translate these trade-offs into concrete deployment guidance.

</details>


### [89] [When Is Emergent Consensus Real? A Measured Coupling Gain and a Validity Diagnostic for LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22203)
*Dongxu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出耦合增益γ作为可测量控制参数，揭示LLM智能体社会的共识/极化动力机制，证明极化只能被诱导产生，并开发了区分真实社会动态与模型伪影的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体社会研究展示了涌现共识或极化现象，但缺乏可测量控制参数、缺乏预测何时出现何种状态的理论、且无法检验结果究竟是真实社会动态还是模型伪影。

Method: (i) 定义耦合增益γ，通过反事实扰动邻居意见进行测量，验证其稳定性与跨模型区分性；(ii) 使用经典动力学模型（Friedkin-Johnsen、signed-Laplacian/结构平衡）拟合测量数据；(iii) 设计随机初始条件诊断方法，通过终态vs初态意见的(斜率, 偏差)区分真实平均化与模型先验伪影。

Result: (i) γ在五个前沿模型中跨度为0.15-0.43， paraphrase不变，社会邻居γ≈数字锚点γ，属证据耦合而非独特社会耦合；(ii) 前沿LLM不会自发逆火(β≤0)，极化只能被诱导产生；(iii) 对已发表"涌现共识"结果的诊断揭示：有争议主张上是真实平均化，已定论事实上是先验伪影；(iv) 成对γ无法预测多邻居结果，而模态匹配的群体耦合可预测(16个模型，Pearson r=-0.70, p=0.008)。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一套测量协议和有效性诊断工具，而非新理论，强调涌现共识必须从目标交互中的耦合读取。

Abstract: LLM "agent societies" are studied via demonstrations of emergent consensus or polarization -- with no measurable control parameter, no theory of when each regime appears, and no test of whether an outcome is a genuine social dynamic or a model artifact. We introduce the coupling gain gamma, measured per-agent by counterfactually perturbing a neighbour's stated opinion. (i) gamma is stable and model-distinguishing -- across five frontier models it spans 0.15-0.43 (n=20, 95% CIs <= 0.025), paraphrase-invariant; social-neighbour gamma roughly equals numeric-anchor gamma, so gamma is evidence-coupling, not uniquely social. (ii) Classical dynamics with measured (not assumed) coefficients organise the regime: Friedkin-Johnsen for consensus/pluralism, signed-Laplacian/structural-balance for polarization. (iii) Frontier LLMs do not spontaneously backfire (beta <= 0), so default societies do not self-polarize -- polarization is always induced; the beta>0 branch arises only in the FJ surrogate, never in the agents. (iv) A randomized-initial-condition diagnostic -- the (slope, bias) of final vs. initial opinion -- separates genuine averaging from model-prior artifacts (boundary-censoring ruled out by construction via interior-valued facts); applied to a published "emergent consensus" result (Chuang et al. 2023) it reveals a model-specific conflation: averaging on debatable claims, prior-artifact on settled facts. (v) Coupling is context-dependent: pairwise gamma does not predict multi-neighbour outcomes -- it can order them backwards -- whereas a modality-matched group coupling does (sixteen closed+open models, Pearson r=-0.70, permutation p=0.008). The regime laws take this matched coupling, not the single-neighbour gamma: emergent consensus must be read from coupling in the target interaction. We contribute a measurement protocol and a validity instrument, not new theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [On the Identifiability of User Adaptation in Co-Adaptive Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20569)
*Philip Waggoner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了共适应人机系统中的可辨识性问题，证明闭环编码器估计不能唯一识别用户适应性，而是反映联合系统的特性，并讨论了行为适应解释的含义及提出识别条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共适应人机系统中，对用户适应性的闭环编码器估计存在可辨识性问题，无法唯一确定用户适应行为，这阻碍了对人机交互中行为适应的准确理解和系统设计。

Method: 通过对闭环人机系统的可辨识性进行理论分析，研究编码器估计与用户适应性之间的映射关系，识别导致非唯一性的数学条件。

Result: 闭环编码器估计反映的是联合系统（人+机器）的特性而非纯粹的用户适应特性；用户适应在闭环条件下无法被唯一识别。

Conclusion: 研究者应谨慎解释闭环系统中的行为适应测量，需要新的实验设计或分析条件（如开环扰动）才能实现用户适应的有效识别。

Abstract: We analyze identifiability in co-adaptive human-machine systems. We show that closed-loop encoder estimates do not uniquely identify user adaptation, but instead reflect properties of the joint system. We discuss implications for interpreting behavioral adaptation and propose conditions for identification.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Fixed Budgets: Characterizing the Inelasticity and Limitations of Tree-of-Thought Reasoning Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20599)
*Atkia Mahila,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种代表性Tree of Thought搜索方法（DPTS和SSDP）在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的表现，发现DPTS存在低预算冷启动瓶颈，而SSDP则面临前沿耗尽问题，表明需要自适应搜索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Tree of Thought搜索已被证明能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但不同搜索策略在不同计算预算、模型规模和问题难度下的行为模式尚未得到系统研究，这限制了ToT方法在实际中的有效部署。

Method: 评估两种代表性ToT方法：DPTS（基于蒙特卡洛树搜索）和SSDP（基于语义去重），在Math500和GSM8K两个数学推理基准上，使用Llama-3B和Llama-8B两个模型规模，在3k-10k四种token预算下进行实验分析。

Result: DPTS在低预算时存在冷启动瓶颈，需要充分探索才能使价值估计可靠，但在高预算下扩展性良好；SSDP能高效达到候选解，但由于激进的节点合并导致前沿耗尽，永久丢弃未探索路径，无法随预算增加而改进。

Conclusion: 固定探索策略和固定剪枝策略在整个计算连续区间内都不够充分，有效的科学推理搜索需要能够根据搜索进展和可用资源自适应调整行为的策略。

Abstract: Tree of Thought (ToT) search has become a promising direction for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but deploying these methods in practice raises a question that has received little systematic attention: how do different search strategies behave under varying compute budgets, model sizes, and problem difficulties? In this work, we evaluate two representative ToT methods; DPTS, a Monte Carlo tree search based approach, and SSDP, a semantic deduplication based approach, across two mathematical reasoning benchmarks (Math500 and GSM8K), two model scales (Llama-3B and Llama-8B), and four token budgets (3k--10k). Our analysis reveals that the two methods exhibit limitations that pull in opposite directions. DPTS suffers from a cold-start bottleneck at low budgets: it requires sufficient exploration before its value estimates become reliable, making it a poor fit for resource-constrained settings despite strong scaling behavior at higher budgets. SSDP, on the other hand, reaches candidate solutions efficiently but is prone to frontier depletion; its aggressive node merging permanently discards unexplored paths, leaving it unable to improve regardless of how much budget remains. Together, these findings suggest that neither a fixed exploration strategy nor a fixed pruning strategy is sufficient across compute continuum. We argue that effective search for scientific reasoning agents requires strategies that can adapt their behavior based on search progress and available resources.

</details>


### [92] [The New Associationism: Lessons from Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20600)
*Daniel Rothschild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现代AI的成功支持一种温和的联想主义：监督学习作为一种统一的、渐进的、错误驱动的学习机制广泛适用于从语言模型到游戏智能体的多种系统，证明了联想机制足以解释人类认知能力，但深度学习依赖的计算架构远超经典联想主义的设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代AI的成功对人类学习机制意味着什么，回应联想主义是否过于局限而无法解释人类认知能力的经典争论。

Method: 通过分析监督学习（基于评价反馈的学习）在当代AI系统中的广泛适用性，从大语言模型到游戏智能体，论证其作为统一学习机制的核心地位，同时指出不同系统在生成反馈信号所需工作量上的差异。

Result: 监督学习在多种AI系统中普遍存在，支持联想主义的核心理念（统一、渐进、错误驱动的跨领域学习机制），但深度学习架构超越了经典联想主义的范畴，且监督学习仅是学习的一个组成部分而非全部。

Conclusion: 现代AI的成功为一种温和的联想主义提供了有力支持——联想机制足以解释人类认知能力，但需要结合更复杂的计算架构，不能将学习完全简化为单一的联想机制。

Abstract: What can the success of modern AI tell us about how humans learn? This paper argues that taking AI seriously as a model of human learning supports a modest but genuine associationism. The central finding is that supervised learning -- learning driven by evaluative feedback -- underlies a surprisingly wide range of contemporary AI systems, from large language models to game-playing agents, differing primarily in how much work is required to generate the relevant feedback signal. This vindicates associationist ideals of a uniform, gradual, error-driven learning mechanism operating across domains, and defuses the once-influential argument that associationist mechanisms are too limited to account for human cognitive capacities. At the same time, the successes of deep learning depend on computational architectures that go well beyond anything classical associationists envisaged, and supervised learning operates within these as one component rather than a complete account of learning.

</details>


### [93] [Specifying AI-SDLC Processes: A Protocol Language for Human-Agent Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20615)
*Ylli Prifti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向AI-SDLC流程的领域特定语言（DSL），用于表达人机协作中的责任边界、审批门控和治理约束。该语言通过策略与机制分离、验证令牌和能力边界等原语约束流程非确定性，并形式化了2+N团队模式和Kleene闭包编排等设计属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理参与软件开发生命周期时，缺乏专门的语言来规范人机之间的责任边界、审批门控和治理约束；现有方法要么将流程编码在提示词中（易漂移），要么针对相邻领域（工作流管理、业务流程），或仅处理碎片化问题（访问控制、审批门控）。

Method: 提出一种领域特定语言，包含形式化语法、良构条件、操作语义和执行不变性。语言区分策略（声明意图）与机制（结构强制），通过验证令牌和能力边界等原语约束流程非确定性。

Result: 1）失效分析表明结构强制将系统失效率约束为代理与验证器失效率的加权乘积，而行为合规导致累积或近饱和增长；2）2+N团队模式形式化了AI-SDLC中的经典职责分离；3）编排循环的Kleene闭包和协议自反性验证成为设计属性而非特例构造。

Conclusion: 已实现可行原型，该贡献定位为与MetaGPT、FlowAgent/BPMN扩展和基于能力的SAGA等框架的特定集成，而非单个原语的新颖性；实证评估为未来工作。

Abstract: AI agents now participate as first-class team members across the software development lifecycle, yet no specification language exists for expressing the human-agent responsibility boundaries, approval gates, and governance constraints this collaboration requires. Existing approaches encode process in agent prompts (subject to drift), target adjacent domains (workflow management, business processes), or address only fragments (access control, approval gates).
  We propose a domain-specific language for specifying AI-SDLC processes as protocols, with formal syntax, well-formedness conditions, operational semantics, and enforcement invariants. The language distinguishes policy (declared intent) from mechanism (structural enforcement), enabling implementations to bound process non-determinism through primitives such as validation tokens and capability boundaries.
  Three results follow. A failure rate analysis shows that structural enforcement bounds system failure rates at a weighted product of agent and validator rates, while behavioral compliance permits cumulative or near-saturating growth. The 2+N team pattern (two human-in-control roles plus N specialized agent members) formalizes classical Separation of Duties for AI-SDLC. Kleene closure of orchestration loops and reflexive protocol-adherence validation emerge as design properties rather than special-case constructs.
  We position the contribution against multi-agent frameworks (MetaGPT), workflow specification (FlowAgent, BPMN extensions), and capability-based security (SAGA): the novelty lies in the specific integration, not any single primitive. A working implementation demonstrates feasibility; empirical evaluation is future work.

</details>


### [94] [PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621)
*Yang Feng,Ziwei Xu,Xia Hu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEAR是一种推理时协议，通过动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑，解决多智能体辩论中固定拓扑导致的定位偏差、不可靠智能体放大和角色分配敏感问题，理论上被表征为等变稀疏路由器，在四个推理基准和六个LLM骨干上优于最强辩论基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定拓扑的多智能体辩论存在持续的位置偏差、不可靠智能体被放大以及角色分配高度敏感等问题，限制了辩论系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出置换等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议，在连续辩论轮次中动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑，基于演化的智能体状态策略性地切换智能体到角色的分配，防止任何智能体永久占据特权网络位置，实现更均衡的影响力分布。

Result: 在四个推理基准和六个不同的LLM骨干上，PEAR在平均准确率上显著优于最强的辩论基线方法。

Conclusion: PEAR作为一种等变稀疏路由器，在保持智能体重标定下准确性的同时，降低了路由复杂度并提高了泛化能力，有效提升了多智能体辩论系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves the reliability of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer critiques. However, fixed topologies often introduce persistent positional biases, amplify unreliable agents, and cause high sensitivity to role assignments. We introduce \textit{Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate (PEAR)}, an inference-time protocol that dynamically reconfigures communication roles and sparse topologies across consecutive debate rounds. By strategically switching agent-to-role assignments based on evolving agent states, PEAR prevents any agent from permanently occupying a privileged network position or distributes influence more evenly across the debate. We theoretically characterize PEAR as an equivariant sparse router: it preserves accuracy under agent relabeling while reducing routing complexity and improving generalization. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across four reasoning benchmarks and six diverse LLM backbones demonstrate PEAR significantly improves average accuracy over the strongest debate baselines. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR.

</details>


### [95] [Darwin Mobile Agent: A Roadmap for Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20622)
*Daniel Beechey,Derek Yuen,Jianheng Liu,Dezhao Luo,Tiantian He,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang,Kun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出移动GUI作为通向通用人工智能的“大世界”代理，并通过Darwin Mobile Agent框架实现去人类先验的自主强化学习，验证了其在大规模策略优化中的稳定性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统依赖大量人类先验（如手工设计的任务、奖励函数和数据标注），限制了智能体在开放环境中实现通用、自适应行为的能力。作者旨在通过系统性地移除人类先验，让智能在与复杂世界的交互中自然涌现智能。

Method: 提出移动GUI作为复杂“大世界”的代理；构建Darwin Mobile Agent开源框架，采用异步智能体-环境循环在并行云手机实例上进行数据收集；设计三阶段路线图：移除任务课程、结果验证、记忆管理中的人类先验。

Result: 验证了Darwin基础设施在GUI域策略优化第一阶段所需的稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作为迈向真正自主、自我进化的GUI智能体奠定了实践和理论基础。

Abstract: The goal of artificial intelligence is to create agents capable of general, adaptive behaviour in open-ended environments. Guided by the "Bitter Lesson", we argue that the most effective path toward this goal is to systematically remove human priors and allow intelligence to naturally emerge through interaction with a "Big World" that is orders of magnitude more complex than the agent itself. We propose the mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) as a practical proxy for such a world and introduce Darwin Mobile Agent, an open-source infrastructure designed as a foundation for autonomous reinforcement learning in this domain. This framework addresses the data-collection bottleneck in real-world mobile interactions by using an asynchronous agent-environment loop across parallel cloud-phone instances. We further propose a conceptual roadmap to systematically remove human priors from three fundamental pillars of a self-evolving agent: task curricula, outcome verification, and memory management. We validate that the Darwin infrastructure provides the stability and scalability required for the first stage of this roadmap: policy optimisation in the GUI domain. This work establishes the practical and theoretical foundation necessary to move toward truly autonomous, self-evolving GUI agents.

</details>


### [96] [Path-dependent program induction under resource constraints explains human sequence learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20623)
*Hanqi Zhou,David G. Nagy,Peter Dayan,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文整合率失真理论与程序归纳，提出层次化适应文法（HAG）模型，描述在有限认知资源下人们如何从序列经验中构建抽象、可复用知识。通过模拟实验和在线旋律学习实验，HAG在率失真权衡、泛化能力和解释个体差异方面均优于其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在有限认知资源约束下，人们如何从序列化经验中构建抽象且可复用的知识结构。

Method: 将率失真理论与程序归纳相结合，提出层次化适应文法（HAG），包含局部（任务内）和全局（跨任务）库，受记忆与计算约束联合调控。通过模拟实验和在线旋律序列学习实验验证模型。

Result: HAG在率失真权衡和泛化能力上优于固定文法或浅层组块方法；实验中被试的回忆错误呈现系统性简化，反应时间在推断的程序边界处增加；层次化库结构最能解释个体在回忆和续写选择上的差异。

Conclusion: 结构化学习可视为有限条件下的程序归纳，经验的顺序塑造了学习者未来构建的抽象结构。

Abstract: How do people build abstract, reusable knowledge from sequential experience under bounded cognitive resources? To answer this question, we integrate rate-distortion theory with recent advances in program induction to describe how prior knowledge shapes which future structures are cheap to encode and easy to discover. We formalize this in a hierarchical Adaptor Grammar (HAG) with distinct local (within-task) and global (across-task) libraries, governed jointly by constraints on memory and computation. In simulations, HAG achieves better rate-distortion trade-offs and stronger generalization than fixed grammars or shallow chunking methods. In an online melodic sequence-learning experiment, participants' recall errors reflected systematic simplifications and reaction times increased at inferred program boundaries. Trial-by-trial fits further showed that hierarchical libraries best explained individual differences in both recall and out-of-sample continuation choices, outperforming all alternative models. These findings cast structured learning as bounded program induction in which the order of experience shapes future abstractions a learner builds.

</details>


### [97] [In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624)
*Kejiang Qian,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“理性价值风险”概念，形式化定义了LLM在推理中即使经过对齐训练仍无法最大化对齐价值的差距，并通过大量实验验证了该风险普遍存在、价值对齐可降低但无法消除、对推理策略高度敏感、以及长推理带来递减的理性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在训练阶段的价值对齐，但即使模型经过良好对齐，其在推理阶段的决策仍可能偏离最优价值函数。作者旨在形式化并实证这一被忽视的推理-价值鸿沟。

Method: 数学形式化了理性价值风险（rational value risk），将其定义为模型实际推理策略与理性策略（在最陡方向上最大化期望效用的响应）之间的效用差异；并将风险估计误差分解为有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器三个来源。在多个模型家族和基准上进行了大量实验。

Result: (1) 理性价值风险普遍存在；(2) 价值对齐可以降低但无法消除该风险；(3) 该风险对推理时的推理策略高度敏感；(4) 更长的推理能提升理性但呈现递减收益。

Conclusion: 对齐训练不足以确保LLM在推理时完全遵循目标价值函数，推理策略的优化对于弥合理性价值差距至关重要。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in aligning LLMs with target value functions. We argue that, even when an LLM has been well aligned in (post-)training, it may still fail to maximise the aligned value in reasoning. We mathematically formalise this gap as rational value risk: the utility discrepancy between a model's deployed reasoning strategy and its rational counterpart, which is defined to be the responses that maximise expected utility in the steepest direction. The estimation error of rational value risk is further decomposed into three components from finite candidates, finite prompts, and imperfect verifiers. Extensive experiments are conducted, covering models Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5, T{\"}ulu-3 families (7B-72B), GPT-5.2, GPT-5.5, and DeepSeek-V4, and benchmarks UltraFeedback, AlpacaEval, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MathArena. The results validate that (1) rational value risk is widespread; (2) value alignment can reduce, but cannot eliminate, it; (3) the risk is highly sensitive to inference-time reasoning strategy; and (4) longer reasoning improves rationality with diminishing returns. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality.

</details>


### [98] [AlphaMemo: Structured Search-Process Memory for Self-Evolving Alpha Mining Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20625)
*Hang Yu,Zifan Zheng,Jeff Z. Pan,Tongliang Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaMemo，一种具备结构化搜索-过程记忆的自进化alpha挖掘智能体，通过记录AST差异中的编辑模式、置信门控残差记忆和非对称否决控制，提升因子发现的样本外表现和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在alpha挖掘中面临组合搜索空间大、反馈噪声非平稳、发现结果冗余以及过度依赖历史成功经验导致过拟合等问题。

Method: AlphaMemo不记忆最终因子或完整轨迹，而是记录特定父因子上下文中编辑模式（motif）的成败证据。具体包括：从AST差异中提取motif，在搜索账本先验之上构建置信门控残差记忆，并使用非对称否决控制抑制高置信失败模式。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500指数上的实验表明，AlphaMemo在样本外表现和固定预算下的发现效率上均有提升。消融实验验证了残差学习、置信门控、AST-diff模式和否决记忆各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 结构化搜索-过程记忆能有效引导alpha挖掘智能体避免重复失败、聚焦有前景的搜索方向，提升量化因子发现的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: LLM agents are promising for alpha mining via combining financial priors, symbolic reasoning, executable factor generation, and feedback-driven refinement. Yet, they face a combinatorial search space, noisy non-stationary feedback, redundant discoveries, and overfitting risks from naively reusing past successes. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaMemo, a self-evolving alpha mining agent with Structured Search-Process Memory. Rather than memorizing only final factors or full trajectories, AlphaMemo records reusable evidence about which edit motifs work or fail under specific parent-factor contexts. It extracts motifs from Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) differences, applies confidence-gated residual memory on top of a search-ledger prior, and uses asymmetric veto control to suppress high-confidence failure patterns. Experiments on CSI 500 and S\&P 500 show improved out-of-sample performance and fixed-budget discovery efficiency, with ablations validating the roles of residual learning, confidence gating, AST-diff motifs, and veto memory. Code is at https://github.com/jarrettyu/AlphaMemo.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Goal Prediction from Language for Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20627)
*Samuel Barbeau,Simon Roy,Giovanni Beltrame,Christian Desrosiers,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAGO提出从语言指令预测潜在子目标序列，并在同一潜在空间进行动作条件 rollout，通过在线更新子目标和软最小轨迹成本实现长程、鲁棒的规划，兼顾视觉目标的精确性与语言控制的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于世界模型的规划受限于累积预测误差和优化目标定义困难：视觉目标提供局部精确梯度但缺乏远距离引导，语言灵活但受限于跨模态对齐噪声或依赖不适合高采样规划的大生成模型。

Method: LAGO框架从语言指令预测中间目标状态的潜在序列（潜在子目标），并在同一潜在空间执行动作条件 rollout。规划时在线更新子目标，采用软最小轨迹成本进行优化，实现对长程潜在轨迹的连贯追踪。

Result: 在多个环境和规划跨度上的评估显示，LAGO避免了先前方法的急剧性能退化，实现了既稳健又精确的长程规划。

Conclusion: LAGO桥接了视觉目标的精确性与文本引导控制的灵活性，仅凭语言即可实现鲁棒且精确的长程规划。

Abstract: Planning with world models is bottlenecked by compounding prediction errors and the difficulty of defining optimizable goals. Visual targets provide precise local gradients but poor distant guidance, while language is flexible yet limited by noisy cross-modal alignment or dependence on large generative models unsuited for the high-sampling nature of model-based planning. To address these challenges, we introduce Latent Goal Prediction from Language (LAGO), a framework that predicts both sequences of intermediate goal states from language instructions and action-conditioned rollouts, all within the same latent space. Rather than optimizing toward a single global objective, LAGO dynamically decomposes instructions into explicitly predicted, locally tractable latent subgoals. By updating these subgoals online and using a soft minimum trajectory cost during planning, LAGO enables an agent to follow coherent latent trajectories over long horizons. Evaluation across multiple environments planning horizons shows that LAGO avoids the sharp degradation of prior methods. By achieving robust and precise long-horizon planning purely from language, LAGO bridges the precision of visual goals with the flexibility of text-guided control.

</details>


### [100] [Human Decision-Making with AI Assistance under Correlated Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20628)
*Yanru Guan,Naveen Raman,Fei Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在特征相关条件下，AI辅助人类决策时应如何推荐测试以平衡短期决策质量与长期人类学习，证明最优策略需遵循"先探索后承诺"的结构，并提出了相应的算法与近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明当特征独立时，静态策略（重复推荐相同测试）是最优的；但现实场景中特征往往存在相关性（如医疗诊断中不同检查结果相互关联），此时静态策略表现可能极差。因此需要研究在特征相关条件下如何设计AI的推荐策略，以在短期决策质量与长期人类学习之间取得平衡。

Method: 首先证明特征相关下静态策略的任意差表现；接着证明最优策略必须遵循"先探索后承诺"结构，探索长度取决于特征相关程度；然后证明求解最优策略是NP难的，并基于动态规划设计了有限时域最优策略算法；最后提出一种截断规划加静态后缀的近似方法。

Result: 理论上证明了相关特征下最优策略的探索-承诺结构及计算复杂性，实验验证了特征相关性越强，探索阶段越长。

Conclusion: 在特征相关的人类-AI协作决策中，静态策略不再适用，需要采用探索后承诺的策略框架，且探索长度需根据特征相关程度动态调整。

Abstract: Humans increasingly make decisions with AI assistance; for example, doctors may follow AI-recommended diagnostic tests and base their diagnoses on the results. A natural question is which tests should AI recommend to balance short-term decision quality and long-term human learning when different features (e.g., test results) are correlated. While prior work establishes that stationary policies that recommend the same tests repeatedly are optimal when features are independent, we prove that feature correlations lead such policies to perform arbitrarily poorly. Instead, we prove that any optimal policy must follow an explore-then-commit structure; initially, the AI should offer diverse tests so humans can learn accurate feature coefficients, then the AI should commit to a single set of tests, with exploration length that depends on the degree of feature correlation. We prove that computing the optimal policy is NP-hard and derive a dynamic programming-based algorithm that finds the optimal policy for finite horizons. We additionally develop an approximation that plans for shorter horizons and appends a stationary suffix, achieving near-optimal performance. Our empirical results complement our theory by showing that stronger feature correlation leads to longer exploration phases.

</details>


### [101] [Harnessing Agent Skills: Architectural Patterns and a Reference Architecture for Skill-Mediated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20631)
*Boming Xia,Liming Zhu,Zhenchang Xing,Qinghua Lu,Dino Sejdinovic,Xiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能利用（skill harnessing）的概念，定义了从静态技能工件到运行中技能（skill-in-use）的架构责任，归纳了10种经验驱动的架构模式（5核心+5辅助），并综合成包含供应链、中介、执行控制、证据与反馈四个责任层的参考架构，通过在8个系统中交叉实例化进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将技能作为智能体行为的外部化可复用知识工件，但缺乏对技能从静态定义到动态执行间架构责任的系统化理解，特别是技能工件的选择、绑定、解释和证据记录等运行态关系（skill-in-use）的治理机制尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用经验驱动的模式归纳方法，从已有系统中提炼出10种架构模式（5种核心模式+5种支撑模式），并综合为四层参考架构（供应链层、中介层、执行控制层、证据与反馈层），最后通过在8个选定系统中的跨实例化进行架构验证。

Result: 提炼了10种经验驱动的架构模式，构建了包含四个责任层的参考架构，并在8个系统中成功进行了跨实例化验证，证明了该架构的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模式和参考架构为分析和比较不同智能体系统中的技能利用责任提供了统一的词汇表和诊断框架，有助于智能体系统的设计、理解和演化。

Abstract: Agent skills externalise reusable agent-facing behavioural knowledge and guidance as persistent artefacts that can be discovered, activated, and interpreted by LLM agents. Although a skill artefact is static at rest, its architectural responsibilities arise in use, when the artefact is selected for a run, bound to context and authority constraints, interpreted by a stochastic agent, and recorded as run evidence. We call this run-specific relation skill-in-use. This paper studies agent skill harnessing: the architectural responsibilities that govern the transition from skill artefacts to skill-in-use, bound the executable consequences associated with skill-in-use, and capture evidence for attribution, verification, repair, and evolution. This paper provides a catalogue of ten empirically grounded architectural patterns (five core, five supporting) for skill harnessing and synthesises them into a reference architecture with four responsibility layers: Supply Chain, Mediation, Execution Control, and Evidence & Feedback. We evaluate the architecture through cross-instantiation across 8 selected systems. The resulting patterns and reference architecture provide a vocabulary and diagnostic frame for analysing skill-harnessing responsibilities across agent systems.

</details>


### [102] [DEMM-Bench: A Cross-Regime Benchmark for Agent-Runtime Governance-Evidence Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20634)
*Oleg Solozobov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEMM-Bench是一个跨体制的智能体运行时治理证据充分性基准，基于决策证据成熟度模型(DEMM)，通过64个手稿案例和8种证据体制，衡量记录是否足以重构决策级属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体运行时系统会产生多种记录（追踪、账本、溯源图等），但这些容器未必能回答关于特定决策的治理问题，缺乏对决策证据充分性的系统化评估方法。

Method: 基于DEMM模型构建跨体制基准，通过适配器标准化8种证据体制，提出8个属性问题（行为者、权威、行动、策略、决策依据、资源接触、生命周期上下文、验证强度），并应用8种确定性退化条件进行测试。

Result: 在64个案例中，trace-present和schema-present基线在75%案例上过度声明，ledger-present在50%上过度声明，而编辑后的属性级候选评分器零过度声明，平均属性充分性准确率为56.25%。

Conclusion: DEMM-Bench提供了64案例数据集、构造预言标签、基线和适配器，支持在异构智能体运行时证据基底上进行可复现的决策证据成熟度评估。

Abstract: Agent-runtime systems emit traces, ledgers, provenance graphs, policy logs, delegation tokens, cache events, and tool-firewall records, but those containers do not necessarily answer governance questions about a specific decision. DEMM-Bench is a cross-regime benchmark for agent-runtime governance-evidence sufficiency, grounded in the Decision Evidence Maturity Model (DEMM): it measures whether records across eight evidence regimes are sufficient to reconstruct decision-level properties rather than merely present. The benchmark normalizes the regimes through adapters, asks property questions over actor, authority, action, policy, decision basis, resource touch, lifecycle context, and verification strength, and applies eight deterministic degradation conditions. Across 64 manuscript cases, trace-present and schema-present baselines overclaim on 75% of cases, ledger-present overclaims on 50%, and the redacted property-level candidate scorer has zero overclaim with 56.25% mean Property Sufficiency Accuracy. The deposited package provides the 64-case dataset, construction-oracle labels, baselines, and adapters, supporting reproducible evaluation of decision-evidence maturity across heterogeneous agent-runtime evidence substrates.

</details>


### [103] [SkillHarness: Harnessing Safe Skills for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20636)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillHarness框架，通过技能边界和选择性技能重用，在动态环境中实现计算机使用代理的安全技能学习与执行，将不安全率降低57.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA技能学习方法假设静态安全环境，忽略了对抗性交互（如提示注入）和环境动态变化（如弹窗）带来的风险，导致技能学习风险高、执行不稳定，亟需一种能在动态环境中安全学习和使用技能的方法。

Method: SkillHarness将技能学习与利用建模为安全约束的交互过程：1）引入技能边界，利用多源监督信号从交互轨迹中识别安全技能；2）构建自改进的安全约束，贯穿技能生命周期；3）提出选择性技能重用，根据上下文引导任务分解并通过选择性激活技能子集完成任务。

Result: SkillHarness将学习技能的不安全率降低57.1%，在动态环境变化下持续提升执行稳定性，全面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: SkillHarness通过安全约束的交互建模和选择性重用机制，有效解决了动态环境中CUA技能学习的安全性问题，为实际部署提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Computer-Use Agents (CUAs) are increasingly deployed in dynamic interactive environments, creating a growing need for continual skill learning during interaction. Recent approaches address this challenge by learning reusable skills from successful trajectories. However, these skill learning methods largely assume static and safe environments, overlooking risks from adversarial interactions (e.g., prompt injections) and environmental dynamics (e.g., pop-ups). In dynamic settings, such assumptions can lead to risky skill learning and brittle execution, undermining the reliability of CUAs. This raises the question: how can CUAs learn and use skills safely in dynamic environments? To address this problem, we propose SkillHarness, a framework for safe skill harnessing in dynamic environments. SkillHarness moves beyond static skill abstractions by modeling skill learning and utilization as a safety-constrained interaction process. Specifically, we introduce the skill boundary that leverages multi-source supervision signals to identify safe skills from interaction trajectories, and construct self-improving safety constraints throughout the skill lifecycle. In addition, SkillHarness introduces selective skill reuse, where tasks are guided to decompose according to context and completed through the selective activation of skill subsets. Our experiments demonstrate that SkillHarness significantly reduces the unsafe rate of learned skills by 57.1% and consistently improves execution stability under dynamic environmental changes, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Constituency Optimisation Through Hamiltonian Representation Of Mandates (COTHROM): Algorithmic Redistricting of Irish Election Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20637)
*Ruaidhrí Campion,Matthew Fenlon,Joshua Cooney Mercedal,Casey Farren-Colloty,Eliza Somerville,Michael A. J. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了爱尔兰PR-STV选举重划选区的首个计算框架，利用统计物理（Potts哈密顿量）、MCMC模拟退火和帕累托最优性，在多目标权衡下优化选区边界，在科克郡的测试中优于现有法律选区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰PR-STV选举制度中，选区重划面临从海量可能配置中选出最优边界集的挑战，且"代表性"需要在多个常相互冲突的宪法目标之间精细平衡，现有方法缺乏系统性的优化框架。

Method: 将选举重划问题解析为统计物理框架，宪法目标作为Potts哈密顿量中的项；运用MCMC方法和模拟退火最小化目标函数，耦合常数作为目标权重的代理；结合多准则决策分析（MCDA）和帕累托最优性解决权重组合的模糊性问题。

Result: 在科克郡的实验中，COTHROM框架在比例代表性和紧凑性目标上，对多种权重组合均一致优于现有的法定选区边界。

Conclusion: 该计算框架能够系统性地优化多个宪法要求，使权衡显式化、可量化，为爱尔兰选举重划提供了有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Electoral redistricting in Ireland's Proportional Representation Single Transferable Vote (PR-STV) system faces the challenge of selecting an optimally representative set of electoral boundaries from an enormous set of possible configurations, and where ``representative'' is a delicate balance of constitutional objectives that are often in tension with one another. We present the first computational framework for Irish electoral redistricting that systematically optimises across multiple constitutional requirements while making trade-offs explicit and quantifiable. The electoral redistricting problem is parsed using statistical physics, where constitutional objectives are considered as terms in a Potts Hamiltonian. Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods and simulated annealing are employed to minimise this objective function, systematically exploring this configuration space, with coupling constants as proxies for objective weightings. Multi Criterion Decision Analysis (MCDA) and Pareto Optimality is then utilised to remedy the ambiguity in choosing a certain objective weighting combination over others. With respect to proportional representation and compactness objectives evaluated in County Cork, COTHROM consistently improves on the existing legal constituency boundaries for a range of objective weightings.

</details>


### [105] [RIZZ: Routing Interactions to Near Zero-Interference Zones for Continual Adaptation of Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20638)
*Sonali Goel,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Lucas Schorling,Natalia Ares,Maike Osborne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIZZ是一种基于记忆、路由和提示编译的黑盒持续适应框架，通过在非平稳输入流中动态生成记忆分支、利用验证器门控机制更新记忆，实现近乎零干扰的在线适应，在多个基准测试上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒适应方法（单提示优化、无区分记忆、基于搜索的方法）在处理非平稳输入流、稀疏反馈以及跨任务族干扰时存在严重不足，亟需一种能在不访问模型权重、在线条件下实现持续适应且能显式控制干扰的框架。

Method: RIZZ提出一种验证器门控的记忆-路由-编译框架：1) 动态生成记忆分支组织输入流；2) 上下文感知路由器在线或离线选择/创建分支，检索分支本地、全局、图结构和工作记忆四类上下文；3) 将检索到的上下文与任务证据编译为有界提示；4) 任务验证器对输出评分，仅已验证的交互才能更新记忆（提升有用规则、降级有害规则或创建反模式）。

Result: 在竞争性基准测试上，RIZZ对比最先进的基线的表现更优，证明了其在在线适应、上下文预算受限条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: RIZZ通过验证器门控的记忆分支和路由机制，在完全黑盒、在线、非平稳且反馈稀疏的条件下实现了有效的持续适应，同时显式控制了任务间的干扰，为长周期语言模型智能体的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as long-lived agents that must adapt across users, tasks, domains, modalities, and feedback regimes without access to model weights. Existing black-box adaptation methods typically optimize a single prompt, maintain an undifferentiated memory, or rely on repeated rollout-heavy search. However, these designs struggle when streams of input are nonstationary, feedback is sparse, and failures from one task family can contaminate behavior on another. We introduce RIZZ (Routing Interactions to Near Zero-interference Zones), a continual adaptation framework for compound language-model systems that learns entirely through verifier-gated memory, routing, and prompt compilation. RIZZ organizes input streams into dynamically spawned memory branches. At inference time, either while online or offline, a context-aware router selects or creates a branch that retrieves branch-local, global, graph-structured, and working-memory context, which is compiled into a bounded prompt together with retrieved task evidence. After the model acts, task verifiers score the output, and only verified interactions can update memory, promote reusable rules, demote harmful rules, or create anti-patterns. This yields a black-box agent that improves through persistent natural-language feedback while explicitly controlling interference. RIZZ targets the regime where adaptation must occur online under context budgets. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework against state-of-the-art baselines on competitive benchmarks.

</details>


### [106] [An LLM-Explainable DRL Framework for Passenger-Directed Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20640)
*Ouided Braoui,Meriem Bouali,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度强化学习（DRL）驱动的自动驾驶控制与大语言模型（LLM）可解释性模块相结合，实现自适应驾驶决策与乘客行为解释。DRL代理采用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network在仿真中训练，支持快、舒适、停止三种驾驶模式切换，LLM模块则负责解析乘客请求、判断解释时机并生成安全导向的说明。实验证明该框架在安全性、适应性和可解释性之间取得平衡，尤其在请求延迟或因安全约束被覆盖时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动驾驶因决策不透明导致的公众信任不足问题，通过结合DRL和LLM增强可解释性。

Method: 采用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network训练DRL代理实现三种驾驶模式（快、舒适、停止），同时引入LLM模块解析乘客请求、判断解释需求并生成简洁的安全导向说明。

Result: DRL代理在仿真中实现稳定学习、安全合规及单次行程中模式可靠切换；LLM模块能有效生成解释，尤其在请求延迟或因安全约束被覆盖时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该集成RL决策与LLM可解释性的框架作为概念验证，证明了在安全性、适应性和可解释性之间取得平衡的可行性，特别适用于请求被延迟或安全约束导致请求被覆盖的场景。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offer the potential for safer and more efficient mobility, yet public trust remains limited due to the lack of transparency in their decision-making. This work addresses this issue by combining deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for adaptive driving control with large language model (LLM)-based explainability modules designed to communicate agent behavior to passengers. DRL agents were trained in simulation using a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network to follow distinct driving requests: \textit{fast}, \textit{comfort}, and \textit{stop}. They demonstrated stable learning, safe compliance with traffic rules, and reliable switching between modes within a single trip. In parallel, LLM modules were introduced to interpret passenger requests, determine when explanations were needed, and generate concise, safety-oriented justifications. Results show that this framework, serving as a proof of concept for integrating RL decision-making and LLMs, balances safety, adaptability, and explainability, and is most effective when requests are delayed or overridden due to safety constraints.

</details>


### [107] [Hypothesis-Disciplined Multi-Agent Automated Formalization of Asymptotic Statistical Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20642)
*Tingzhou Wei,Zeyu Zheng,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体协作的假设约束型Lean 4形式化验证流水线，成功实现了渐近统计理论（含参数与半参数模型）的系统性形式化，成果已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近统计理论包含收敛性陈述、渐近展开、泛函分析及大量正则性条件，与现有Lean 4形式化基础设施之间存在巨大鸿沟，亟需一种系统化的形式化方法学来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出假设约束型Lean 4形式化流水线：一个管理者（Manager）协调七个专业角色（证明规划、骨架搭建、Mathlib侦察、证明构建、集成、独立审阅、审计）。核心方法是审计员（Auditor）执行的假设约束审计：每个主定理假设和概念层字段必须锚定于源数学散文，标记为Lean编码适配器、源隐含项，或作为不支持的强化而拒绝。

Result: 成功构建了渐近统计理论的系统性形式化，包含参数与半参数模型的渐近分布与效率结果。形式化代码公理干净、忠实于原始文献，通过了Lean检查与人工审计。核心定理的基础设施与统计概念定义与定理特定组装分离，组织良好。

Conclusion: 该假设约束型多智能体流水线能有效弥合渐近统计理论与Lean形式化基础设施之间的差距，产生公理干净且源忠实的形式化成果，为AI辅助下复杂数学理论的形式化提供了可复用的方法论范式。

Abstract: Asymptotic statistical theory is a challenging domain for AI-assisted formalization: its central results mix convergence statements, asymptotic expansions, functional analysis, and regularity conditions that have a large gap from existing infrastructure in Lean 4 formalization. To address these challenges, we propose a hypothesis-disciplined Lean 4 formalization pipeline built from multiple agents: a manager that coordinates seven specialist roles for proof planning, skeleton scaffolding, Mathlib reconnaissance, proof construction, integration, independent review, and audit. The main methodological discipline is the hypothesis-disciplined audit, implemented by the Auditor agent: every main-theorem hypothesis and concept-layer field must be anchored in the source mathematical prose, justified as a Lean encoding adapter, marked as source-implied, or rejected as an unsupported strengthening. Using this workflow, we build a systematic formalization of asymptotic statistical theory, especially the parametric and semi-parametric models' asymptotic distribution and efficiency results. The resulting Lean development is axiom-clean and source-faithful, with Lean-checked and human-audited proofs of core parametric and semi-parametric theorems organized so that theorem-agnostic infrastructure and statistical concept definitions are separated from theorem-specific assembly. The formalization results are available at https://github.com/junwei-lu/Lean-Asymptotic-Statistical-Theory.

</details>


### [108] [SPARC: A Multi-Agent System for Electrical Circuit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20643)
*Mushtari Sadia,Zhenning Yang,Umme Habiba Lamia,Nishat Shawrin,Ang Chen,Amrita Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARC是一个多智能体系统，通过可执行的物理仿真来回答电路图问题，相比基线方法准确率提高达58%，达到83%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路图问答任务需要复杂的数学推理，这对多模态大语言模型仍然具有挑战性。

Method: SPARC采用多智能体系统，利用LLM智能体合成、执行和分析仿真程序，将推理过程 grounding 在可执行的基于物理的仿真中。

Result: SPARC达到了83%的准确率，相比基线方法有高达58%的绝对提升，并实现了系统化的错误诊断能力。

Conclusion: 通过将推理 grounding 在可执行仿真中，SPARC按设计提高了准确性和可靠性，有效解决了电路图问答中的数学推理挑战。

Abstract: Electrical circuit diagram QA tasks require complex mathematical reasoning, which remains challenging for multimodal LLMs. We present SPARC, a multi-agent system that answers questions over circuit diagrams by grounding reasoning in executable physics-based simulations. SPARC uses LLM agents to synthesize, execute, and analyze simulation programs, improving accuracy and reliability by design. It achieves 83% accuracy, with up to a 58% absolute improvement over baselines, while enabling systematic error diagnosis.

</details>


### [109] [Bridging Multi-Valued Heuristics and Dimensionality Reduction in Multi-Objective Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20644)
*Maya Wolff,Ariel Felner,Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多目标最短路径（MOSP）算法中多值启发式（MVH）与维度缩减（DR）技术结合使用时的正确性问题，提出了两种理论框架：NAMOA*dr-mvh 通过强制启发式一致性保证搜索正确性，而 L-NAMOA*dr-mvh 采用"惰性"乐观方法，动态检测并修复局部排序违规，在仅需可接纳MVH的条件下实现精确正确性。实验表明后者在多个基准测试中匹配或超越现有最优算法，且在某些实例中因MVH提供的更强剪枝能力实现了超过10倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOSP算法使用单值启发式（SVH）无法捕捉帕累托前沿的权衡结构，导致搜索引导效果弱；而多值启发式（MVH）虽能提供更丰富的代价估计集，但将其与维度缩减（DR）技术结合时存在正确性挑战——简单组合会破坏DR所需的排序不变量，导致搜索不可靠且不完备。

Method: 提出两种理论框架：1) NAMOA*dr-mvh：通过强制启发式一致性来恢复搜索正确性；2) L-NAMOA*dr-mvh：采用"惰性"乐观方法，仅在需要时进行DR，动态检测并修复局部排序违规，仅需可接纳MVH即可保证精确正确性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，L-NAMOA*dr-mvh匹配或超越了现有最优MOSP算法，并在某些实例中因MVH提供的更强剪枝能力实现了超过10倍的加速。

Conclusion: 首次建立了安全集成MVH与DR的理论框架，解决了该组合中的正确性挑战，其中L-NAMOA*dr-mvh在保证精确正确性的同时实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-objective shortest-path (MOSP) algorithms traditionally rely on single-valued heuristics (SVHs), which associate each state with a single admissible cost vector. While SVHs provide safe lower bounds, they fail to capture the trade-off structure of the Pareto frontier and often yield weak search guidance. Multi-valued heuristics (MVHs) address this limitation by mapping states to sets of cost estimates, enabling a richer approximation of possible trade-offs. Modern MOSP algorithms are highly dependent on dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques to efficiently perform dominance checks. However, integrating MVHs with DR introduces subtle correctness challenges. We show that naively combining DR with MVHs destroys the ordering invariants required for DR, leading to unsound and incomplete search. To address this issue, we develop the first theoretical frameworks for safely integrating MVHs with DR. First, we introduce $\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$, a theoretical baseline that restores search correctness by enforcing heuristic consistency. Recognizing the practical limitations of this approach, we then introduce our primary contribution, $\text{L}\text{-}\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$. This algorithm employs a "lazy," optimistic approach to DR, preserving exact correctness with only an admissible MVH by dynamically detecting and repairing local ordering violations. Across a range of benchmarks, $\text{L}\text{-}\text{NAMOA}^*{\text{dr}\text{-}\text{mvh}}$ matches or improves over state-of-the-art MOSP algorithms, and achieves speedups of over 10x in instances where the additional guidance provided by the MVH translates into stronger pruning.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Splitting Heuristics for Parallel String Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20656)
*Chenhao Gao,Peisen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的方法来自动生成字符串约束求解器的分裂启发式策略，通过将分裂原子选择问题转化为学习任务，利用输入公式特征和求解器执行动态数据，在Z3seq和Z3str4两个求解器上实现，在求解数量和平均求解时间上均优于人工设计的启发式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符串约束求解对于推理字符串操作程序至关重要，但许多实际字符串约束不可判定，且现有求解器难以处理复杂约束。多核架构的兴起为并行求解提供了机会，其中cube-and-conquer方法的关键在于分裂启发式策略的质量。然而，手动设计启发式策略耗时且往往次优。

Method: 将分裂原子的选择问题形式化为学习任务，使用输入公式的特征和求解器执行的动态数据作为特征，通过数据驱动的方式自动生成分裂启发式策略。

Result: 在Z3seq和Z3str4两个流行的字符串求解器上实现该方法，实验表明学习得到的启发式策略在求解公式数量和平均求解时间上均优于手动设计的启发式策略。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的自动生成分裂启发式策略方法能有效提升字符串约束求解器的性能，优于人工设计的启发式策略。

Abstract: String constraint solvers are crucial for reasoning about string-manipulating programs. However, many practical string constraints are undecidable, and real-world applications often present complex constraints that challenge current solvers. The rise of multi-core architectures offers an opportunity for parallel solving. A key parallel solving method is \emph{cube-and-conquer}, in which the quality of splitting heuristics is critical to effectively dividing the search space. Unfortunately, manually designing the heuristics is labor-intensive, and handcrafted heuristics are often sub-optimal. This paper introduces a data-driven approach to automatically generating splitting heuristics. We frame the problem of selecting a splitting atom as a learning task, using features from input formulas and dynamic data from solver execution. We implement this approach in two popular string solvers, Z3seq and Z3str4, demonstrating that the learned heuristics outperform manually designed ones in the number of solved formulas and the average solving time.

</details>


### [111] [A-Evolve-Training: Autonomous Post-Training of a 30B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20657)
*Zhan Shi,Bing He,Yisi Sang,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报道了一个无需人工干预的自主后训练系统，成功对300亿参数的Nemotron模型进行了多轮后训练，在公开排行榜上达到0.86分（人类最佳0.87分），并在过程中自主发现并修正了评估指标偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的后训练通常需要数周的人工工作（设计数据、调整配方、运行评估、决策取舍），本文旨在构建一个能自主执行这一循环的系统，从而实现后训练过程的自动化。

Method: 构建了一个无需人工介入的闭环自主系统，自动执行"提出数据与配方变更→启动训练运行→读取评估结果→决定保留什么"的完整后训练循环，在30B Nemotron模型上进行了四轮迭代。

Result: 自主生成的模型在NVIDIA Nemotron-Reasoning Challenge排行榜上达到0.86分（人类最佳0.87分），在约4000个提交中排名第8。系统还自主发现开发指标与外部性能脱钩，并主动修正搜索策略，不再最大化开发指标而是寻找降低误导性代理指标同时提升外部目标的干预措施。

Conclusion: 证明了规模化自主闭环能够产生"发现"而非仅"优化"——系统检测到评估框架产生误导并自主改变了证据标准。这是首个公开报道的在此规模（300亿参数）上的自主后训练运行，此前公开的自主ML研究演示仅停留在GPT-2级别（约1.24亿参数）。

Abstract: Post-training a frontier model is normally weeks of human work: proposing data and recipe changes, launching runs, reading evals, deciding what to keep. We report an autonomous system that runs this loop with no human in the loop, post-training a 30B Nemotron across four rounds over multiple weeks. The autonomously produced model reaches a held-out score of 0.86 against the top human submission's 0.87 on the public NVIDIA Nemotron-Reasoning Challenge leaderboard, placing 8th of ~4000 at the time of writing. More striking than the number: the loop detected that its own dev metric had stopped tracking external performance on the weakest domain -- candidates drove dev to record highs without moving the external target -- and revised its own search policy, no longer maximizing dev but seeking interventions that lowered the now-misleading proxy while improving the external target. We treat this as direct, auditable evidence that a scaled autonomous loop can produce discovery, not only optimization: it detected that its measurement frame had become misleading and changed what counted as evidence. We take the operational view that any system worth the "recursive self-improvement" label must eventually perform end-to-end post-training of a frontier-class model; this is one datapoint of that bar being cleared. We do not claim a "first autonomous match" of human researchers. The claim we make is narrower and auditable: to our knowledge, this is the first publicly reported autonomous post-training run at this scale, where prior public autonomous-ML-research demonstrations sit at GPT-2-class (~124M) budgets. The same system also post-trains the 120B and 550B Nemotron; with no public human baseline there, this shows only that the loop closes at that scale, not that its output is competitive -- infrastructure evidence, with the effectiveness claim deferred until a comparable human anchor exists.

</details>


### [112] [Expected Free Energy-based Planning as Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20658)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Thijs van de Laar,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将主动推理中的期望自由能（EFE）规划问题重新表述为带有认知先验的生成模型上的变分自由能最小化，证明了该方法在理论上与自由能原理一致，并在三种环境中验证了其信息寻求行为、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EFE的规划方法通常采用专门的优化过程，难以扩展或分析。作者希望将EFE规划统一到变分自由能最小化的框架下，使规划与感知、学习遵循相同的推理过程。

Method: 将EFE规划重新表述为在增广了认知先验的生成模型上进行变分自由能最小化；证明最小化带有恰当先验的变分自由能泛函可分解为期望计划成本（EFE）加上复杂度项；在确定性T-maze、随机Reactivity Maze和部分可观测MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8三种环境中进行实验验证。

Result: 1）认知先验能够诱导信息寻求行为；2）在随机转移下，基于策略的推理优于基于计划的方法；3）时间分解使得该方法能够扩展到现有表格型主动推理方法无法处理的环境。

Conclusion: 该变分公式在理论上与自由能原则保持一致，将规划视为与感知和学习相同的推理过程，并在实践中具有更好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty requires agents to balance goal achievement with information gathering. Active inference addresses this through the Expected Free Energy (EFE), a cost function that unifies instrumental and epistemic objectives. However, existing EFE-based methods typically employ specialized optimization procedures that are difficult to extend or analyze. In this paper, we show that EFE-based planning can be formulated as Variational Free Energy minimization on a generative model augmented with epistemic priors. Our main result demonstrates that minimizing a Variational Free Energy functional with appropriately chosen priors yields a decomposition into expected plan costs (the EFE) plus a complexity term. This formulation reinforces theoretical consistency with the Free Energy Principle by casting planning as the same inferential process that governs perception and learning. We validate our approach on three environments of increasing complexity: a deterministic T-maze, a stochastic Reactivity Maze, and a partially observable MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8 environment. The experiments demonstrate that the epistemic priors induce information-seeking behavior, that the variational formulation yields policy-based inference outperforming plan-based methods under stochastic transitions, and that temporal factorization enables scalability to environments where existing tabular active inference methods cannot operate.

</details>


### [113] [Skill Coverage: A Test Adequacy Metric for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20659)
*Boyin Tan,Xiaowei Huang,Youcheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能覆盖度（skill coverage）这一测试充分性度量指标，用于评估智能体在执行任务时是否充分测试了技能文档中记录的行为约束。对SkillsBench的分析显示，现有基准执行仅覆盖39.90%到43.98%的技能行为约束，说明任务成功并不等同于技能工件的充分测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体技能评估主要关注任务层面的结果（任务成功与否），但任务成功无法揭示技能的哪些部分被实际执行、哪些部分未被测试。需要一种能够衡量技能工件本身测试充分性的方法。

Method: 从技能文档中提取可观察的技能行为约束，通过二元判定（覆盖/未覆盖）来衡量智能体轨迹是否为每个约束提供了足够的可观察证据，而不对行为赋予额外的结果标签。

Result: 将技能覆盖度应用于SkillsBench，发现现有基准执行仅覆盖39.90%至43.98%的技能行为约束，表明当前基准任务留下了大量已记录技能指导未被测试。

Conclusion: 成功的任务完成并不意味着对技能工件进行了充分测试，技能覆盖度可作为衡量智能体技能测试彻底性的重要指标。
