Abstract: Self-improvement can self-regress. In REINFORCE post-training for code, a model can quickly improve on its optimized metric and then collapse within the same training campaign. We study this in a controlled multi-seed testbed using Qwen-2.5-3B and Qwen-2.5-7B, trained on competitive-programming tasks with binary CodeGrader reward across 10 sequential 20-step campaigns. Across campaigns, pass@1 shows a robust rise-then-collapse pattern: it peaks within tens of gradient steps and then falls back, sometimes to near zero. This is not cross-task catastrophic forgetting, but within-task policy over-optimization on a fixed distribution; KL- and EWC-style constraints do not prevent it.
  We ask where the control loop should sit. We compare three levels: CARE, a between-campaign memory mechanism with a capability posterior, transfer gate, and regression-aware belief revision; ES, a within-campaign early-stop rule that rolls forward the peak checkpoint and sets the next budget to peak_step+3; and GRPO, which changes the RL update using group-relative reward normalization.
  The answer is regime-dependent. On Qwen-2.5-3B, where naive REINFORCE is fragile, CARE v2 nearly doubles end-of-chain pass@1 from 4.9% to 9.5%, with paired bootstrap 95% CI [+0.4,+8.9] and gains in 4/5 seeds. On Qwen-2.5-7B, CARE reaches parity with naive REINFORCE, 13.8% vs. 11.8%, while ES reaches 22.2% [14.1,28.0]. Out-of-the-box GRPO reaches 20.7% [15.7,25.1], nearly matching REINFORCE+ES.
  GRPO raises the floor but does not remove the cliff. Its 7B gain mainly comes from better between-campaign carryover, while the within-campaign peak-to-end gap remains about 17 points under both REINFORCE and GRPO. GRPO+ES gives mixed evidence: 2/3 seeds improve, but one final cliff lowers the mean to 17.0% [0.0,28.1]. A Gemma-3-4B pilot shows the same signature, suggesting the phenomenon is not limited to Qwen.

</details>


### [148] [Answer Engineering: Local Trajectory Editing for Protocol-Constrained Decision Making in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21121)
*Victor Lavrenko,Anastasiia Molodnitskaia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Answer Engineering，一种在自回归生成过程中对推理轨迹进行确定性规则引导干预的方法，无需重训练或修改模型权重。在突发性感觉神经性听力损失（SSNHL）临床基准测试中，该方法将合规率从54.5%提升至83.5%，平衡准确率从42.0%提升至80.7%，但同时也暴露出规则覆盖、触发器可靠性和诊断优先生成动力学等局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要严格程序合规的领域中，能够生成看似自信但实际违反协议的答案，现有方法缺乏对推理轨迹的可审计运行时控制。

Method: Answer Engineering：一种确定性的运行时和创作层，在标准自回归生成过程中对可见推理轨迹应用局部化规则引导干预，无需重训练、修改模型权重或进行全局搜索。

Result: 在SSNHL临床基准中：未经引导的逐步推理使SSNHL合规率从54.5%降至25.1%；局部轨迹编辑将SSNHL合规率提升至83.5%，传导性病例依从性提升至77.9%，平衡准确率从42.0%提升至80.7%。

Conclusion: 通过可审计的运行时控制推理轨迹可提升协议合规性，但存在规则覆盖、触发器可靠性和诊断优先生成动力学等限制。

Abstract: Large language models can produce confident but protocol-invalid answers in domains where procedural compliance is critical. This paper presents Answer Engineering, a deterministic runtime and authoring layer that applies localized rule-guided interventions to the visible reasoning trajectory during standard autoregressive generation, without retraining, modifying model weights, or performing global search. The method is evaluated on a controlled clinical benchmark for sudden sensorineural hearing loss (SSNHL), where correct management depends on protocol-consistent interpretation of symptom timing, Weber/Rinne tuning-fork findings, and otoscopic findings. In the benchmark, step-by-step reasoning shifted rather than eliminated errors: compliant outcomes for SSNHL decreased from 54.5% under unguided generation to 25.1%, while acceptance on the conductive contrast condition increased from 1.6% to 58.9%. Local trajectory editing increased SSNHL compliance to 83.5% and conductive-case adherence to 77.9%, raising balanced accuracy from 42.0% under reasoning-only generation to 80.7%. The results support a systems-level view in which protocol adherence can be improved through auditable runtime control of reasoning trajectories, while also identifying limitations caused by rule coverage, trigger reliability, and persistent diagnosis-first generation dynamics.

</details>


### [149] [PulseCX: Breaking the Closed-World Assumption in Real-Time CX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21124)
*Rajat Agarwal,Suvidha Tripathi,Shubham Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PulseCX是一个解耦知识获取与消费的框架，采用结构优先范式和异步智能体，通过衰减感知时序知识图谱(DA-TKG)和强化-衰减动态管理信息生命周期，结合分层意图门控，实现<10ms开销的动态环境下意图解析和客户满意度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话AI智能体受限于封闭世界约束，无法感知高速外部变化（如病毒式传播趋势或服务中断），而临时性网络搜索引入过高延迟和上下文污染，亟需一种高效、低延迟的知识更新机制。

Method: 提出PulseCX框架：(1) 异步智能体将外部信号线性化为结构数据；(2) 构建衰减感知时序知识图谱(DA-TKG)，通过强化-衰减动态管理信息生命周期；(3) 分层意图门控机制实现自进化记忆的消费，解耦知识获取与消费，消除同步搜索瓶颈。

Result: PulseCX实现<10ms的开销，在动态环境中显著提升了意图解析率(IRR)和客户满意度(s-CSAT)。

Conclusion: 通过解耦知识获取与消费、引入衰减感知时序知识图谱和分层意图门控，PulseCX有效克服了传统对话AI的封闭世界约束，在低延迟下实现动态环境中的高性能表现。

Abstract: Conversational AI agents in Customer Experience (CX) typically suffer from a Closed-World Constraint, ignoring high-velocity external shifts like viral trends or outages. Ad-hoc web search attempts to bridge this gap but often introduce prohibitive latency and context poisoning. We introduce PulseCX, a framework that decouples knowledge acquisition from consumption. Adopting a structure-first paradigm, PulseCX employs an asynchronous agent to linearize signals into a Decay-Aware Temporal Knowledge Graph (DA-TKG) governed by reinforcement--decay dynamics to actively manage information lifecycles. By coupling this self-evolving memory with hierarchical intent gating, PulseCX removes synchronous search bottlenecks (<10ms overhead) and drives significant gains in Intent Resolution (IRR) and Customer Satisfaction (s-CSAT) in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Burst-Aware Early Warning Models for Capacity Stress under AI Workload Surges in Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21130)
*Zihan Yu,Xianling Zeng,Zhiming Xue,Yalun Qi,Sichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向部署的、突发感知的早期预警框架，用于预测AI工作负载激增下的数据中心容量压力。该框架采用轻量级树模型（XGBoost）处理多变量遥测数据，在高度不平衡环境中实现高召回率预测（Recall=0.914），并展示了与运营控制回路的集成能力，支持主动式资源调度与限流等干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI工作负载（特别是LLM训练和推理）具有突发性、高强度、快速变化的资源需求特征，传统基于阈值反应的机制难以有效应对此类突发容量压力，亟需一种能够提前预警、支持主动干预的预测框架。

Method: 将问题建模为多变量遥测窗口上的高召回率预测任务；引入突发注入方法模拟AI负载尖峰模式；采用轻量级XGBoost树模型捕捉高度不平衡环境下的非线性交互特征；通过面向部署的阈值选择策略优化召回率。

Result: XGBoost模型达到ROC AUC为0.697、AP为0.670，显著优于基线方法；在面向部署的阈值选择下，召回率达到0.914，能够检测大部分压力易发时段且误报成本可控。

Conclusion: 基于学习的高召回率早期预警系统在实际数据中心运营中具有重要价值，能够有效支撑弹性自适应运营，支持工作负载限流和资源扩展等主动操作，为AI驱动的工作负载时代提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large-scale AI workloads, particularly Large Language Model (LLM) training and inference, is fundamentally reshaping the operational dynamics of hyperscale data centers. Unlike traditional cloud workloads, AI-driven jobs exhibit bursty, high-intensity, and rapidly shifting resource demands, often leading to sudden capacity stress that cannot be effectively handled by reactive threshold-based mechanisms. In this paper, we propose a deployment-oriented, burst-aware early warning framework for proactive capacity stress prediction under AI workload surges. We formulate the problem as a high-recall forecasting task over multivariate telemetry windows, with the explicit goal of enabling operational intervention before system degradation occurs. The proposed framework integrates workload intensity, temporal variation, and system pressure signals, and employs a lightweight tree-based learning model to capture nonlinear interactions in highly imbalanced environments. To evaluate the system under realistic conditions, we introduce an AI workload surge injection methodology that simulates burst-driven demand patterns observed in large-scale AI systems. Our XGBoost-based model achieves an ROC AUC of 0.697 and an AP of 0.670, significantly outperforming baseline methods. Under deployment-oriented threshold selection, the framework achieves a Recall of 0.914, enabling the detection of the majority of stress-prone periods with acceptable false-alarm cost. Beyond predictive performance, we show how the proposed framework can be integrated into operational control loops to support proactive actions such as workload throttling and resource scaling. Our results highlight the practical value of high-recall, learning-based early warning systems in enabling resilient and adaptive data center operations in the era of AI-driven workloads.

</details>


### [151] [Trip+: Benchmarking Agents in Personalized Interactive Travel Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21169)
*Junle Chen,Wei Chen,Yehong Xu,Zhengjun Huang,Yuqian Wu,Zhoujin Tian,Kai Wang,Lei Wang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trip+是一个用于评估语言代理在动态交互中进行整体旅行规划能力的新基准。它要求代理根据旅行者档案生成和修改分钟级行程，并通过基于LLM的模拟器评估端到端体验（包括主观指标如疲劳度）。实验发现18个语言模型在体验质量上存在一致差距，模型倾向于生成技术上可行但令人疲惫且偏离用户偏好的行程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常在相对孤立的设置中评估可行性、个性化或交互性，缺乏对旅行规划中整体体验质量的综合评估。

Method: 提出Trip+基准，包含旅行者档案和动态交互场景，要求代理生成并修订分钟级行程；使用基于LLM的模拟器评估端到端旅行体验，包括疲劳度等主观指标；场景范围从简单请求解决到复杂环境驱动的重新规划。

Result: 对18个语言模型进行评估，发现所有模型在体验质量上存在一致差距——模型倾向于输出技术上可行但令人疲惫的行程，与旅行者档案偏好严重偏离。

Conclusion: 现有语言模型在整体旅行规划中未能平衡可行性与用户体验，需要更好的方法来对齐模型输出与用户个性化需求。

Abstract: Interactive travel planning has become a popular use case for language models. Agents are deployed to manage evolving preferences and unexpected disruptions over multiple turns. Such settings require models to make complex, profile-conditioned planning decisions. However, existing benchmarks often evaluate feasibility, personalization, or interaction in relatively isolated settings. We therefore introduce Trip+ to measure the ability of agents to plan travel holistically. In Trip+, given traveler profiles and dynamic interactions, agents must generate and revise minute-level itineraries. End-to-end traveler experiences are evaluated via an LLM-based simulator, enabling the assessment of subjective metrics like fatigue. Our scenarios range from simple request resolutions to complex environment-driven replanning. We evaluate 18 LMs and find a consistent gap in experiential quality. Models favor technically feasible but exhausting itineraries that diverge sharply from profiled traveler preferences.

</details>


### [152] [Whistleblowing and the machine -- towards a considered position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21201)
*Marija Slavkovik,Liuwen Yu,Leon van der Torre,Reka Markovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能代理和自主系统的告密（whistleblowing）问题，认为机器告密必须基于规范性和原则性，根植于现有的告密理论，并呼吁政府监管机构就机器告密的内容和法律保护制定明确立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能代理嵌入人类环境，既能生成数据又能从中得出结论，即机器可以"拥有秘密"。已有研究表明人工代理能够进行告密，且数字多智能体环境应允许代理告密，因此需要探讨机器告密的规范基础。

Method: 基于现有的告密理论，对机器告密进行规范性分析，论证机器告密必须是有原则的、规范的，并指出需要政府监管介入。

Result: 机器告密应当被理解为社会中重要的规则打破机制，需要建立规范基础；政府监管机构需要明确机器应被允许告密的内容以及如何法律保护开发告密机器的人。

Conclusion: 机器告密必须是规范性和原则性的，根植于现有的告密理论；需要政府监管机构就机器告密的内容和法律保护制定明确立场。

Abstract: Artificial intelligent agents and autonomous systems are embedded in our environments. They are both a commercial product and a personal tool that generates a lot of data and can draw conclusions from it: machines generate and keep secrets. But should machines protect all secrets? It has been shown that artificial agents are able to whistleblow and it has been argued that digital multi-agent environments should allow for agents in them to whistleblow. We argue that machine whistleblowing must be normative and principled and routed in the existing understanding of whistleblowing as an important rule-breaking mechanism in society. We also argue that there is a need for government regulators to formulate an informed stance on both what machines should be allowed to whistleblow on and how to legally protect those who develop whistleblowing machines

</details>


### [153] [ARCO: Adaptive Rubric with Co-Evolution for Multi-Step LLM-Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21262)
*Zihang Tian,Jingsen Zhang,Rui Li,Xiaohe Bo,Yuanzi Li,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCO提出了一种自适应评分标准协同进化框架，通过共享骨干网络的生成头和评分头实现步骤级别的奖励分配，并让评分标准与策略在参数层面共同进化，在多个多步问答数据集上取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法基于标量奖励无法解释轨迹优劣的原因；基于评分标准的奖励虽可通过自然语言标准提高可解释性，但现有方法仅能在轨迹级别评分，且评分标准固定不变，无法解决步骤级别的信用分配问题。

Method: 提出ARCO框架，使用同规模模型μ共享骨干网络，包含两个头：生成头产生每步评分标准，评分头预测基于评分标准的步骤级奖励。通过轨迹分解约束将步骤奖励之和与最终结果绑定，实现无需步骤标签的信用分配；同时μ与策略π在在线策略数据上联合更新，使评分标准内容和评分函数在参数层面协同进化。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，使用两种开源骨干模型，ARCO在所有设置下均优于强基线方法（包括基于结果、评分标准和过程奖励的方法），取得了最佳Exact Match分数。

Conclusion: ARCO生成的评分标准具有步骤特异性，对设计选择具有鲁棒性，并且可用于诊断智能体行为。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-step LLM agents often relies on scalar rewards that indicate success but cannot explain why a trajectory is good or bad. Rubric-based rewards improve interpretability through natural-language criteria, but existing methods score at the trajectory level and freeze the scorer behind a closed-source judge, leaving step-level credit assignment unresolved and the judge itself static. We propose ARCO (Adaptive Rubric CO-evolution), a rubric framework in which a same-scale model $μ$ shares a backbone with two heads: a generation head that produces per-step criteria, and a score head that predicts rubric-conditioned step-level rewards. A trajectory decomposition constraint ties the sum of step rewards to the terminal outcome, enabling credit assignment without step-level labels, while $μ$ and the policy $π$ are jointly updated on on-policy data so that the rubric content and the scoring function co-evolve at the parameter level. Across HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue with two open-source backbones, ARCO improves the best EM in every setting over strong outcome-, rubric-, and process-reward baselines, and analyses show that its rubrics are step-specific, robust to design choices, and useful for diagnosing agent behavior. Codes and data are available at https://github.com/zihangtian/ARCO.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Dys-XAI: Influence-Based Explanations for Dysarthria Severity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21306)
*Xiaoliang Wu,Qiyang Sun,Yupei Li,Erfan Loweimi,Jennifer Williams,Zhengjun Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于影响力的实例级可解释性框架，通过支持性和竞争性训练样本来解释语音严重度评估模型的决策，并采用受控删除实验验证解释的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍严重度评估依赖人工感知评分，耗时且评分者间差异大；深度学习模型虽性能优异但黑箱特性限制临床采用；现有语音可解释方法提供的声学特征重要性分数难以被终端用户理解。

Method: 提出基于梯度的影响近似方法，计算每个语音片段的实例级影响力分数，识别支持性（促进预测）和竞争性（抑制预测）训练样本；通过受控删除实验（5%-20%删除率）验证解释质量。

Result: 在5%至20%的受控删除实验中，移除高影响力样本会系统性改变模型预测结果，验证了所提解释方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将决策链接到可感知的参考案例，提供了可审计的解释，有望提升临床对深度学习评估工具的信任和采纳。

Abstract: Dysarthria severity assessment is essential for therapy planning and longitudinal monitoring, yet manual perceptual rating is time-consuming and variable across clinicians. Although deep learning models achieve strong performance, their black-box nature limits clinical adoption. Existing speech explainability methods typically provide acoustic feature importance scores that are difficult for end-users to interpret. We propose an influence-based, instance-level explainability framework that explains each decision through supportive and competing training samples. Using gradient-based influence approximations, we compute per-utterance influence scores to identify supportive and competing training samples for each prediction. Controlled deletion experiments from 5 to 20 percent validate the explanations, showing that removing highly influential samples systematically shifts predictions. This approach provides auditable explanations by linking decisions to perceptible reference cases.

</details>


### [155] [Social World Model for Lifelong Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21315)
*Yu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出社会世界模型（Social World Model），将社交互动解构为五个维度构建闭环学习框架，通过交互经验生成偏好信号更新模型策略，并提供可复用的数据合成机制与终身学习基准。实验表明，交互训练的Qwen2.5-7B在五项核心指标上全面超越基线，零遗忘地匹配甚至超越闭源模型Gemini 3 Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦于静态能力评估，忽视了社交能力的持续塑造与积累过程。社交交互轨迹缺乏统一结构化表征以形成可迭代学习信号，能力提升与保持被孤立研究，阻碍了持续演化的评估。

Method: 提出Social World Model，将社交互动分解为场景设置、观察、心理状态、行动和对话五个维度，构建闭环学习框架。智能体收集交互经验转化为偏好信号更新模型，再部署更新后的策略继续学习。同时提供可复用的数据合成机制和终身学习基准。

Result: 在ASCENT-Bench上，Qwen2.5-7B在五项核心指标上超越基线，在完成率上匹配Gemini 3 Flash，通过率超越之，且三个难度级别上实现零遗忘。

Conclusion: 该端到端方法提供了可训练、可验证、可保持的路径，证明小型开源模型能够可持续地获得具有竞争力的社交协作能力，将社交能力从"评估对象"转变为"可持续训练对象"。

Abstract: Social intelligence is a core competency for language agents, yet current research primarily focuses on static capability evaluation rather than how these skills are continuously shaped and accumulated. This gap calls for a shift toward sustainable learning paradigms. Currently, two methodological pain points exist: social interaction trajectories lack unified structured representations to form iterable learning signals, and capability improvement and retention are typically studied in isolation, hindering the assessment of continuous evolution.
  To bridge this gap, we propose the Social World Model. We decompose social interaction into five dimensions (scene setting, observation, mental state, action, and dialogue) to build a closed-loop learning framework. In this setup, agents collect interaction experiences, convert them into preference signals for model updating, and redeploy the updated policy for continued learning. Additionally, we provide a reusable data synthesis mechanism and a lifelong learning benchmark, transforming social capabilities from an "object of evaluation" into an "object of sustainable training".
  Validating our framework on the ASCENT-Bench, the interactively trained Qwen2.5-7B model outperforms its baseline across all five core metrics. Notably, it matches the closed-source Gemini 3 Flash in completion rate, exceeds it in pass rate, and achieves zero forgetting across three difficulty levels. Unlike prior works that merely report static comparisons or capability decay, this end-to-end approach provides a trainable, verifiable, and retainable pathway, demonstrating that small open-source models can sustainably acquire competitive social coordination capabilities.

</details>


### [156] [Mind the Noise: Sensitivity of Transformer-based Interaction-Aware Trajectory Prediction Models to Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21344)
*Shahab Salehi,Luca Lusvarghi,Miguel Sepulcre,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Transformer的交互感知轨迹预测模型在噪声输入下的性能退化问题，发现预测精度在低噪声下下降1.3倍，高噪声下下降3.9倍，凸显了模型对噪声数据的强敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测模型多基于长距离高质量数据集训练和评估，但实际部署中物体状态估计（位置、速度、航向）受感知不确定性和定位误差影响，尤其通过V2X通信接收的数据噪声更大。作者旨在分析这种噪声对轨迹预测精度的实际影响。

Method: 采用状态-of-the-art的基于Transformer的交互感知轨迹预测模型，引入不同强度的噪声（从低到高），评估噪声对预测精度的影响。

Result: 预测精度在低噪声下降低1.3倍，在最高噪声条件（但现实存在）下降低3.9倍。

Conclusion: 轨迹预测模型对噪声数据高度敏感，需要更真实的训练和评估数据集以及噪声缓解策略。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction allows autonomous vehicles to anticipate the future behavior of surrounding objects (or agents) and, accordingly, maximize the safety and efficiency of their driving. State-of-the-art Transformed-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models, which rely on attention mechanisms to capture multi-agent interactions and maximize prediction accuracy, are commonly trained and evaluated on long-range high-quality datasets. These datasets are typically obtained by aggregating data from multiple vehicles or drones and removing any object detection or tracking noise offline. Yet, information about a surrounding object's state (its position, speed, heading) is far from being noiseless in real-world deployments. Object state estimation is affected by perception uncertainties and localization errors that can be particularly large for objects received via Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications. In this paper, we analyze the impact of noisy object state information on the trajectory prediction accuracy of a state-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction model. Our study demonstrates that trajectory prediction accuracy can rapidly deteriorate as the noise intensity increases. Numerical results show that the prediction accuracy can reduce by a 1.3x factor under small noise levels and by as much as a 3.9x factor under the highest (yet realistic) noise conditions. These findings reveal the strong sensitivity of trajectory prediction models to noisy data, underscoring the need for more realistic training and evaluation datasets as well as noise mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [157] [Calibration Is Not Control: Why LLM-Agent Oversight Needs Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21399)
*Chubin Zhang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yu,Jingxuan Wu,Qi Wen,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM智能体的运行时监督应从标量风险评分转向基于动作条件化的价值估计，因为前者存在目标误差——相同风险估计可能对应可恢复与不可恢复的不同轨迹，而干预优势（预期效用增益）才是正确的决策对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体监督框架将运行时监督简化为标量风险预测（估计失败概率、置信度或不确定性，超出阈值即干预），但本文论证这种框架定位了错误的控制目标——关键问题不是智能体继续执行时失败的可能性有多大，而是可用的干预措施能否改善结果。两个轨迹前缀可能具有相同风险估计却需要不同干预动作，因为一个可恢复而另一个不可恢复。

Method: 形式化了标量风险与干预决策之间的错配为"目标误差"，提出"干预优势"（预期效用增益）作为监督的决策对象。引入"前缀分支"（prefix branching）——一种同前缀反事实协议，从相同轨迹状态执行候选动作。在四个基准上评估动作条件化控制与标量路由的性能差异，并进行校准分解实验。

Result: 动作条件化控制相比标量路由在四个基准上呈现情境依赖的增益。校准分解表明：重新校准相同的标量分数可改善预测指标，但控制遗憾不变，说明校准本身不能修复目标误差。简单的仅前缀动作条件化控制器在最强交互情境下显著降低遗憾，ALFWorld上从0.506降至0.110。当干预措施薄弱或标量路由已保留干预相关信息时，增益缩小。

Conclusion: LLM智能体监督应从标量风险评分转向动作条件化价值估计，因为标量校准不足以解决目标误差问题。

Abstract: Runtime oversight for LLM agents is commonly framed as scalar risk prediction: estimate failure likelihood, confidence, or uncertainty, then intervene once the score crosses a threshold. We argue that this framing targets the wrong object for control. The relevant question is not how likely the agent is to fail if it continues, but whether an available intervention would improve the outcome. Two trajectory prefixes can have the same risk estimate while requiring different actions, because one remains recoverable and the other does not. We formalize this mismatch as target error and identify intervention advantage, the expected utility gain from intervening rather than continuing, as the decision object for oversight. To measure this mismatch, we introduce prefix branching, a same-prefix counterfactual protocol that executes candidate actions from identical trajectory states. Across four benchmarks, action-conditioned control yields regime-dependent gains over scalar routing. In a calibration decomposition, recalibrating the same scalar score improves prediction metrics but leaves control regret unchanged, showing that calibration alone does not repair target error. A simple prefix-only action-conditioned controller substantially reduces regret in the strongest interactive regime, from 0.506 to 0.110 on ALFWorld. Gains shrink when interventions are weak or when scalar routing already preserves intervention-relevant information. These results suggest that LLM-agent oversight should move from calibrated risk scoring toward action-conditioned value estimation.

</details>


### [158] [Don't Blindly Trust It: How Unreliable Feedback Breaks Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21409)
*Chubin Zhang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yu,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Jingxuan Wu,Yaxin Zhou,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验发现，工具增强型智能体在获得不可靠反馈时，其性能可能低于无反馈基线，表明仅靠干净工具下的性能提升会高估工具的实际价值，因此需要引入匹配的无反馈对照来评估工具增强型智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型智能体评估通常依赖可靠外部反馈下的性能提升，但忽略了当反馈不可靠时的一个关键反事实问题：智能体是否反而更不接入任何任务证据？作者旨在探究不可靠反馈下工具价值的真实边界。

Method: 采用受控匹配循环对比实验，固定智能体循环、提示、动作空间和解码策略，仅改变返回的观测内容（忠实反馈、误导反馈、无反馈），在问答和事实验证任务上进行系统比较。

Result: 持续的误导性反馈会导致价值反转：干净工具下表现良好的智能体（HotpotQA上Qwen2.5-7B达44.8 F1）反而低于无反馈基线（22.3 F1），在打乱检索下降至4.7 F1。该反转在更强干净检索和局部合理干扰下依旧存在，但后期干净证据可修复轨迹时减弱。早期轨迹信号可预测许多失败，但简单修复受限于回退可靠性。

Conclusion: 干净工具下的性能提升会高估工具的实际价值，评估工具增强型智能体时必须纳入匹配的无反馈回退对照。

Abstract: Tool-augmented agents are typically evaluated by their gains under reliable external feedback. Yet these gains leave open a key counterfactual: when feedback is unreliable, would the agent be better off receiving no task evidence? We study this question with a controlled matched-loop comparison that fixes the agent loop, prompt, action space, and decoding, while varying only the returned observation: faithful, misleading, or absent. Across question answering and fact verification, persistent misleading feedback produces a value inversion: agents that benefit from clean tools can perform worse than the matched no-feedback fallback. On HotpotQA, Qwen2.5-7B reaches 44.8 F1 with clean retrieval and 22.3 F1 with no feedback, but drops to 4.7 F1 under shuffled retrieval. The inversion persists under stronger clean retrieval and locally plausible distractors, but weakens when later clean evidence can repair the trajectory. Early trajectory signals predict many failures, yet simple repairs remain fallback-limited: rejecting bad evidence helps only when the exposed fallback is reliable. These results show that clean-tool gains can overstate tool value, and that matched no-feedback fallback controls are necessary for evaluating tool-augmented agents.

</details>


### [159] [AutoRAS: Learning Robust Agentic Systems with Primitive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21445)
*Yang Yue,Xuancheng Zhu,Yuyang Ma,Guoshun Nan,Zihan Dou,Jingru Shan,Congyu Guo,Ji Zhang,Hua Wang,Jingfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRAS 是一个面向鲁棒多智能体系统自动设计的框架，通过符号原语序列编码结构与行为，利用执行安全信号和流式序列目标优化，在常规和对抗场景下均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化多智能体工作流设计方法主要追求任务性能，鲁棒性常被忽略，导致系统易受外部攻击和内部故障影响。

Method: 将系统设计形式化为生成符号原语序列（联合编码结构连接和行为动作），利用执行衍生的安全信号和基于流的序列级目标来优化该序列。

Result: 在常规和对抗设置下均取得最佳性能，攻击下性能退化最小；展现强可迁移性、稳定优化行为、原语集稳定性和良好成本权衡。

Conclusion: AutoRAS 证明了将鲁棒性显式纳入自动化智能体系统设计的可行性和有效性，为构建安全可靠的 LLM 多智能体系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: The automated design of agentic systems offers a promising pathway for scaling large language models (LLMs) beyond single-agent reasoning. While prior work has advanced task performance through handcrafted or automatically generated multi-agent workflows, robustness is often treated as an afterthought, leaving systems vulnerable to external adversaries and internal failures. We propose AutoRAS, a framework for the Automated design of Robust Agentic Systems. AutoRAS formulates system design as generating a sequence of symbolic primitives that jointly encode structural connectivity and behavioral actions, and learns to optimize this sequence using execution-derived safety signals and flow-based sequence-level objectives. Extensive experiments show that AutoRAS achieves the best performance in both vanilla and adversarial settings, with the smallest performance degradation under attacks. Further analyses demonstrate strong transferability, stable optimization behavior, stability across primitive sets, and favorable cost trade-offs. Our code is available at $\href{https://github.com/guohezuy/AutoRAS}{\text{this https URL}}$.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Transparent Mental Health Insights: An Explainable AI Model for Career-Related Depression and Anxiety Among University Students Using Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21474)
*Arsham Azam,Rasikh Ali,Tayyaba Farhat,Sheeraz Akram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多模态数据和联邦学习的可解释AI框架，用于隐私保护且文化敏感地识别大学生职业焦虑和抑郁的早期指标。模型在巴基斯坦学生数据集上取得92.08%的准确率和89.12%的F1分数，并通过Integrated Gradients和SHAP识别出回避直视、面部表情减少和社交退缩等关键行为标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学生职业焦虑和抑郁对心理健康和学业成就构成日益严峻的挑战，现有方法在隐私保护、文化适应性和可解释性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用多模态数据（结构化行为数据+面试视频面部情感特征）通过中间融合神经网络和注意力机制进行融合；应用标签平滑提升泛化性；利用联邦学习实现跨机构协作训练而不共享原始数据；使用Integrated Gradients和SHAP进行模型解释。

Result: 模型在巴基斯坦大学生心理健康调查数据集上达到F1分数89.12%、召回率86.54%、准确率92.08%、精确率91.88%。识别出回避直视、面部表情减少、社交退缩等关键行为标志。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可解释、可扩展且上下文敏感的AI系统用于心理健康预诊断，具有整合到全球学生支持服务中的潜力。

Abstract: Career anxiety and depression among university students present a growing challenge to mental health and academic achievement. This study proposes an Explainable AI (XAI) framework using multimodal data and Federated Learning (FL) to identify early indicators of career-related mental health problems in a privacy-preserving and culturally responsive manner. The framework combines structured behavioral data and facial emotion features from interview videos via an intermediate fusion neural network with attention mechanisms. Label smoothing was applied to improve model generalizability. FL was used across institutions to enable collaborative training without raw data sharing. Evaluation was conducted using the Student Mental Health Survey dataset from university students across Pakistan. Our model attained an F1-score of 89.12%, recall of 86.54%, accuracy of 92.08%, and precision of 91.88%. Using Integrated Gradients and SHAP, the model identified key behavioral markers of depression including avoidance of direct gaze, lower facial expressiveness, and social withdrawal, consistent with psychological theory. This research presents an interpretable, scalable, and context-sensitive AI system for mental health pre-diagnosis with potential integration into student support services globally.

</details>


### [161] [Balancing Performance and Diversity in GRPO Autoregressive Text-to-Image Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21498)
*Yuanhao Chiang,Hongbo Duan,Chunru Yang,Jiahua Pei,Yi Liu,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自回归文生图模型中GRPO风格在线强化学习的参考策略散度固定问题，提出在统一f-散度框架（前向KL、反向KL、JS散度）下进行分析，理论证明JS散度能实现最优的优化性能与生成多样性平衡，在LlamaGen和Janus-7B上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO风格在线强化学习方法在自回归文生图对齐中，将参考策略散度视为固定超参数，忽略了不同散度对策略优化的直接影响，缺乏系统性的理论分析。

Method: 在统一f-散度框架下对前向KL、反向KL和JS散度进行理论分析，揭示不同散度对token级更新的不同重塑方式，特别提出JS正则化在采样token形式下能缓解均匀偏置同时抑制大偏差。

Result: 在LlamaGen和Janus-7B上的大量实验表明，JS散度在大多数评估指标上取得最强或高度竞争的优化性能，同时保持良好的生成多样性。

Conclusion: JS散度是自回归文生图GRPO对齐中更优的正则化选择，为后续强化学习对齐研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-image (T2I) generation has recently advanced rapidly, yet aligning generated images with human preferences remains challenging. GRPO-style online reinforcement learning provides an effective framework; however, existing methods typically treat reference-policy divergence as fixed, despite its direct impact on policy optimization. We study this overlooked factor within a unified f-divergence framework, encompassing forward KL, reverse KL, and JS divergence, for GRPO-style autoregressive T2I alignment. Our systematic theoretical analysis reveals that different divergences reshape token-level updates in distinct ways. In particular, under the sampled-token shaping form used, JS regularization achieves a favorable trade-off by mitigating uniform bias relative to the reference policy while still discouraging large deviations. Extensive experiments on LlamaGen and Janus-7B show that JS divergence achieves the strongest or highly competitive optimization performance on most evaluation metrics while maintaining favorable generation diversity. The code is available at https://github.com/tuoyou-hao/BPD-GRPO.

</details>


### [162] [AI Alignment From Social Choice Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21550)
*Daniel Halpern,Evi Micha,Ariel D. Procaccia,Benjamin Schiffer,Itai Shapira,Shirley Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了从人类反馈中学习对齐时，当人类判断存在冲突时如何通过社会选择理论进行偏好聚合的研究，揭示了现有聚合层的失败模式并提出了处理分歧的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通过人类反馈进行对齐时，不同人类评价者可能对“理想行为”存在分歧，现有方法将冲突观点简单地聚合为单一目标，导致模型行为失当。本文旨在引入社会选择理论来系统分析并改进这一聚合过程。

Method: 对近年来将社会选择理论应用于人类反馈聚合的相关工作进行了系统综述，分析了不同聚合机制（如投票、排序聚合等）在反馈层中的适用性，并识别了聚合过程中的失败模式。

Result: 揭示了反馈聚合层中存在的失败模式（如阿罗不可能定理等社会选择理论中的经典问题），并展示了更广泛的设计空间，包括显式处理人类意见分歧的原则性方法。

Conclusion: 社会选择视角有助于识别反馈聚合中的问题，并为设计更鲁棒、更公正的人类反馈对齐方法提供了理论指导，未来应更加注重在反馈聚合中显式地处理价值分歧。

Abstract: Alignment from human feedback uses human judgments about model outputs to steer the behavior of language models after pretraining. When those judgments reflect conflicting views of desirable behavior, the learned objective becomes an aggregate determination of what the model should prefer. We survey recent work that has studied this aggregation problem through the lens of social choice theory. We illustrate how the social choice perspective helps identify failure modes in the feedback aggregation layer and reveals a broader design space for handling disagreement in explicit and principled ways.

</details>


### [163] [Composing Verifiable Conceptual Models via Building Blocks: Towards Design-Time Verification of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21565)
*Noe Y. Flandre,Alexander C. Nwala,Philippe J. Giabbanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对Agentic AI工作流的设计时验证方法，通过12条结构性规则检查可复用构建块的兼容性，并在两个数据集上验证了该方法能有效检测设计缺陷，即使这些缺陷被结构变换所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI平台侧重于运行时安全保障，但在系统设计阶段缺乏对工作流进行验证的支持。从建模与仿真角度看，这相当于在未验证构建块交互一致性的情况下组合概念模型，存在设计缺陷无法早期发现的风险。

Method: 将Agentic工作流建模为可复用构建块的组合，并定义了12条结构性规则来检查构建块之间的兼容性。实现了软件原型验证器，并在两个公开数据集（48个含已知缺陷的工作流和168个保持逻辑但改变图结构的变体）上进行了评估。

Result: 验证器能够可靠地检测违规行为，即使缺陷设计通过任务拆分等结构变换被掩盖，仍能有效识别。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了Agentic AI系统设计时验证的空白。未来可将该验证方法与社区构建块仓库结合，以组合安全的Agentic工作流。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents through workflow architectures that coordinate decisions, tools, and external actions. While current platforms emphasize runtime safeguards, little support exists for verifying workflows during system design. From a Modeling \& Simulation perspective, this gap is analogous to composing conceptual models without verifying whether their building blocks interact coherently. We propose a design-time verification approach that models agentic workflows as compositions of reusable building blocks and checks their compatibility through twelve structural rules. We implemented these rules in a software prototype and evaluated them using two openly released datasets: 48 workflows with known design flaws and 168 variants that preserve workflow logic but alter graph structure. Results show that our verifier reliably detects violations even when flawed designs are obscured through structural transformations such as splitting tasks between agents. Future works could combine our verification with community repositories of building blocks to compose safe agentic workflows.

</details>


### [164] [Counsel: A Meta-Evaluation Dataset for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21627)
*Sashank Pisupati,Henry Broomfield,Eujeong Choi,Antonia Calvi,Charlie Wang,Roman Engeler,Max Bartolo,Patrick Lewis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Counsel是首个面向智能体任务的元评估公开数据集，包含开源LLM裁判对tau-bench和DA-Code两个智能体基准测试的过程级批评及人工元评估。研究发现，更强的裁判模型和更多推理努力能提升与人类的一致性，最强裁判在错误定位上达~88%一致性，推理上达~65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统处理日益复杂的多步任务时，其轨迹评估成为瓶颈——人工标注单条轨迹可能耗时数小时，导致难以规模化评估性能或整理训练数据。尽管LLM-as-a-judge被广泛用于自动化评估，但其批评的可靠性往往未被衡量。

Method: 构建Counsel数据集：使用开源权重LLM裁判对tau-bench（客服智能体）和DA-Code（编程智能体）生成过程级批评；人工标注者对每条标注错误标记为"精准命中"、"位置正确但推理不佳"或"不应标注"，达到Krippendorff's alpha 0.78的可靠一致性。数据集按人类对齐程度对LLM裁判批评进行分层，涵盖错误位置和推理质量。

Result: 更强的裁判模型和更多推理努力能提升与人类的一致性；最强裁判在错误定位上约88%与人类一致，推理质量上约65%一致。

Conclusion: Counsel数据集为校准、改进或训练面向智能体的LLM裁判提供了有价值的数据，并采用开放许可供社区广泛使用，有望促进对LLM评估器在智能体系统中对齐的严格研究。

Abstract: As agentic systems tackle increasingly complex multi-step tasks, evaluating their trajectories presents a major bottleneck - human annotation of a single trajectory on popular agentic benchmarks can take hours, making it difficult to scale evaluations for measuring performance or curating training data. This has driven widespread reliance on automated approaches such as LLM-as-a-judge (LLMJ) to critique agents at the process and outcome-levels at scale, however, the soundness of LLMJ critiques often goes unmeasured. Here, we introduce Counsel, the first public dataset of meta-evaluations for agentic tasks. Counsel consists of process-level critiques from open-weight LLMJs on two agent benchmarks: tau-bench (customer support agents) and DA-Code (coding agents), and human meta-evaluations of these critiques. Human annotators label critiques on each flagged error as "spot on", "correct location but poor reasoning", or "should not have flagged", achieving reliable inter-annotator agreement (Krippendorff's alpha of 0.78). The resulting dataset stratifies LLMJ critiques by human alignment across both error location within a trajectory and reasoning quality, serving as valuable data to calibrate, improve, or train LLMJs for agents. Comparing open-weight judges, we find that more capable judge models and more reasoning effort both enabled improved human agreement, with the strongest judge reaching ~88% agreement on location and ~65% on reasoning. Counsel is generated using open-weight models and is permissively licensed for broad community use, which we hope will enable rigorous study and improved alignment of LLM-based evaluators for agentic systems.

</details>


### [165] [ChainWorld: Composing Long-Horizon Desktop Workloads from Atomic OSWorld Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21654)
*Vincent Siu,Manasi Sharma,Dawn Song,Daniel Yue Zhang,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有计算机使用代理评估几乎只关注原子化桌面任务，但实际桌面工作需要跨多个目标维持状态。本文提出ChainWorld，通过方向兼容性搜索将OSWorld原子任务组合成长序列桌面工作负载，生成347条长度为2-4的任务链，并比较两种任务呈现方式。单轮评估中所有任务一次性呈现，多轮评估中任务逐个揭示。四种当前代理的最大链完成率为31%。多轮评估提升了三个模型的完成率，但两种协议仍然具有挑战性，且暴露了不同的失败模式：单轮失败集中于工件精度，多轮失败更多反映会话管理问题（如碎片化进度和后期任务脱节）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理评估局限于孤立的原子任务，无法反映真实桌面工作中需要跨多个目标维持状态和上下文的复杂场景，存在评估与真实应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出ChainWorld框架，通过方向兼容性搜索将OSWorld原子任务组合成长序列工作负载，保留原始评估器；设计了单轮（任务一次性呈现）和多轮（任务逐个揭示）两种评估协议。

Result: 构建了347条任务链；四种当前计算机使用代理的最大链完成率仅31%；多轮评估提升了部分模型表现，但两种协议都极具挑战性；两种协议暴露了不同的失败模式。

Conclusion: 现有代理在长序列桌面任务上表现有限，单轮评估暴露工件精度问题，多轮评估暴露会话管理问题，表明需要针对长期任务能力进行专门改进。

Abstract: Computer use agents are evaluated almost exclusively on atomic desktop tasks, but realistic desktop work requires sustaining state across multiple objectives. We study this gap with ChainWorld, which composes atomic OSWorld tasks into long horizon desktop workloads through directional compatibility search while preserving the source evaluators. The resulting workload contains 347 chains of length two to four and compares two renderings of the same task sequence. In single turn evaluation, all tasks are presented together in one prompt. In multi turn evaluation, tasks are revealed one at a time. Across four current computer use agents, maximum chain completion is 31%. Multi turn evaluation improves completion for three models, but both protocols remain challenging. The two protocols also expose different failure profiles. Single turn failures concentrate on artifact precision, while multi turn failures more often reflect session management problems such as fragmented progress and later turn disengagement.

</details>


### [166] [Hallucination as Context Drift: Synchronization Protocols for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21666)
*Carson Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统中的幻觉问题并非仅由模型能力不足引起，而主要源于**上下文漂移**（即并发智能体之间内部知识状态的分歧）。本文提出**上下文分歧得分（CDS）**量化智能体间知识状态差异，并设计**共享状态验证协议（SSVP）**，通过周期性交换压缩状态摘要和标记高分歧条件来减少幻觉。实验表明，盲目全广播同步会使幻觉率上升34%（污染效应），而SSVP在减少58%API调用的同时显著降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将多智能体幻觉归因于模型自身缺陷，但作者观察到一类重要的失败模式源于并发智能体之间共享世界状态的表示不一致（即上下文漂移），而非模型能力不足。需要将幻觉缓解问题重新定义为分布式系统问题。

Method: 1. 定义**上下文分歧得分（CDS）**：在空间、时间和任务三个维度上量化智能体对之间知识状态差异的轻量级标量指标。2. 提出**共享状态验证协议（SSVP）**：智能体周期性交换压缩状态摘要，在联合推理前标记高分歧条件，避免盲目同步带来的污染。

Result: 在旅行规划（n=30/条件）和软件项目规划（n=10）8个场景中：1）全广播同步导致幻觉率比无同步基线上升34%（HR: 0.658 vs. 0.492, p=0.0022, d=1.18），即污染效应。2）SSVP避免该问题，幻觉率降至0.463（d=0.30），与全广播相比显著降低（p=0.0005, d=1.47），且API调用减少58%。3）污染效应在软件领域未复现（所有条件HR<0.2），表明该效应仅出现在单一错误信念级联传播的任务中。

Conclusion: 应将多智能体LLM系统中的幻觉缓解重新定义为分布式系统问题，并将上下文同步确立为多智能体LLM设计中的第一类原语。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems routinely produce hallucinated outputs that cannot be explained by model deficiencies alone. A significant class of these failures arises not from model incapacity but from context drift: the divergence of internal knowledge states between concurrent agents. When agents enter a collaborative task with mismatched or stale representations of shared world state, their joint reasoning produces contradictions that manifest as hallucination. We define the Context Divergence Score (CDS), a lightweight scalar metric quantifying knowledge-state discrepancy between agent pairs across spatial, temporal, and task dimensions, and propose the Shared State Verification Protocol (SSVP), which lets agents periodically exchange compressed state summaries and flag high-divergence conditions before joint reasoning. We evaluate SSVP across two domains (multi-agent travel and software project planning) using Claude Haiku. In controlled experiments (n=30 per condition, travel; n=10, software) across 8 scenarios, naive full-broadcast synchronization increases hallucination rate by 34% above the no-sync baseline (HR: 0.658 vs. 0.492, p=0.0022, d=1.18), a contamination effect from propagating erroneous agent states. SSVP avoids this failure mode while showing modest, consistent reduction (HR: 0.463, d=0.30) and achieves significantly lower hallucination than full-broadcast (p=0.0005, d=1.47) using 58% fewer API calls. The contamination effect does not replicate in the software domain, where all conditions converge to low HR (<0.2), confirming it is specific to tasks where one erroneous shared belief cascades across evaluation dimensions. Our results reframe hallucination mitigation as a distributed systems problem and establish context synchronization as a first-class primitive in multi-agent LLM design.

</details>


### [167] [Entropy Objectives in Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21726)
*S. Akshay,Raghav Goyal,Aditya Neeraje,Piyush Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机系统中综合满足状态分布集中性质的控制策略问题，形式化为马尔可夫决策过程中基于熵的目标策略综合，证明了问题的复杂性，提出了一种结合凸对偶与不变式合成的完备方法，并探讨了记忆与随机化的作用，最后通过实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统中状态分布的集中性质对于系统的安全性、稳定性和性能保障至关重要，然而现有方法难以有效处理这类基于熵的非线性目标，亟需一种形式化的验证与综合方法。

Method: 将问题形式化为MDP中基于熵的目标策略综合问题，通过凸对偶与不变式合成相结合的技术路径，处理熵目标的非线性特性，构建了可靠且相对完备的验证与综合框架。

Result: 证明了即使松弛版本的问题也具有较高的计算复杂度；所提出的方法在理论上具有可靠性且条件相对完备；实验结果表明方法在实际基准测试中有效。

Conclusion: 本文为随机系统中基于熵的集中性质策略综合提供了理论框架与实用算法，揭示了记忆与随机化在实现熵目标中的关键作用。

Abstract: We consider the problem of synthesizing control policies that enforce a concentration property on the state distributions of a stochastic system. We present a formalization of this problem in terms of synthesizing strategies for maintaining an entropy-based objective in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). We first show that even relaxed versions of this problem are complexity-theoretically hard. We then present a sound and (conditionally) relatively complete method to verify and synthesize strategies for such entropy objectives. The main challenge is the non-linear nature of such objectives, and our approach addresses this by exploiting and combining ideas from convex duality and invariant synthesis. We also investigate the role of memory and randomization in ensuring entropy objectives. Finally, we implement our ideas to evaluate our approach empirically on a few illustrative benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [Training the Orchestrator: A Supervised Approach to End-to-End PDDL Planning with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21740)
*Rajesh Mangannavar,Zachary Coalson,Pranay Dugar,Prasad Tadepalli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HALO是一个混合智能体-学习编排器，通过QLoRA微调的小策略和三条硬编码规则替代大型语言模型作为编排器，利用验证器提供的修正轨迹进行训练，在多个规划基准上匹配或超越前沿LLM基线，同时将编排成本降低一个数量级以上（约45倍），并将每轮总LLM调用减少40-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言规划意图转化为验证计划是一个长期挑战：人们用语言表达目标，而经典规划器需要形式化的PDDL规范。现有智能体框架通过编排一组专门的修复智能体在验证器检查的循环中弥合这一差距，但中心的编排器本身是前沿LLM，每一步都需要付费API调用，成本高昂。

Method: HALO将小型的QLoRA微调策略与三条针对可简单判定选择的硬编码规则配对，在21个智能体的动作空间上运行。关键观察是验证器已经提供了强指导：每个被接受的轨迹都是可证明正确的（状态，智能体）决策序列，可直接用作监督信号来训练编排器。

Result: 在PlanBench、Natural Plan和经典规划基准上，HALO匹配或超越GPT-5-mini基线的成功率，与更强的Gemini-3-Flash基线差距在3个百分点以内；编排成本降低一个数量级以上（相对GPT-5-mini约45倍、相对Gemini-3-Flash约15倍）；每轮LLM调用减少40-50%。

Conclusion: 利用验证器提供的修正轨迹作为监督信号来训练小模型替代前沿LLM进行编排，可以在保持规划性能的同时大幅降低成本和LLM调用次数，为自然语言规划意图的验证式规划提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Translating natural-language planning intent into verified plans is a longstanding challenge: people communicate goals in language, while classical planners require formal PDDL specifications. Recent agentic frameworks bridge this gap by orchestrating a pool of specialized repair agents inside a verifier-checked refinement loop, but the orchestrator at the centre is itself a prompted frontier LLM, paying a frontier-LLM API call at every refinement step. We present HALO (Hybrid Agent-Learned Orchestrator), which trains the orchestrator from refinement trajectories that an external verifier has certified as ending in valid plans, across 11 PDDL domains. HALO pairs a small QLoRA-tuned policy with three hardcoded rules for trivially decidable selections, and operates over an expanded 21-agent action space. Unlike approaches that prompt a frontier LLM at every step or learn an orchestrator from sparse end-of-episode rewards, our key observation is that the verifier already provides strong guidance: every accepted trajectory is a sequence of demonstrably correct (state, agent) decisions, directly usable as supervision. Across PlanBench, Natural Plan, and classical planning benchmarks, HALO matches or exceeds the GPT-5-mini prompted baseline on success rate, sits within three percentage points of the stronger Gemini-3-Flash prompted baseline, reduces orchestration cost by more than an order of magnitude (\$0.18 to \$0.004 per task against GPT-5-mini, roughly 45$\times$ cheaper; roughly 15$\times$ cheaper than Gemini-3-Flash), and cuts total LLM calls per episode by 40 to 50 percent.

</details>


### [169] [AgentCAT: Simulating Computerized Adaptive Testing via Multi-Agent Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21832)
*Weiyuan Zhou,Haiping Ma,Xiaoshan Yu,Changqian Wang,Shangshang Yang,Xingyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentCAT，一个基于大语言模型的多智能体模拟系统，用于构建高保真的计算机自适应测试（CAT）基准测试环境，解决了现有CAT研究受限于静态离线数据和孤立组件优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAT研究受限于静态离线数据的部分标签和孤立组件优化，将动态评估过程退化为静态序列预测，缺乏对整体CAT交互过程的建模。

Method: 提出AgentCAT框架，包含三个模块：(1) 具有记忆检索和思维链推理的考生智能体，基于认知特征模拟作答；(2) 使用粗到细分桶和知识图谱探索的选题智能体，平衡局部难度与全局覆盖；(3) 采用双重审核和鲁棒更新的监督者智能体，确保收敛性和有效性。

Result: 在真实数据集上从宏观能力收敛、微观交互逻辑和数据稀疏鲁棒性三个维度进行验证，AgentCAT实现有效的能力估计，选题策略平衡了难度适应与教学连贯性，符合人类教学直觉。

Conclusion: AgentCAT通过多智能体模拟系统有效解决了CAT中的动态测试问题，为个性化教育中的自适应测试提供了高保真基准环境。

Abstract: Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT), as a key technology for personalized education, aims to accurately assess examinee proficiency by retrieving exercises dynamically matching current ability estimates. However, existing CAT research is constrained by limitations of static offline data and isolated component optimization. Restricted by partial labels in offline logs, researchers degrade the dynamic assessment process into static sequence prediction. Current research focuses on isolated perspectives, e.g., selection or diagnosis, neglecting the overall CAT interaction process. To address this, we propose AgentCAT, a Large Language Model-based multi-agent simulation system, to construct a high-fidelity benchmarking environment for dynamic testing. This framework comprises three modules: (1) The examinee agent with memory retrieval and Chain-of-Thought reasoning simulates responses based on cognitive profiles; (2) The selection agent uses coarse-to-fine bucketing and knowledge graph exploration to balance local difficulty and global coverage; (3) The supervisor uses dual-auditing and robust update to ensure convergence and validity. To validate the framework, we evaluated on two real-world datasets across three dimensions: macro-level ability convergence, micro-level interaction logic, and data sparsity resilience. Results show AgentCAT achieves effective ability estimation, and its selection strategy balances difficulty adaptation and instructional coherence, aligning with human pedagogical intuition.

</details>


### [170] [Measuring What Persists: Conditioning Mechanisms and a Geometric Framework for AI Agent Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21843)
*Andrew Tanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何框架（√JSD度量空间和丰富范畴论中的量同调）来测量AI代理身份结构的方法，验证发现身份规范在行为真空中填充、在安全盆地中位移，但漂移实验显示测量到的变化实际上是填充伪影而非真实上下文漂移，框架的诊断潜力尚未得到实证确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只能在质量退化明显后才能检测到AI代理在长上下文任务中的身份漂移，缺乏早期检测的理论框架。

Method: 使用√JSD度量空间和丰富范畴论中的量同调构建几何框架，将身份定义为非测地结构，漂移定义为向测地线的松弛过程。通过等边探针基线和扰动理论分析身份规范的响应模式，通过漂移实验测量上下文压力下的量变化。

Result: 发现了双机制条件结构：身份真空簇和安全盆地簇。等边探针基线显示身份规范创建了55种独特响应模式（基础模型仅1种）。漂移实验揭示量下降反映的是重复填充伪影而非真实上下文漂移，多样化填充在150K tokens内无可测量变形。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论上建立了身份结构的几何和拓扑形式化，但实验上尚未验证其完整的诊断能力（各向异性收缩和结构坍缩的同调简化检测）。

Abstract: AI agents in long-context applications drift from their specified identity. Current methods detect this only after qualitative degradation is visible. We present a geometric framework for measuring identity structure using $\sqrt{\mathrm{JSD}}$ metric spaces and magnitude homology from enriched category theory, where identity is non-geodesic structure and drift is its relaxation toward the geodesic.
  Validated on a persistent AI agent, the framework's strongest empirical finding is a two-mechanism conditioning structure: cross-condition distances reveal an identity-vacuum cluster where the identity specification fills a behavioral void, and a safety-basin cluster where it displaces from post-training attractors. An equilateral probe baseline confirms that the identity specification creates measurable behavioral richness (55 unique response patterns vs. 1 for the base model) at maximum probe separation. A first-order perturbation theory for equilateral configurations predicts magnitude changes from perimeter changes alone, with shape perturbations first-order cancelled by the $S_n$ symmetry; the formula is self-consistent at the observed perturbation amplitudes.
  A drift experiment measuring magnitude decrease under context pressure was subsequently found to reflect repetitive-padding artifacts rather than genuine context-length drift; diverse padding produces no measurable deformation through 150K tokens. The magnitude homology framework's full diagnostic promise -- detecting anisotropic contraction and structural collapse via homological simplification -- is architecturally grounded in the perturbation theory and selection rules but remains empirically unconfirmed.

</details>


### [171] [ForEx: A Formal Verification Framework for Explainable Reasoning in Logical Fallacy Detection and Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21867)
*Pei-Cing Huang,Chienyu Liu,Chan Hsu,Ci-Siang Chen,Pei-Ju Lee,Yihuang Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForEx框架，将LLM在逻辑谬误检测中生成的解释翻译为Lean4代码进行形式化验证，发现超过90%的推理链可通过形式验证，但与人类标注的一致性仅约20%，揭示了形式可推导性与标签一致性之间的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM逻辑谬误检测评估仅关注预测标签的正确性，未检验模型提供的推理过程是否真正支持其结论，缺乏对推理链条可解释性和可靠性的形式化验证。

Method: 提出ForEx框架，将LLM生成的解释翻译为Lean4形式化语言，在编码前提条件下验证翻译后的推理链是否可推导；并引入LLM参数验证矩阵，区分标签一致性与形式验证状态两个维度。

Result: 在LOGIC-Climate数据集上，超过90%的LLM输出可转化为通过形式验证的推理链，但与人类标注的一致性仅约20%。

Conclusion: 形式可推导性与标签一致性之间存在系统性差距，传统基于标签正确性的评估指标无法捕捉这一差异；ForEx将LLM评估从标签正确性扩展到可机器校验的形式化推理链分析。

Abstract: Current evaluations of Large Language Models (LLMs) on logical fallacy detection focus on predicted labels, but do not establish whether those labels are supported by the reasoning the models provide. We propose ForEx (Formal Verification for Explainable Reasoning), a framework that translates LLM-generated explanations into Lean4 and verifies whether the translated rationale is derivable under encoded premises, not the logical validity of the original natural language argument. To distinguish prediction outcomes from the formal status of the supporting reasoning, we introduce the LLM Argument Verification Matrix, which separates label consistency from formal verification status. Experiments on LOGIC-Climate show that over 90% of LLM outputs can be translated into formal reasoning chains that pass verification, while agreement with human annotations remains around 20%. These results expose a systematic gap between formal derivability and label agreement, a distinction invisible to prediction-based metrics. ForEx moves LLM evaluation beyond label correctness toward machine-checkable analysis of formalized reasoning chains.

</details>


### [172] [AgentRiskBOM: A Risk-Scoping Security Bill of Materials for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21877)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentRiskBOM，一种针对工具使用型AI代理的安全物料清单（BOM）扩展，填补了现有SBOM/AIBOM/MLBOM在代理透明度方面的能力不透明性缺口，通过添加运行时权限字段（自主性、工具权限、内存、凭证范围、审批门控、审计信号、代理间通信、外部行动能力）来实现风险范围界定。在13个开源代理和52个风险场景上的评估显示，其能力覆盖维度得分14/16（对比SBOM的1、AIBOM的1.5、MLBOM的2），风险类别可见性100%（对比SBOM-like的10.5%、AIBOM-like的20.9%），且差异检测器能100%识别33种部署突变类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BOM制品（SBOM、AIBOM、MLBOM）专注于依赖、模型元数据和训练溯源，但存在一个关键缺口——能力不透明性（capability opacity），即缺乏对部署后的AI代理能够访问、记忆、更改、委托以及事后证明的内容的结构化描述。这种不透明性使得风险范围界定无法进行。

Method: 提出AgentRiskBOM，作为SBOM/AIBOM/MLBOM的附加层，新增运行时权限字段（自主性、工具权限、内存、凭证范围、审批门控、审计信号、代理间通信、外部行动能力）。实现为JSON-schema制品，包含可复现语料库、风险场景、评分器、差异检测器、控制映射器和报告生成器。

Result: 在13个开源代理（覆盖编码、RAG、多代理原型）和52个风险场景（14个类别）上评估：schema验证全部通过；能力维度覆盖得分14/16（SBOM 1、AIBOM 1.5、MLBOM 2）；风险类别可见性100%（SBOM-like 10.5%、AIBOM-like 20.9%）；差异检测器对33种部署突变识别正确率100%；辅助惩罚评分器与主评分器Spearman相关系数0.73。

Conclusion: 代理型AI安全需要在事件发生之前就具备机器可读的权限和风险制品，AgentRiskBOM填补了这一空白，并为代理部署前的风险范围界定提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems retrieve private context, invoke tools, write files, call external services, coordinate with other agents, and may act without human approval. Existing bill of materials artifacts improve transparency for dependencies, model metadata, and training provenance, but leave an agentic transparency gap: capability opacity, the absence of a structured account of what a deployed agent can access, remember, change, delegate, and prove afterward. This paper introduces AgentRiskBOM, a security BOM for risk-scoping tool-using AI agents. It is an additive layer over SBOM, AIBOM, and MLBOM artifacts, referencing them where authoritative while adding fields for runtime authority: autonomy, tool permissions, memory, credential scope, approval gates, audit signals, inter-agent communication, and external action capability. We implement AgentRiskBOM as a JSON-schema artifact with a reproducible corpus, risk scenarios, scorer, diff detector, control mapper, and reports. We evaluate AgentRiskBOM on 13 open-source agents spanning coding, RAG, and multi-agent archetypes, plus 52 risk scenarios across 14 categories. The schema validates all 13 corpus artifacts. Coverage analysis gives AgentRiskBOM a native-equivalent score of 14 across 16 capability dimensions, vs. 1 for SBOM, 1.5 for AIBOM and 2 for MLBOM. Across modeled risk categories, AgentRiskBOM exposes 100% risk-category visibility vs. 10.5% for SBOM-like and 20.9% for AIBOM-like views. To test agentic authority drift, we inject 33 structured deployment mutations; the diff detector identifies the correct change type for all mutations. A secondary penalty-based scorer yields a Spearman correlation of 0.73 with the primary scorer, supporting rank-level consistency while showing that thresholds require human calibration. The results show that agentic AI security needs a machine-readable authority-and-risk artifact before incidents occur.

</details>


### [173] [Learning the ARTS of Search for Automated Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21891)
*Gurusha Juneja,Arnav Kumar Jain,Deepak Nathani,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARTS框架，利用推理语言模型进行树搜索，通过测试时训练将搜索树知识注入模型权重，在22个机器学习任务上超越现有算法，15.3%相对提升，且4B参数模型匹配闭源前沿模型性能，推理成本降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学发现搜索方法（如MCTS）将假设价值与实验执行质量相混淆，导致有潜力的假设因初步执行不佳而被低估；同时搜索日志累积超出上下文窗口导致历史信息被剪枝。亟需一种能解耦假设评估与执行质量、并有效利用完整搜索历史的方法。

Method: ARTS利用推理语言模型代理来导航假设-实验搜索空间。模型检查历史执行日志，诊断失败原因是实现错误还是假设缺陷，据此选择下一步构建的假设。为缓解上下文长度限制，采用测试时训练（test-time training）将搜索树知识编码到模型权重中，从而无需依赖有限的上下文窗口。

Result: 在MLGym和MLEBench的22个任务上，ARTS优于领先算法，归一化分数相对提升超15.3%。Qwen3-4B代理经测试时训练后匹配Gemini-3 Pro和GPT o3-reasoning等闭源前沿模型性能，推理成本降低5倍。在部分可观测RL任务中，4B模型重新发现了被启发式方法剪枝的人类最优循环记忆解，超越o3科学家。

Conclusion: ARTS通过推理语言模型与测试时训练的结合，有效解耦了假设评估与执行质量，解决了搜索历史上下文溢出问题，显著提升了科学发现搜索的效率与效果，展示了小模型通过针对性训练可匹敌甚至超越大型闭源模型的能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery can be formulated as an iterative search process over the space of hypotheses and experiments. Contemporary methods navigate this space using heuristics such as MCTS. These algorithms conflate the merit of a hypothesis with the quality of its experimental execution. A promising hypothesis with preliminary execution is therefore ranked below a modest hypothesis whose execution is refined. Moreover, prior methods prune the search logs as the search progresses because the accumulated history outgrows the context window. We propose Agentic Reasoning for Tree Search (ARTS), where we deploy a reasoning language model to navigate this space. The model inspects prior execution logs, diagnoses whether earlier failures arose from faulty implementations or bad hypotheses, and selects the hypothesis to build on next. To mitigate challenges with context length, ARTS uses test-time training to instill the knowledge of search tree in the model weights. Across 22 tasks from MLGym and MLEBench, we show that ARTS outperforms leading algorithms, with over 15.3% relative improvement in the normalized score. With test-time training we show that a Qwen3-4B agent can match performance with closed-source frontier models like Gemini-3 Pro and GPT o3-reasoning with upto 5x lower inference cost. We further observe that on partially observable RL tasks, the test-time trained Qwen3-4B scientist surpasses ARTS with the o3 scientist by rediscovering the human-best recurrent-memory solution that heuristic methods prune away.

</details>


### [174] [Holmes: Multimodal Agentic Diagnosis for Mixed-Language Mobile Crashes at Industrial Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21963)
*Jia Li,Wenyuan Ma,Ting Peng,Haibin Zheng,Yuetang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Holmes是一个多智能体系统，通过综合多模态运行时信号（堆栈轨迹、日志和线程状态），在不依赖复现环境的情况下自动进行根因分析。它采用分层Retrieve-Explore-Reason架构，利用低级构件（寄存器、汇编）弥合开源业务逻辑与闭源系统框架之间的语义鸿沟，在7000万行代码库中精确定位非局部缺陷。在微信真实崩溃案例上，Holmes实现了87.6%的函数级故障定位准确率，并将平均调查时间减少98%以上（降至约77秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模工业应用（如微信）中移动崩溃诊断面临巨大挑战：代码量庞大（7000万行）、混合语言环境复杂、无法在本地复现失败场景。传统静态分析难以扩展，而现有基于LLM的智能体通常需要可复现环境，在事后分析场景中不可用。

Method: Holmes采用多智能体系统，核心是分层Retrieve-Explore-Reason架构：1）综合多模态运行时信号（堆栈轨迹、日志、线程状态）重建失败上下文；2）利用低级构件（寄存器、汇编代码）作为桥梁，弥合开源业务逻辑层与闭源系统框架层之间的语义鸿沟；3）通过运行时线索动态压缩搜索空间，在7000万行代码中进行精准导航。

Result: 在微信真实崩溃案例上，Holmes实现了87.6%的函数级故障定位准确率，将平均调查时间减少98%以上（从数小时降至约77秒）。

Conclusion: Holmes证明了多智能体系统结合多模态运行时信号和层次化推理架构，能够有效将劳动密集型调试工作转变为高效的验证流程，在超大规模工业应用中具有显著的实际价值。

Abstract: Diagnosing mobile crashes in ultra-large-scale industrial applications is a formidable challenge due to the sheer volume of code, the complexity of mixed-language environments, and the inability to reproduce failures locally. Traditional static analysis struggles with scalability, while existing LLM-based agents often rely on reproducible environments unavailable in post-mortem scenarios. We present Holmes, a multi-agent system that automates root cause analysis by synthesizing multimodal runtime signals--stack traces, logs, and thread states--to reconstruct failure contexts without reproduction. Holmes introduces a hierarchical Retrieve-Explore-Reason architecture that leverages low-level artifacts (e.g., registers, assembly) to bridge the semantic gap between open-source business logic and closed-source system frameworks. By dynamically compressing the search space using runtime clues, Holmes precisely navigates 70-million-line codebases to identify non-local defects. Evaluated on real-world crashes from WeChat, Holmes achieves 87.6% accuracy in function-level fault localization and reduces average investigation time by over 98% (to ~77 seconds), demonstrating its effectiveness in transforming labor-intensive debugging into an efficient verification workflow.

</details>


### [175] [REBA: A Revealed Belief Automaton Framework for Online Planning in Continuous POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21971)
*Xiangwei Chen,Lingling Fang,Andreas Holzinger,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REBA（Revealed Belief Automaton）框架，通过在线认证信念空间中的“揭示事件”来构建有限信念自动机，实现连续POMDP中ω-正则规格下的在线规划，相比基线方法性能提升17%-47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续POMDP在线规划方法在处理ω-正则规格时存在局限：直接信念空间搜索缺乏符号记忆以跟踪长时逻辑进展，预定义离散抽象难以从含噪在线信念中认证。核心挑战在于如何从连续观测中在线获得可靠的符号状态。

Method: 提出REBA框架：(1) 在线揭示方法，通过信息论门控从连续信念空间中动态分析并建立信念抽象，发现含噪信念中的可靠锚点；(2) 增量拓扑适应机制，在已认证锚点上构建在线有限信念自动机；(3) 结合ω-正则规格支持形式化奇偶策略综合，指导MCTS进行超越局部视野的在线搜索；(4) 误差分解分析评估离散引导对底层连续POMDP的有效性与可靠性。

Result: 在巡逻与导航场景的实证评估中，REBA匹配或超越所有基线方法，主要指标上相比最先进方法提升17.0%至47.4%。

Conclusion: REBA将研究从全局信念空间离散化推进到在线认证揭示事件的新范式，有效解决了连续POMDP中ω-正则规格在线规划的关键难题。

Abstract: Online planning in continuous partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) using $ω$-regular specifications requires handling continuous belief dynamics within the finite symbolic memory in order to track temporal progress. Existing methods based on either direct search in belief space or predefined discrete abstractions suffer from drawbacks, e.g., lack of symbolic memory for long-horizon logical progress or difficult to certify from noisy online beliefs. As such, obtaining reliable symbolic states online from continuous observations remains a challenge. To address this issue, we introduce the Revealed Belief Automaton (REBA), an event-driven framework that advances the research from global belief-space discretization to a fundamental new way of thinking, namely online certification of revelation events. Specifically, we propose an online revelation method that, through information-theoretic gates, can dynamically analyse and establish belief abstraction from the continuous belief space by discovering reliable anchors among noisy beliefs. We then develop an incremental topology adaptation mechanism over the certified anchors to realise the online finite Belief Automaton. By combining with the $ω$-regular specification, REBA is able to support formal parity policy synthesis without a predefined discrete abstraction, which in turn can guide the Monte Carlo Tree Search process to perform online search beyond its local horizon. In addition, we design an error decomposition analysis which can assess the effectiveness and reliability of this discrete guidance for the underlying continuous POMDP. Empirical evaluations in patrolling and navigation scenarios show that REBA matches or exceeds all evaluated baselines, with primary metric gains of +17.0\% to +47.4\% over state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [176] [Human vs Machine Mathematical Difficulty on Project Euler: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21972)
*David Holmes,Johannes Schmitt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿AI系统在Project Euler数学问题上，其努力程度和成功概率如何随人类难度标度变化。通过分析3840次尝试、50个问题和26个模型配置，发现机器成本与人类时间呈幂律关系且指数b<1，同时成功概率可用指数衰减模型拟合。最强模型在最快人类基线下的50%任务长度视野约为2.5-4.3小时，SOTA的h50每约75天翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Timothy Gowers提议启发，旨在检验AI系统在数学问题上的性能随问题难度标度的规律，验证是否存在机器随难度增长比人类更差的预测。

Method: 基于MathArena基准的Project Euler问题数据集（3840次尝试、50个问题、26个模型配置），以人类解题时间衡量问题难度。检验幂律关系t_machine = a·t_human^b，以及指数衰减模型p_success = e^{c·t_human}，并采用分箱聚合和逻辑斯蒂曲线拟合方法。

Result: 20/25个模型拟合显示b<1，不支持机器随难度扩展性更差的预测；指数衰减模型得到中等支持（中位数R²=0.92）；最强配置的h50为2.5-4.3小时，SOTA h50每约75天翻倍。

Conclusion: 该操作化方式不支持机器在难度扩展上劣于人类的早期预测；AI系统在数学问题上的能力正以约75天翻倍的速度快速提升。

Abstract: We study how the effort and success probability of frontier AI systems scale with human difficulty on problems from Project Euler, an online platform of computational mathematics problems. Our dataset, from the MathArena benchmark, consists of 3840 attempts across 50 problems and 26 model configurations, with problem difficulty measured by the site's public human solve times. Motivated by a proposal of Timothy Gowers, we test a power-law relation $t_{\text{machine}} = a \cdot t_{\text{human}}^b$ between generated-token cost per successful answer and human time, and find $b < 1$ for 20 of the 25 models with usable fits, including the strongest base models; this operationalization therefore does not support an earlier prediction that machines scale worse than humans with difficulty. We also investigate whether success probability on the tested problems can be modeled by a simple exponential decay $p_{\text{success}} = e^{c t_{\text{human}}}$, predicting a linear relation between $\log p_{\text{success}}$ and $t_{\text{human}}$. Using a binning approach for data aggregation we find moderate empirical support (median bin-level $R^2 = 0.92$ across the 22 best-covered configurations) for this model. Following METR, we also fit logistic success curves and extract 50\% task-length horizons $h_{50}$; the strongest configurations in our 20 April 2026 snapshot reach roughly $2.5$--$4.3$ hours on our fastest-five human baseline, with a log-linear fit through the state-of-the-art frontier giving a descriptive doubling time of about $75$~days for the SOTA $h_{50}$.

</details>


### [177] [IRumAI: Reinforcement Learning for Indian Rummy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21975)
*Vignesh Mohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个针对印度拉米纸牌游戏的强化学习智能体IRumAI，采用PPO算法、融合感知编码、弃牌堆驱动的奖励塑造和双分支卷积架构，在仅对抗弱启发式算法训练后，能以53.9%胜率击败最强搜索型对手，且推理速度提升7000倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拉米纸牌游戏拥有庞大的玩家基础且具有复杂的隐藏信息动态特性，但此前没有任何强化学习研究涉足该领域。现有智能体依赖组合搜索方法，虽然战术上表现强劲，但推理速度慢。

Method: IRumAI集成了近端策略优化（PPO）、融合感知的观测编码、弃牌堆驱动的奖励塑造以及双分支卷积架构。先通过一次性的行为克隆（用更强的演示数据热启动），然后仅对抗弱启发式规则进行强化学习训练。

Result: IRumAI击败了整个基线层次的所有对手，包括对训练时未见的最强搜索型对手取得53.9%的胜率。推理速度仅需0.33毫秒/动作，比最先进的启发式算法快7000倍以上。消融实验验证了架构选择的有效性，线性探针实验表明网络能够从公开交互中隐式建模对手的隐藏手牌。

Conclusion: IRumAI证明了在无需显式搜索的情况下，强化学习可以高效解决印度拉米纸牌游戏中的隐藏信息问题，同时大幅提升推理速度，并且网络具有隐式建模对手隐藏状态的能力。

Abstract: Despite its massive player base and complex hidden-information dynamics, Indian Rummy has received no reinforcement learning attention. Existing agents rely on combinatorial search, which is tactically strong but slow at inference. We present IRumAI, the first RL agent for the domain. IRumAI integrates Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), meld-aware observation encoding, deadwood-driven reward shaping, and a dual-branch convolutional architecture. IRumAI is RL-trained solely against weak heuristics, after a one-time behaviour-cloning warm-start on stronger demonstration data. It generalises to defeat the entire baseline hierarchy, including a 53.9% win rate against the strongest search-based opponent unseen during RL training. Bypassing explicit search, IRumAI requires just 0.33 ms per action, which is over 7,000x faster than the state-of-the-art heuristic. Ablations validate our architectural choices, and linear probing reveals that the network implicitly models the opponent's hidden hand from public interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Towards CSI-Native Foundation Models: A Channel-Adaptive Roadmap for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20670)
*Chenyu Zhang,Xinchen Lyu,Chenshan Ren,Shuhan Liu,Qimei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向CSI原生基础模型的信道自适应框架，通过对齐预训练、位置建模和注意力控制与三大信道需求（尺度感知异构暴露、物理时频空坐标、相关约束token交互），在零样本泛化（NMSE降低>4 dB）、尺度外推（未见过天线扩展下增益5.4 dB）和推理效率（移动处理加速18.8%）上显著优于现有方法，并以仅7.01%的密集导频开销实现-18.64 dB NMSE和36.6%的频谱效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型将CSI视为任务张量而非传播条件感知的信道响应，未能捕捉无线环境固有的时频-空间几何结构，缺乏面向信道物理特性的统一预训练框架。

Method: 提出信道自适应统一框架，三个核心设计：1) 尺度感知异构暴露（scale-aware heterogeneous exposure）应对不同尺度CSI数据；2) 物理时频-天线坐标位置建模（physical time-frequency-antenna coordinates）；3) 相关约束的token交互（correlation-bounded token interaction）。

Result: 零样本泛化：跨时空频任务NMSE降低超4 dB；尺度外推：8倍未见过天线规模下增益5.4 dB；推理效率：移动感知处理加速18.8%；系统级评估：仅7.01%密集导频开销，平均NMSE -18.64 dB，净频谱效率较密集LMMSE提升36.6%、较WiFo提升15.5%。

Conclusion: CSI原生表示学习可有效支持导频高效无线接入，证明面向信道物理特性的基础模型预训练是6G无线智能化的可行路径。

Abstract: Wireless foundation models offer a path toward reusable channel state information (CSI) intelligence for sixth-generation (6G) systems. However, existing generic-backbone adaptation and CSI pretraining methods often treat CSI as task tensors rather than propagation-conditioned channel responses, thereby failing to capture the intrinsic time-frequency-spatial geometry of wireless environments. This paper presents a channel-adaptive roadmap toward CSI-native foundation models, proposing a unified framework that aligns pretraining, positional modeling, and attention control with three channel requirements: scale-aware heterogeneous exposure, physical time-frequency-antenna coordinates, and correlation-bounded token interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed framework across three dimensions: zero-shot generalization, reducing NMSE by more than 4 dB across spatial-temporal-frequency tasks; scale extrapolation, yielding up to a 5.4 dB gain under 8 times unseen antenna scaling; and inference efficiency, accelerating mobility-aware processing by up to 18.8%. A system-level evaluation with Sionna SYS further shows that the proposed framework uses only 7.01% of dense-pilot overhead, reaches -18.64 dB average NMSE, and improves average net spectral efficiency by 36.6% over dense LMMSE and 15.5% over WiFo, indicating that CSI-native representation learning can support pilot-efficient radio access.

</details>


### [179] [NeuroShield: A Device-Agnostic Foundation Model for EEG Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20673)
*Matin Fallahi,Patricia Arias-Cabarcos,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroShield是一个可复用的EEG身份认证基础模型，采用双阶段Transformer架构，能处理可变通道和可变长度的EEG信号。在15,762名受试者、28,116个session的三个公开数据集上预训练，微调后在两个未见下游数据集上将等错误率降低0.44-8.06个百分点，并支持跨通道布局和更长信号段的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG认证模型与采集设置（硬件、通道布局、信号时长）紧密绑定，导致异构记录无法统一处理，每个新设备或数据集都需要重新开发模型，造成碎片化问题，限制了多数据集学习、知识迁移和模型复用。

Method: 提出双阶段Transformer架构的NeuroShield基础模型，支持可变通道和可变长度EEG信号输入，学习身份判别性嵌入表示。在三个公开数据集上预训练，然后在未见下游数据集上进行微调迁移。

Result: 微调后，NeuroShield在等错误率上相比现有最优方法降低0.44-8.06个百分点；能泛化到比训练时更长的信号段；能在预训练未见的通道布局上运行。

Conclusion: NeuroShield是一个可复用、可适应的EEG身份编码器，能够跨异构记录设置工作，以开源方式发布以支持可复现性和社区采用。

Abstract: A central challenge in EEG authentication is that models are typically tied to the acquisition settings in which they are trained. In particular, variations in headset hardware, channel layout, and signal duration create heterogeneous recordings that existing models are not designed to handle, causing each new headset or dataset to be treated as a separate model-development problem. This fragmentation limits multi-dataset learning, hinders knowledge transfer, and reduces model reusability. To address this limitation, we present NeuroShield, a reusable foundation model for EEG authentication that learns identity-discriminative embeddings from variable-channel and variable-length EEG recordings through a dual-stage transformer architecture. We pretrain NeuroShield on three public EEG datasets comprising 15{,}762 subjects and 28{,}116 sessions, and evaluate transfer on two unseen downstream datasets. Our evaluations show that, after fine-tuning, NeuroShield reduces equal error rate by 0.44--8.06 percentage points relative to the state of the art. NeuroShield further generalizes to segments longer than those seen during training and operates across channel layouts not encountered during pretraining. These results establish NeuroShield as a reusable and adaptable EEG identity encoder across heterogeneous recording settings. We release NeuroShield as open source to support reproducibility and community adoption.

</details>


### [180] [Massive Activations Are Architecturally Robust: A Controlled Scratch/Commitment Residual Stream Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20743)
*Maruthi Vemula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ledger Residuals架构，将残差流分为可写的思考流和只读的承诺流，以检验大规模激活（massive activations）是否是残差流读写角色冲突导致的伪影。实验表明，即使有专用的解码通道，模型仍会在保护通道中重建出固定维度、集中于起始token的离群激活，且更强的稀疏惩罚反而使其更集中，证明大规模激活是功能性而非偶然性的架构鲁棒特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在关于大规模激活（massive activations）的争论：它们究竟是残差流同时承担读写角色的可移除伪影，还是模型功能不可或缺的必要组件。本文旨在通过架构干预直接检验这一伪影假说。

Method: 提出Ledger Residuals架构，将残差流拆分为两个通道：Deliberation（可变的草稿流，中间计算可自由覆写）和Commitment（受保护的、仅用于解码的承诺流，保存模型用于读取的表示）。在160M和290M规模下训练匹配损失的语言模型，观察离群激活的分布变化。

Result: 即使提供了专用的答案通道，模型仍在受保护的承诺流中重建了标准Transformer中固定维度、集中于起始token的离群激活。该重建特征幅度小于标准Transformer，但更尖锐地集中于起始token；施加更强的稀疏惩罚后，离群激活变得更持久、更集中，而非消失。

Conclusion: 大规模激活是架构鲁棒的：它们会在模型解码所使用的任何表示中重新出现，这一行为符合其具有功能性而非偶然性伪影的预期。

Abstract: Trained transformers reliably develop massive activations, a small number of hidden dimensions whose magnitude is far above the median and which concentrate on the sequence-start token. Whether these outliers are a removable artifact of the residual stream's overloaded read and write role, or instead a functional necessity, is actively debated. We test the artifact hypothesis directly, with an architectural intervention. Our architecture, Ledger Residuals, splits the residual stream into a mutable scratch stream (Deliberation) that intermediate computation may freely overwrite and a protected, decode-only accumulator (Commitment) that holds the representation the model reads out. If massive activations exist only because one stream is forced to be both scratchpad and answer, then a dedicated answer channel should remove the need for them. We find that it does not. In matched-loss language models at the 160M and 290M scales, the model rebuilds the canonical fixed-dimension, start-token outlier inside the protected channel. The rebuilt feature is smaller in magnitude than in a standard transformer but more sharply concentrated on the start token, and a stronger sparsity penalty makes it more persistent and more concentrated still, rather than removing it. Massive activations therefore look architecturally robust: they re-emerge in whichever representation the model decodes from, which is what we would expect if they are functional rather than incidental. We release our architecture and measurement code.

</details>


### [181] [CIExplainer++: Generating Causal and Interpretable Explanations for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20747)
*Francisco Caldas,Sahil Satish Kumar,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIExplainer和G2TeXplainer两个方法：前者基于因果推断（潜在结果框架）识别对GNN预测具有最大因果效应的子图；后者将因果子图转化为包含特征和关系信息的自然语言解释，以提升GNN的可解释性和人类可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释人工智能旨在通过呈现影响模型输出的关键元素来使黑箱模型更可信，但现有方法缺乏对GNN预测中真正因果影响成分的识别，且难以将子图解释转化为人类可理解的形式。

Method: CIExplainer基于潜在结果框架进行扰动分析，识别对GNN预测具有最大因果效应的子图；G2TeXplainer将因果子图转化为包含特征级和关系级信息的自然语言解释。

Result: 在多种GNN架构（GCN、GraphSAGE、GAT、GIN）和数据集上对CIExplainer进行了评估和比较。

Conclusion: CIExplainer通过因果推断方法有效识别GNN预测的关键子图，G2TeXplainer进一步弥合了子图解释与人类可解释性之间的鸿沟，实现了从结构到自然语言的解释转化。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence aims to make black-box models more trustworthy by presenting, in a human-understandable manner, the elements that lead to the model's output. This involves both (i) identifying components and connections with genuine causal influence on outputs and (ii) translating such structures into an interpretable representation. For the former, we introduce CIExplainer, a novel perturbation-based method grounded in causal inference for explaining Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). CIExplainer identifies the subgraph with the highest causal effects on GNN predictions using the Potential Outcome Framework. We evaluate and compare CIExplainer on various GNN architectures (GCN, GraphSAGE, GAT, GIN) and datasets.
  To bridge subgraph explanations with human interpretability, we further propose G2TeXplainer, a method that transforms causal subgraphs into natural language explanations that capture both feature-level and relational information.

</details>


### [182] [Evidential Fusion Network for Multimodal Survival Prediction under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20757)
*Yucheng Xing,Hailan Mo,Zi Wang,Ling Huang,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于证据理论的缺失模态生存预测模型EMMS，通过Dempster-Shafer理论和高斯随机模糊数进行多模态决策融合，在无需生成缺失数据的情况下，实现了对缺失模态的鲁棒生存预测，并在四个癌症数据集上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生存预测模型假设数据完整，但在临床实际中模态缺失频繁发生，缺乏对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用Dempster-Shafer理论和高斯随机模糊数进行多模态决策融合，同时考虑偶然不确定性和认知不确定性以及模态可靠性；将缺失模态视为空证据，避免干扰可用输入。

Result: 在四个癌症数据集上取得了最先进性能，提供了校准且可解释的不确定性估计，且未引入额外计算开销。

Conclusion: EMMS方法简单、计算高效，无需生成缺失数据，在缺失模态场景下具有鲁棒性和校准的不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: Recent multimodal survival prediction models have demonstrated strong predictive performance by leveraging complementary information across modalities. However, such models generally assume data completeness and exhibit limited robustness toward missing modalities, which are frequently encountered in real-world clinical settings. We propose the Evidential Missing Modality Survival Fusion (EMMS) model for multimodal survival prediction under missing modalities. EMMS offers a straightforward, computationally effective approach to survival analysis without requiring a generative phase for missing data. By employing Dempster-Shafer theory and Gaussian Random Fuzzy Numbers for multimodal decision fusion, it considers both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty alongside modality reliability for fusion. Moreover, the model treats missing modalities as vacuous evidence, preventing interference with available inputs and naturally reflecting increased uncertainty and calibrated predictions. Extensive experiments on four cancer datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance while providing calibrated and interpretable uncertainty estimates under incomplete multimodal observations, without introducing additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [183] [ELADO: Elliptic PDE Assessment Datasets for Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20771)
*Frank Ehebrecht,Toni Scharle,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ELADO基准测试套件，专门用于揭示和量化神经算子架构在求解椭圆型偏微分方程时的失效模式。该套件包含基于泊松方程和亥姆霍兹方程构建的多个数据集，每个数据集针对特定的困难来源（重尾解分布、谱分布偏移、频域重尾分布、输入敏感性、输入信号复杂度）进行设计。实验表明，重尾目标、谱偏移和输入敏感性会显著降低预测精度，而这些退化在标准数据集和指标（如平均相对L²误差）中可能被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子基准测试主要关注平均性能，无法系统性地揭示在椭圆型PDE问题中自然出现的各种失败模式。作者旨在构建一个能够隔离和量化这些挑战的基准套件，帮助研究者识别神经算子在重尾分布、谱偏移和输入敏感性等方面的退化问题。

Method: 通过可控的数据生成过程，基于泊松方程和亥姆霍兹方程构建多个数据集。每个数据集设计用于隔离特定的困难来源：轻尾系数场导致的重尾解分布、输入数据的谱分布偏移、轻尾系数场导致的频域重尾分布、通过经验局部Lipschitz分析量化输入敏感性、以及控制幅度归一化下输入信号复杂度对预测精度的影响。然后在所有数据集上评估多种神经算子架构。

Result: 重尾目标、谱偏移和输入敏感性均会导致神经算子预测精度的显著下降。这些退化在标准数据集和平均相对L²误差等常用指标下可能被掩盖，而ELADO基准能够有效揭示这些问题。

Conclusion: ELADO基准套件能够系统性地揭示神经算子在椭圆型PDE中的失效模式，特别是重尾分布、谱偏移和输入敏感性带来的挑战。建议研究者使用这些更具挑战性的基准来评估和提升神经算子的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce ELADO (Elliptic PDE Assessment Datasets for Operator Learning), a systematic benchmark suite constructed to show and quantify failure modes of neural operator architectures when learning solution operators of elliptic PDEs. While the benchmarks of existing datasets focus on average case performance, the ELADO datasets are constructed to highlight challenges that arise naturally in elliptic PDE problems. In particular, we construct several datasets built around Poisson's equation and the Helmholtz equation, each with non-constant coefficients. We define a controllable data-generating process to create datasets, that are designed to isolate a distinct source of difficulty. Specifically, these are (1) heavy-tailed solution distributions arising from light-tailed coefficient field distributions, (2) spectral distribution shift of the input data, (3) heavy-tailed distributions in the frequency domain of solutions, arising from light-tailed coefficient field distributions, (4) input sensitivity of learned operators, quantified by an empirical local Lipschitz analysis, and (5) the effect of input signal complexity on prediction accuracy under controlled amplitude normalization.
