Serving as a flexible "backend" for any downstream graph architecture, our pretrained encoder enhances cross-database knowledge transfer on the established RelBench benchmarks while improving learning convergence and memory footprint.

</details>


### [256] [Fast-TurboQuant: A Multiplier-Free Online Vector Quantization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21448)
*Pedro M. R. Pereira,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo,Rausley A. A. de Souza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fast-TurboQuant提出了一种免乘法的投影架构，用快速沃尔什-哈达玛变换（FWHT）替代密集随机旋转矩阵，在保持1-bit量化效果的同时，将算术复杂度降至仅需加法运算，在DBpedia数据集上实现了19.7倍的算法加速并提升了召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型扩展时，键值缓存和检索增强生成系统的内存带宽成为关键瓶颈。1-bit量化虽能缓解该约束，但现有TurboQuant依赖密集随机旋转矩阵进行投影，每次嵌入需要数百万次浮点乘法，难以部署在资源受限的边缘硬件上。

Method: 提出Fast-TurboQuant，用结构化快速Johnson-Lindenstrauss变换替代密集矩阵：先进行Rademacher相位反转，再应用快速沃尔什-哈达玛变换（FWHT）。利用亚高斯集中性质满足标量Lloyd-Max量化的前置条件，无需高斯投影，将算术复杂度从乘法降至仅加法。

Result: 在DBpedia OpenAI-3 Large嵌入数据集上，顺序执行下实现19.7倍算法加速；FWHT零填充带来的维度扩展降低了均方误差并提升了Recall@10。

Conclusion: Fast-TurboQuant通过结构化变换实现了免乘法的高效1-bit量化投影，显著降低了计算复杂度，适合边缘部署场景，同时在量化精度和检索性能上有所提升。

Abstract: As large language models scale, memory bandwidth for key-value caches and retrieval-augmented generation systems becomes a critical bottleneck. While 1-bit quantization addresses this constraint, recent TurboQuant relies on dense random rotation matrices to condition the vector distribution before quantization. This projection demands millions of floating-point multiplications per embedding, making it difficult to deploy on constrained edge silicon. We introduce Fast-TurboQuant, a multiplier-free projection architecture that replaces the dense matrix with a structured fast Johnson-Lindenstrauss transform. By applying a Rademacher phase inversion followed by a fast Walsh-Hadamard transform (FWHT), the method leverages sub-Gaussian concentration to satisfy the prerequisites of scalar Lloyd-Max quantization without Gaussian projections. This substitution reduces the arithmetic complexity to only additions, eliminating hardware multipliers. Evaluation on DBpedia OpenAI-3 Large embeddings demonstrates a 19.7 times algorithmic speedup under sequential execution. Furthermore, the dimension expansion due to the FWHT zero-padding reduces the mean squared error and improves Recall@10.

</details>


### [257] [Post-Training Speech Enhancement Language Models with Perceptual Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21458)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Antonis Asonitis,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对语音增强语言模型，提出使用组序列策略优化（GSPO）结合多指标感知奖励的后训练阶段，直接优化非可微质量指标（DNSMOS、WER、UTMOS），无需学习代理或离线偏好对，在两个自回归基模型上取得SOTA结果，且多指标奖励优于单指标变体，避免了奖励破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音增强语言模型在训练时使用离散音频令牌的token级交叉熵损失，而评估时使用感知指标（如DNSMOS、WER、UTMOS），导致训练与评估目标不一致，模型无法直接优化真正关心的质量指标。

Method: 引入后训练阶段，使用组序列策略优化（GSPO）方法，以多指标感知奖励（DNSMOS、WER、UTMOS的复合奖励）直接优化自回归语音增强语言模型，无需代理模型或离线偏好对。

Result: 在UniSE和GenSE两个自回归基模型上应用后，在DNS2020基准上取得SOTA结果；人工评估消融实验表明复合多指标奖励优于任何单指标变体，有效避免奖励破解。

Conclusion: GSPO结合多指标感知奖励的后训练是一种有效且鲁棒的语音增强语言模型优化策略，能够弥合训练与评估之间的鸿沟，多奖励联合优化相比单奖励训练更能避免奖励破解问题。

Abstract: Speech enhancement language models achieve strong results when trained on discrete audio tokens, but their optimization relies on token-level cross-entropy rather than the perceptual metrics used for evaluation. We introduce a post-training stage for autoregressive speech enhancement language models using Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) with multi-metric perceptual rewards. Our method directly optimizes non-differentiable quality metrics (DNSMOS, WER, and UTMOS) as reward signals, without learned surrogates or offline preference pairs. Applied to two autoregressive base models, UniSE and GenSE, our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on the DNS2020 benchmark. A human evaluation ablation further shows that the composite multi-metric reward is preferred over any single-metric variant, confirming that multi-reward optimization avoids the reward hacking observed with single-metric training.

</details>


### [258] [Deep Learning for Soil Moisture Estimation: Fusing Satellite Data with Optimally-Lagged Meteorological Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21475)
*Adrian Canovas-Rodriguez,Aurora González Vidal,Antonio F. Skarmeta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于互相关函数（CCF）的方法来确定气象变量与土壤水分之间的最优时间滞后（0-30天）以及地表到深层（10cm至20-50cm）的垂直水分传播滞后（0-15天），并结合遥感与气象信息，在西班牙东南部七个农业地块验证了三种深度学习架构（CNN、LSTM、CNN-LSTM混合模型）。结果表明，显式建模大气和垂直地下延迟显著提升了土壤水分估算精度，其中CNN-LSTM混合模型取得了最优整体性能（R²=0.930, CVRMSE=8.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半干旱农业区域精准土壤水分估算需要融合遥感和气象信息，同时必须考虑土壤水分对大气强迫的延迟响应，现有方法对此建模不足。

Method: 提出CCF方法确定最优时间滞后与垂直传播滞后；评估三种深度学习架构（逐像素CNN、逐帧LSTM、滑动窗口CNN-LSTM混合）在五种特征配置（从仅卫星到卫星-气象-深度全融合）下的性能。

Result: 气象变量优于仅卫星基线；深度信息对所有架构至关重要；逐像素CNN单块最优R²=0.877，七块平均R²=0.535（较基线+1.00）；CNN-LSTM混合模型整体最优R²=0.930, CVRMSE=8.0%。

Conclusion: 显式建模大气延迟和垂直地下水分传播延迟可显著提升半干旱农业区土壤水分估算精度，对精准农业具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accurate soil moisture estimation in semi-arid agricultural regions requires integrating remote sensing and meteorological information while accounting for the delayed response of soil moisture to atmospheric forcing. This study introduces a Cross-Correlation Function (CCF) methodology to determine optimal temporal lags (0-30 days) between meteorological variables and soil moisture, as well as inter-depth lags (0-15 days) describing vertical moisture propagation from the surface (10 cm) to deeper layers (20-50 cm). The approach was validated across seven agricultural plots in southeastern Spain. Three deep learning architectures, each targeting a distinct prediction granularity, were evaluated under five feature configurations ranging from satellite-only to full satellite-meteorology-depth fusion: a CNN for per-pixel estimation within each plot, an LSTM for frame-level (daily plot-mean) prediction, and a CNN-LSTM hybrid operating on sliding windows with pooled multi-patch training. Models were assessed on held-out data to measure genuine generalisation. Meteorological variables improved performance over the satellite-only baseline, while subsurface depth information proved decisive across all architectures. The per-pixel CNN achieved the strongest single-patch result (R^2 = 0.877, RMSE = 2.28), with a seven-patch average R^2 of 0.535, representing an improvement of +1.00 over the satellite-only baseline. The pooled CNN-LSTM hybrid obtained the highest overall performance (R^2 = 0.930, CVRMSE = 8.0%). These results demonstrate that explicitly modelling atmospheric and vertical subsurface delays substantially improves soil moisture estimation for precision agriculture.

</details>


### [259] [Robustness Cannot be Reduced to Regularization: Studying Adversarial Training Beyond the Linear Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21488)
*David A. R. Robin,Rafael Pinot,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对两层神经网络而言，对抗风险与简单正则化风险之间不存在形式等价关系，并通过实验表明这种不可能性在更深层架构中同样存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练计算成本高，线性模型中对抗风险与正则化风险的等价关系可大幅降低训练成本，但该等价关系能否扩展到非线性模型尚不清楚。

Method: 通过归约论证，证明两层网络中对抗风险与任何仅具有弱数据依赖性的简单正则化风险之间存在根本性分离；并在Wide-ResNets上提供实证证据。

Result: 严格证明了两层网络中不存在上述等价关系，且深层架构的实验结果支持同样的不可能性结论。

Conclusion: 对抗风险与简单正则化风险的等价性仅在线性模型中成立，无法扩展到更复杂的神经网络架构。

Abstract: The vulnerability of ML models to adversarial examples has recently emerged as a major concern. While adversarial training is one of the most effective countermeasures to this issue, its high computational cost remains an obstacle to practical deployment. Recent progress in reducing this cost has relied, in the case of linear models, on a formal equivalence between the adversarial risk and a simpler form of regularized risk. This enabled significantly more efficient training procedures, which naturally raises the question of whether such an equivalence can be extended beyond linear models. In this work, we formally show that no such equivalence is possible for two-layer networks. Our proofs proceed via a reduction to key properties that fundamentally separate the adversarial risk from any simple regularized risk which would only exhibit a weak form of data dependence. Beyond this setting, we provide empirical evidence on Wide-ResNets indicating that the same type of impossibility persists in deeper and more expressive architectures.

</details>


### [260] [Predicting High-Risk Colorectal Polyps in African Americans Using Pre-Colonoscopy Clinical Features: Machine Learning Model Development and Temporal Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21492)
*Basheer Qolomany,Mrinalini Deverapall,Adeyinka Laiyemo,Zaki Sherif,Mori Yuichi,Omer Ahmed,Hassan Brim,Hassan Ashktorab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习模型，仅基于结肠镜检查前的非侵入性特征（人口学、临床和行为数据），在多元化的非裔美国人为主的城市队列中预测高风险结直肠息肉，模型在内部和外部验证中均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结直肠息肉风险分层依赖结肠镜和病理结果，但非侵入性术前特征可能提前识别高风险患者，优化结肠镜资源配置，减少不必要的检查，并在资源有限或分配不均的环境中促进更公平的风险分层。

Method: 回顾性队列研究，利用霍华德大学医院2015-2022年4,681名患者数据（内部验证）和2023-2024年1,562名患者数据（外部验证），基于人口学、生活方式和合并症特征，构建并验证了神经网络、随机森林、SVM、朴素贝叶斯、逻辑回归、决策树、KNN和XGBoost等多种机器学习模型。高风险息肉定义为绒毛状/管状绒毛状腺瘤、高级别异型增生、息肉≥10mm或≥3个息肉/次。

Result: 基于非侵入性术前特征，多种机器学习模型能有效预测高风险结直肠息肉，并在外部验证中保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 仅使用结肠镜检查前的非侵入性特征即可实现有效的结直肠息肉风险分层，有助于在资源有限的环境中优化筛查资源配置和提升医疗公平性。

Abstract: Risk stratification for advanced colorectal polyps typically relies on colonoscopy and/or pathology findings. However, there is growing interest in whether non-invasive features available prior to colonoscopy can help identify patients at higher risk. Such approaches may enhance clinical decision-making by prioritizing surveillance for individuals most likely to harbor high-risk polyps, when colonoscopy resources are limited while potentially reducing unnecessary procedures in lower-risk patients. Importantly, the use of non-invasive, pre-procedural information may also help promote more equitable access to risk stratification, particularly in settings where colonoscopy resources are limited or unevenly distributed. We aimed to develop and externally validate machine learning models to predict high-risk colorectal polyps using only non-invasive, pre-colonoscopy demographic, clinical, and behavioral features in a diverse, predominantly African American, urban cohort.
  We conducted a retrospective cohort study using demographic, lifestyle, and comorbidity data from patients who underwent colonoscopy at Howard University Hospital to develop and validate several machine learning models, including neural networks, random forest, support vector machines (SVM), Naive Bayes, logistic regression, decision trees, k-nearest neighbors (KNN), and XGBoost, for predicting high-risk colorectal polyps. High-risk polyps (HRP) were defined as villous or tubullovillous adenomas, high-grade dysplasia, polyps >= 10 mm in size, and/or the presence of >= 3 polyps per procedure; all other cases were classified as low-risk polyps (LRP). The dataset included 4,681 patients from 2015-2022 used for internal validation and 1,562 patients from 2023-2024 used for external validation.

</details>


### [261] [Breaking chains with trees: Deep learning with $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ parallel time complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21497)
*Neeraj Mohan Sushma,Aditya Nagarsekar,Cabrel Teguemne Fokam,Robin Schiewer,Amit Kumar Pal,Anand Subramoney,David Kappel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层块局部学习(HBLL)框架，通过变分原理驱动的局部学习目标分解深度神经网络为层级链接的块，实现O(log N)并行训练复杂度，无需端到端反向传播，在视觉和语言任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决反向传播中存在的锁定问题（层间更新严格依赖、无法并行）和权重传输问题（需要对称的前向与后向通路），这些约束限制了并行性、增加了内存和通信开销，对可扩展学习构成挑战。

Method: 将深度神经网络分解为层级链接的块，每个块使用基于变分原理的局部学习目标进行训练，消除完整端到端反向传播需求的同时保持跨网络的有效信息传播。该方法首次实现O(log N)并行时间复杂度（N为网络层数），并隐式定义了对应不同层级路径的子网络族，支持不同有效层数的灵活推理。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的视觉和语言建模任务上取得竞争性表现，并扩展到循环序列架构，应用于原本依赖时间反向传播的设置。

Conclusion: HBLL通过层级化块局部学习成功突破了反向传播的并行性瓶颈，为深度神经网络的高效可扩展训练提供了新范式，同时保持了下游任务性能。

Abstract: Modern deep neural network architectures are trained via backpropagation, which requires errors to be sequentially propagated through all layers before parameters can be updated. This introduces two limitations: locking, where layer-wise updates are strictly interdependent and cannot proceed in parallel, and the weight transport problem, which requires symmetric forward and backward pathways for exact gradient computation. These constraints restrict parallelism, increase memory and communication overhead, and pose challenges for scalable learning. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Block-Local Learning (HBLL), a framework that decomposes deep neural networks into hierarchically linked blocks trained using local learning objectives derived from variational principles, eliminating the need for full end-to-end backpropagation while maintaining effective information propagation across the network. HBLL is the first algorithm that is able to train deep neural networks in $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ parallel time complexity, where $N$ is the number of network layers. We show that HBLL implicitly defines a family of subnetworks corresponding to different hierarchical paths, enabling flexible inference with different effective numbers of layers. We evaluate HBLL on a set of challenging vision and language modeling tasks, achieving competitive performance. We also extend HBLL to recurrent sequence architectures, applying to settings that otherwise rely on backpropagation through time.

</details>


### [262] [Privacy-Preserving Federated Temporal Graph Learning with Digital Twin--Guided Adaptive Deception for Cyber-Resilient IoMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21513)
*Syed Zeeshan Haider,Anwar Shah,Muneeb Arif,Hamza Iftikhar,Waqas Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦时序图卷积网络结合优势演员-评论家（Federated TGCN-A2C）隐私保护防御架构，在CICDDoS 2019和TON-IoT基准测试上分别达到99.48%和99.61%的测试准确率，支持16类和10类攻击的细粒度检测，并具备可解释性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和医疗物联网设备资源受限且受严格延迟和数据隐私法规约束，现有网络安全方案难以在保护隐私的同时高效检测多样化网络威胁。

Method: 集成四种机制：(1)基于PyG的时序GCN（GCNConv层+全局平均池化+学习型异常门）进行流级威胁分类；(2)LSTM数字孪生生成设备级异常分数，通过sigmoid耦合门控分类器；(3)联邦A2C智能体基于七维状态选择ALLOW/ISOLATE/HONEYPOT-REDIRECT动作；(4)增强蜜罐层将可疑流量转化为威胁情报。联邦聚合采用EMA平滑的客户端验证损失作为逆加权FedAvg系数，结合余弦退火学习率。

Result: CICDDoS 2019准确率99.48%、加权F1 0.9948；TON-IoT准确率99.61%、加权F1 0.9961；分别在25轮和10轮内收敛。全部16个CICDDoS 2019类别F1≥0.9237，全部10个TON-IoT类别F1≥0.9488（含严重不平衡的MITM类别）。优于Fed-Inforce-Fusion 0.21个百分点，覆盖额外三类攻击。SHAP/LIME/Grad-CAM/反事实分析验证了决策可解释性。

Conclusion: 提出的联邦时序图卷积网络架构在隐私保护、检测精度、非独立同分布鲁棒性和可解释性方面均有显著优势，适用于临床部署的监管问责需求。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of IoT and IoMT devices introduces critical cybersecurity vulnerabilities in healthcare and industrial environments where resource-constrained devices operate under strict latency and data-privacy regulations. This paper presents the Federated Temporal Graph Convolutional Network with Advantage Actor-Critic (Federated TGCN-A2C), a privacy-preserving defense architecture integrating four mechanisms: a PyG-based Temporal GCN using GCNConv layers with global mean pooling and a learned anomaly gate for flow-level threat classification; LSTM-based Digital Twins generating per-device anomaly scores gating the classifier via learned sigmoid coupling; a Federated A2C agent selecting among ALLOW, ISOLATE, and HONEYPOT-REDIRECT actions based on a seven-dimensional state capturing confidence, entropy, anomaly magnitude, and traffic composition; and an enhanced honeypot layer converting suspicious traffic into threat intelligence with adaptive thresholds. Federated aggregation employs EMA-smoothed per-client validation losses as inverse-weighted FedAvg coefficients to stabilize global model updates under non-IID distributions, with cosine-annealed learning rates per round. Evaluated on CICDDoS 2019 and TON-IoT benchmarks, the framework achieves 99.48% and 99.61% test accuracy with weighted-F1 scores of 0.9948 and 0.9961, converging within 25 and 10 federated rounds, outperforming Fed-Inforce-Fusion by 0.21 percentage points while covering three additional attack categories. All sixteen CICDDoS 2019 classes achieve F1 of at least 0.9237 and all ten TON-IoT classes achieve F1 of at least 0.9488, including the severely imbalanced MITM category. Post-hoc explainability via SHAP, LIME, Grad-CAM, and counterfactual analysis confirms decisions are grounded in semantically meaningful flow features, supporting regulatory accountability in clinical deployments.

</details>


### [263] [Towards Understanding the Power and Limits of the Muon Optimizer: A River-Valley Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21514)
*Tianqi Shen,Jinji Yang,Runze Shi,Jianhao Ma,Jiaye Teng,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个轨迹级理论框架，统一解释Muon优化器在LLM训练中的优势（早期快速收敛）和局限（后期振荡慢收敛），并建议采用两阶段策略：前期用Muon加速，后期切换为GD类优化器进行精调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献宣称Muon通过谱归一化具有理论优势，但其在LLM训练中的实证表现与Adam/AdamW相比并不稳定、对调度敏感、且非普遍优越，理论与实际存在差距，亟需弥合这一解释鸿沟。

Method: 建立混合尖峰矩阵感知模型，将感知算子分解为信号、尖峰和体分量，捕捉各向异性结构与长尾信息；引入"河谷视角"将优化景观分为河流方向（信息承载）和山丘方向（噪声/无关信息）；在无动量设置下理论分析Muon与GD的收敛差异；扩展至一般非凸目标，研究谱河流上带动量的正交化更新行为。

Result: 无动量下：Muon沿信息承载方向早期移动更快，但河底附近收敛远慢于GD；带动量下：Muon早期收敛快，但正交化更新丢失残差尺度信息，导致目标解附近过冲和振荡。初步LLM实验支持两阶段策略的有效性。

Conclusion: Muon的优势主要在早期优化阶段，其正交化更新在后期引入固有缺陷，因此不应仅依赖固定学习率调度，而应在最后阶段切换为GD类的精调优化器。

Abstract: Recently, Muon has gained substantial attention as an appealing alternative to Adam-like optimizers, with many works highlighting its advantages through spectral normalization and improved conditioning. Yet this positive theoretical narrative contrasts with its empirical performance in large language model (LLM) training, where Muon's gains over Adam/AdamW are often mixed, schedule-sensitive, and not uniformly superior. To address this gap, we develop a trajectory-level theory characterizing both the strengths and limitations of Muon. We introduce a mixed-spiked matrix sensing model whose sensing operator decomposes into signal, spike, and bulk components, capturing a mixture of anisotropic structure and long-tail information reminiscent of LLM training. On top of it, we adopted a river-valley perspective in which we view the landscape as composed of a river direction flowing to the desired solution and hill directions encoding nuisance or task-irrelevant information. In the momentum-free setting, we show that Muon moves faster along the information-bearing river direction during early optimization, but can converge much more slowly near the river bottom than gradient descent. We then extend the river-valley perspective to general nonconvex objectives with momentum by studying points on the spectral river. There, while Muon converges faster early on, its orthogonalized update removes residual scale information, making it prone to overshooting and oscillation near the target solution. Together, these results suggest that our characterizations extend beyond spiked matrix sensing and motivate switching to GD-like refinement optimizers in the final phase, rather than relying only on a fixed learning-rate schedule for Muon. We also provide preliminary evidence supporting this two-stage approach in language model training experiments.

</details>


### [264] [Backpropagating Through Simulation: Analytic Policy Gradients for Sample and Learning Efficient Differentiable Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21525)
*Yueci Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Analytic Policy Gradients (APG)，一种利用可微环境动力学通过反向传播计算精确策略梯度的方法，并在四个连续控制任务上与 PPO 进行对比评估。APG 在样本效率上显著优于 PPO，尤其是在短任务中，并通过分段反向传播方案缓解了长任务中的梯度退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习算法（如 PPO）需要大量环境交互且依赖高方差的优势估计，样本效率低下。当环境动力学可微时，可以通过反向传播直接计算精确梯度，从而提高样本效率。

Method: 提出 Analytic Policy Gradients (APG)：将环境动力学视为可微函数，通过反向传播计算回报对策略参数的精确梯度。设计了分段反向传播方案，支持 MC 和基于 Critic 的 Bootstrap 两种模式，以缓解长序列上的梯度退化问题。在与 PPO 相同的模型架构、观测归一化和优化器设置下进行对比。

Result: 在四个连续控制任务（1D点质量目标跟踪、2D点质量避障导航、2D刚体T块推、7-DOF Franka FR3末端执行器到达）上，APG 在样本效率上优于 PPO，尤其是在动力学较简单的短任务上优势明显。分段反向传播方案有效缓解了长任务中的梯度退化。

Conclusion: APG 利用可微环境动力学实现精确梯度计算，显著提升了样本效率，是对传统无模型强化学习的有效替代方案，尤其适用于动力学已知或可学习的场景。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning algorithms such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) treat the environment as a black box, estimating policy gradients from sampled rewards; this process demands millions of interactions and relies on high-variance advantage estimates. When environment dynamics are differentiable, the return is an end-to-end differentiable function of the policy parameters, enabling exact gradient computation via backpropagation through simulation. We term this approach Analytic Policy Gradients (APG) and evaluate it against PPO on four continuous control tasks of increasing dynamical complexity: a one-dimensional point-mass target-reaching task, a 2D point-mass navigation task with obstacle avoidance, a 2D rigid-body T-block pushing task, and a 7-DOF Franka FR3 end-effector reaching task. Both algorithms share identical model architectures, observation normalization, and optimizer settings. To decouple sample efficiency from compute efficiency, we design a multi-axis evaluation protocol that records performance against environment steps and gradient steps. We report a segmented backpropagation scheme with MC and critic-based bootstrap modes that mitigates gradient degradation on long-horizon tasks, and present ablations over segment length and bootstrap strategy.

</details>


### [265] [Memory Is No Longer a Bottleneck: Memory-Efficient Graph Filtering for Scalable Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21540)
*Jin-Duk Park,Won-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mem-GF，一种基于Krylov子空间的内存高效图滤波协同过滤方法，在不显式存储物品相似度图的情况下近似多项式图滤波器，实现最高5.74倍内存降低和4.38倍加速，同时超越现有图滤波和GCN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练图滤波协同过滤方法虽避免了繁重的模型训练，但需要存储完整的物品相似度图，导致大规模数据集上的内存开销过高，限制了实际应用。

Method: 利用Krylov子空间结构作为核心机制来近似多项式图滤波器，避免显式存储物品相似度图；并从理论上分析了保证无损近似所需的最小Krylov子空间大小。

Result: 与现有方法相比，Mem-GF实现最高5.74倍内存降低、4.38倍运行速度提升，推荐准确率持续超越最先进的图滤波和GCN方法，并能稳健扩展到数千万交互规模的数据集。

Conclusion: Mem-GF是一种兼具理论保证和实际可行性的高效协同过滤解决方案，有效解决了图滤波方法的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Graph convolutional networks (GCNs) have demonstrated significant success in capturing complex user-item relationships for collaborative filtering (CF). However, due to their reliance on extensive model training, training-free graph filtering (GF)-based CF methods have emerged as a promising alternative, offering computational efficiency by smoothing graph signals via matrix operations. In particular, polynomial GF-based approaches demonstrate improved accuracy through their ability to design more expressive and flexible filtering functions. Despite these advantages, existing GF methods suffer from a critical memory bottleneck: they necessitate storing the full item similarity graph, incurring prohibitive memory costs for large-scale datasets, which limits their practical applicability. To tackle this challenge, we propose Mem-GF (Memory-efficient GF), a new GF-based CF method that departs from conventional designs by principally leveraging the structure of Krylov subspaces as a core mechanism for approximating polynomial graph filters without explicitly storing the item similarity graph. We theoretically analyze the minimum Krylov subspace size that guarantees lossless approximation. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that Mem-GF achieves up to 5.74$\times$ lower memory usage and 4.38$\times$ speedup in runtime, while consistently exceeding the recommendation accuracy of state-of-the-art GF and GCN-based methods. Mem-GF robustly scales to datasets with tens of millions of interactions, establishing itself as a practically viable and theoretically grounded solution for efficient CF.

</details>


### [266] [LIG: Layer-wise Integrated Gradients for Within-Layer Flow Analysis in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21564)
*Eight Suzuki,Hideitsu Hino,Noboru Murata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LIG（分层积分梯度）方法，通过在Transformer各层模块边界（ATT和MLP）应用集合到集合的积分梯度，实现了对模型内部信息流动的细粒度归因分析，并验证了其在BERT-base和PTB上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型内部计算过程不透明，现有可解释性方法难以在模块边界粒度上分析层内信息流动和归因一致性。

Method: 将每层Transformer建模为动态图，节点为token表示和每头注意力输出，模块边界为ATT和MLP。在非线性模块边界上应用LIG，通过L2标量化将积分梯度从标量目标扩展至集合到集合映射，并沿LRP风格逐层组合贡献。

Result: 在BERT-base和PTB上，最佳配置为：ATT基线使用目标token的嵌入，MLP基线使用ATT输出（a=0）或零向量；该方法能够在模块边界粒度上分析层内一致性和分离ATT/MLP的信息流。

Conclusion: LIG是一种无需模型特定重训练或逐操作解释器设计的模块边界粒度XAI诊断工具，可有效分析Transformer内部信息流动。

Abstract: Transformers achieve strong performance, but their internal computations remain opaque. We view each Transformer layer as a dynamic graph whose nodes are token representations and per-head attention outputs, with Multi-Head Attention (ATT) and MLP as module boundaries. On this graph we use LIG (Layer-wise Integrated Gradients), which applies set-to-set Integrated Gradients (IG) at nonlinear module boundaries. Set-to-set IG applies IG to a map from a set of input token representations to a set of output representations, evaluating token-to-token contributions, which is not standard in prior IG applications. This extends IG from the usual scalar-objective setting to set-to-set maps via an L2 scalarization, and composes within-layer contributions in the spirit of Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP), with IG completeness playing the role of LRP-style conservation at each boundary. We use LIG to analyze (i) the agreement between module-wise composition and layer-whole attribution under an L2 criterion, and (ii) within-layer information flow by tracing separated ATT and MLP contributions. On BERT-base and PTB, configurations that best preserved within-layer consistency used the target token's embedding as the ATT baseline and either the ATT output at a=0 or Zero as the MLP baseline. We therefore present LIG as a diagnostic XAI tool at module-boundary granularity, without model-specific retraining or per-operation interpreter design. Code is available at https://github.com/eightsuzuki/layer-wise-integrated-gradients.

</details>


### [267] [The Cost Geometry of Belief: finite-resource inference under noisy observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21585)
*Laurent Caraffa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在信念空间上引入了一种基于最优传输和Fisher信息共形加权的代价几何（Wasserstein-Fisher几何），证明了三个核心不变性质：推断拒绝确定性的“墙”、诚实（程函）代价导致与Fisher信息成比例的几何、“刚性”表明这些几何是双曲的且高斯分布是极值分布。代价单位的改变仅缩放几何而不改变这些不变性，表明只有相对代价有意义。热力学为这一框架提供了物理动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信念空间的几何（如Fisher-Rao度量）未能反映从一种信念过渡到另一种信念的“代价”。本文旨在通过最优传输（Wasserstein距离）结合Fisher信息的共形加权，构建一种更符合物理直觉的代价几何，以刻画有限智能体通过噪声传感器维持数字孪生时的信念演化成本。

Method: 将Wasserstein空间中的最优传输代价与Fisher信息（精度代价）进行共形加权，定义了一种新的代价几何（区别于Fisher-Rao度量）。通过分析该几何在位置-尺度族上的共形类，推导出三个不变性质（墙、诚实、刚性），并利用热力学固定代价单位。

Result: (1) 墙：当代价主导Fisher信息时，推断拒绝确定性趋于无穷远距离；(2) 诚实：程函代价（每纳特等长）选择与Fisher信息成比例的几何；(3) 刚性：这些几何是双曲的，Stam界表明高斯分布是最双曲的位置-尺度信念。代价单位缩放不改变上述不变性，仅相对代价有意义，曲率值-1/4是其中一个像。

Conclusion: 信念空间的代价几何由热力学固定单位后具有普适的不变性质，确定性的代价存在几何下界并在趋于确定时发散。该框架揭示了信息几何与物理代价之间的深层联系，为数字孪生和有限感知系统的信念建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We equip the space of beliefs with a cost geometry (what it costs to pass from one belief to another): optimal transport in Wasserstein space, reweighted conformally by Fisher information (the price of the precision at stake), distinct from the Fisher-Rao metric. In the setting we consider, a finite machine maintains a digital twin of a system; observing the territory through finite, noisy sensors, we model its coherent output as a belief: a probability density over states, the Bayes posterior. Certainty (the perfect twin) is denied twice, by observation and by physics, both read off the Fisher information. On the conformal class, essentially location-scale, three results emerge, all invariants of one change of cost unit. A wall: a well-posed inference rejects certainty to infinite distance as soon as the cost dominates the Fisher information (necessity conjectured beyond power laws). An honesty: an honest (eikonal) cost, each nat the same length everywhere, selects the geometries proportional to the Fisher information. A rigidity: these geometries are hyperbolic, and the Stam bound crowns the Gaussian, the most hyperbolic location-scale belief. Changing the unit dilates the geometry yet preserves the wall, the curvature ordering, and the extremality of the Gaussian: an absolute cost says nothing, only relative cost carries meaning, the value -1/4 being one of its images. The cost of reaching a given precision then has a geometric floor diverging at certainty. Thermodynamics fixes the cost unit and motivates this framework; the results are geometric, in nats.

</details>


### [268] [FAST: A Framework for Aligned Sampling and Training in Parallel Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21587)
*Bonan Wang,Letian Tao,Bin Shuai,Jiaxin Gao,Wenxin Zhao,Wei Xiong,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对闭环自动驾驶中并行采样存在的掉队者效应问题，提出FAST框架，通过动态并行采样对齐（DPSA）和缩放掩码填充优化（SMPO），实现同步并行采样加速，在保持统计无偏性的前提下取得至少1.78倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在闭环自动驾驶中面临严重的采样效率瓶颈，标准并行采样虽可缓解但存在掉队者效应——单个环境提前终止迫使整个批次同步重置，导致样本利用率低下和重置延迟过高。

Method: 提出FAST同步并行框架：(1) 动态并行采样对齐（DPSA），通过虚拟延续扩展终止轨迹以保持向量化同步，并根据并行片段终止率动态触发全局截断；(2) 缩放掩码填充优化（SMPO），利用有效性掩码和自适应损失归一化消除辅助填充数据带来的偏差。

Result: 相比单片段基线，FAST实现至少1.78倍墙钟时间加速，且保持统计无偏性。

Conclusion: FAST有效解决了并行采样中的掉队者效应瓶颈，在保证理论一致性的前提下显著提升闭环自动驾驶强化学习的采样效率。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning is pivotal for closed-loop autonomous driving yet remains constrained by severe bottlenecks in sampling efficiency. Standard parallel sampling mitigates this but suffers from the straggler effect, where the premature termination of a single environment necessitates a synchronized batch re-initialization, leading to suboptimal sample utilization and prohibitive re-initialization latency. To address this, we propose FAST, a synchronous parallel framework tailored for closed-loop simulation. Specifically, FAST employs Dynamic Parallel Sampling Alignment (DPSA) to maintain vectorization synchronization by extending terminated episodes via virtual continuation, thereby decoupling the sampling loop from individual terminations. By dynamically triggering global truncation based on the termination rate of parallel clips, FAST effectively eliminates the bottleneck of premature resets without sacrificing data diversity. Furthermore, to strictly preserve theoretical consistency, we incorporate a Scaled Mask-Padding Optimization (SMPO) that leverages validity masking and adaptive loss normalization to nullify the bias from auxiliary padding data. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FAST achieves at least a 1.78 times wall-clock speedup over the single-clip baseline while preserving statistical unbiasedness.

</details>


### [269] [Geometric and Information Compression of Representations in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21593)
*Linara Adilova,Henning Petzka,Asja Fischer,Bernhard C. Geiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过条件熵瓶颈网络和连续dropout网络，系统研究了深度神经网络中互信息（MI）与几何压缩（类簇化）之间的关系，发现两者呈负向非线性关系，且随训练设置变化可能反转，并提出泛化能力是二者关系的潜在混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的潜在表示可从信息论（互信息）和几何（压缩程度）两个维度刻画，但两者之间的关系尚不明确。核心开放问题是：低互信息是否必然意味着几何压缩，反之亦然。

Method: 使用类级聚类作为几何压缩的度量，在条件熵瓶颈（CEB）网络和连续dropout网络中采用理论上可靠的互信息估计方法，在分类任务中通过受控噪声注入方案评估互信息、几何压缩与泛化之间的相互作用。

Result: 低互信息与几何压缩之间不存在可靠的对应关系；两者呈负向非线性关系，且在不同训练设置下会反转；泛化能力是二者关系的潜在混杂因素，而非直接因果结果。

Conclusion: 互信息与几何压缩之间的关系比通常假设的更为微妙，泛化在其中扮演混杂角色而非直接产物。

Abstract: Deep neural networks transform input data into latent representations that support a wide range of downstream tasks. These representations can be characterized along information-theoretic and geometric dimensions, but their relationship remains poorly understood. A central open question is whether low mutual information (MI) between inputs and representations necessarily implies geometrically compressed latent spaces and vice versa. We investigate this question using class-wise clustering as a measure of geometric compression and theoretically sound MI estimation in conditional entropy bottleneck (CEB) networks and continuous dropout networks. We evaluate the interplay between MI, geometric compression, and generalization on classification tasks under controlled noise injection schemes. Our findings show that low MI does not reliably correspond to geometric compression, and that the connection between the two is more nuanced than often assumed. Indeed, our experiments reveal a negative and nonlinear relationship that can reverse when varying training setup. Our results put forward a hypothesis that generalization acts as a potential confounder in this connection rather than being their direct consequence.

</details>


### [270] [Learning to Place Guards by Reinforcement: A Geo-Free Neural Policy for the Vertex-Guard Art Gallery Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21604)
*Domagoj Ševerdija,Jurica Maltar,Nathan Chappel,Domagoj Matijević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经组合优化（NCO）在顶点守卫艺术画廊问题上的表现，发现强化学习训练的编码器已内化了解决可行性所需的几何知识，但解码器校准不足导致失败；通过冻结编码器并附加简单分类器，可大幅缩小可行性差距，表明探针方法能有效评估NCO模型真正学到的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCO研究关注策略能构建强解，但对其实际学到什么（而非解码器表达了什么）认识不足。作者希望区分编码器内化知识与解码器表达能力，以定位NCO模型失败的根本原因。

Method: 在顶点守卫艺术画廊问题上，训练基于指针网络的策略，采用覆盖感知奖励和自滚动的强化学习，并施加"无几何推理"约束（测试时仅见顶点坐标，无可见性计算）。随后冻结训练好的编码器，附加一个小型单射分类器（仍无几何推理），在编码器嵌入上直接预测是否覆盖所有区域。

Result: 原始策略部署守卫经济但存在大量未覆盖多边形（分布外差距扩大）。而冻结编码器+分类器的方法在分布内外、高达约5倍训练范围内，将未覆盖多边形数量减少约一个数量级，同时明确报告守卫数量的代价。

Conclusion: 强化学习训练的表示已编码了解可行性所需的几何信息，残余失败源于解码器校准而非缺失知识。探针冻结编码器是评估神经组合求解器内化知识的实用方法。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) has shown that policies trained by reinforcement can construct strong solutions to NP-hard problems directly from raw instances. What such a policy actually learns, as opposed to what its decoder expresses, remains much less clear. We study this distinction on the vertex-guard Art Gallery Problem, the NP-hard task of choosing polygon vertices from which to observe an entire region. A pointer-network policy is trained from a coverage-aware reward over its own rollouts under the constraint we call geo-free inference: at test time it sees only vertex coordinates, with no visibility computation and no geometric oracle. The policy places guards economically but leaves a tail of under-covered polygons that widens far beyond the training range. To locate the cause, we freeze the trained encoder and read its embeddings with a small single-shot classifier, still geo-free at inference. The classifier closes most of the feasibility gap, in and out of distribution and at up to roughly five times the training range, cutting under-covered polygons by about an order of magnitude at an explicitly reported cost in guard count. We read this as evidence that the reinforcement-trained representation already encodes the geometry required for feasibility, and that residual failures reflect decoder calibration rather than missing knowledge. Probing a frozen encoder thus offers a practical way to ask what a neural combinatorial solver has internalized.

</details>


### [271] [The Two-Hump Problem: Bridging the Difficulty Gap in Mathematical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21611)
*Lucas Fagan,Michele Tarquini,Ali Shehper,Maksymilian Manko,Angus Gruen,Coco Huang,Giorgi Butbaia,Davide Passaro,Sergei Gukov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强化学习在数学搜索问题中面临的稀疏奖励和巨大搜索空间挑战，以Andrews-Curtis猜想为例，识别了"双峰分布"结构障碍，并通过数据生成技术和算法增强（超移动、Transformer架构）填补难度缺口，显著提升性能，并发布两个大规模公开基准数据集AC-19和AC-1M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学搜索问题中，强化学习面临巨大搜索空间和稀疏奖励的挑战。以Andrews-Curtis猜想为例，现有的AC问题实例呈现"双峰分布"——要么极易解决，要么几乎不可能，缺乏中间难度的"困难但可解"实例，这构成了有效学习的结构性障碍。

Method: 提出两条主要途径：1）新颖的数据生成技术，用于填充难度缺口（即生成更多中等难度的实例）；2）算法层面的增强，包括引入超移动（supermoves）和基于Transformer的架构。

Result: 相比以往基线方法，性能有显著提升。同时发布了两个大规模基准数据集：AC-19（包含125,192个长度不超过19的AC平凡表示，难度各异）和AC-1M（包含1,136,154个长度不超过30的困难AC平凡表示），这是该领域首个大规模公开数据集。

Conclusion: 通过数据生成和算法增强有效克服了AC问题中的双峰分布障碍，大幅提升了强化学习在数学搜索问题上的表现，并为后续研究提供了公开基准。

Abstract: Mathematical search problems present a unique challenge for Reinforcement Learning (RL) due to vast search spaces and sparse rewards. In previous works, the Andrews-Curtis (AC) conjecture was established as an illustrative example of such problems. In this work, we identify a critical structural barrier in the AC landscape: a "Two-Hump" distribution, where problem instances are either trivially solvable or effectively impossible, with a scarcity of intermediate "hard-but-solvable" instances required for effective learning. We tackle this challenge through two primary avenues: novel data generation techniques to populate the difficulty gap, and significant algorithmic enhancements including the introduction of supermoves and Transformer-based architectures. We demonstrate substantial performance improvements over previous baselines, and release new comprehensive benchmark datasets including AC-19 (125,192 AC-trivial presentations of varying difficulty with length at most 19) and AC-1M (1,136,154 hard AC-trivial presentations of length at most 30), the first large-scale, publicly available datasets of this kind.

</details>


### [272] [HERALD: High-Throughput Block Diffusion LLM Serving via CPU-GPU Cooperative KV Cache Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21633)
*Omin Kwon,Doyeon Kim,Jongseok Park,Seung Yul Lee,Ion Stoica,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD是一个面向块级扩散LLM的KV缓存卸载系统，通过利用块内去噪步骤间KV条目一致性，仅需一次性选择top-k条目即可在整个块内复用，将选择计算量降低块大小倍，并实现选择与去噪过程重叠，在5-10% KV预算下实现近乎无损的精度，较纯GPU推理延迟降低1.59倍、吞吐量提升2.47倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散LLM虽然能提升GPU利用率，但其KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，导致长上下文场景下的吞吐瓶颈。将KV缓存卸载到主机DRAM虽可缓解显存压力，但PCIe带宽有限，只能召回稀疏的KV子集。

Method: 利用块级扩散LLM中同一块内去噪步骤间相关KV条目保持一致的特性，一次性选择top-k条目并在整个块的所有去噪步骤中复用，从而将选择计算量降低块大小倍，并使选择过程与去噪过程重叠。

Result: 在三个块级扩散LLM和五个长上下文任务上，HERALD在5-10% KV预算下实现近乎无损的准确率，每块延迟降低1.59倍，吞吐量提升2.47倍，且加速比随上下文长度增长而增加。

Conclusion: HERALD通过块级复用策略有效解决了块级扩散LLM的KV缓存卸载难题，在有限的PCIe带宽下实现了高精度稀疏注意力，显著提升了长上下文场景的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) improve GPU utilization over autoregressive decoding by generating multiple tokens per forward pass, but their KV cache still grows linearly with context, limiting throughput at long contexts. KV cache offloading to host DRAM alleviates this memory pressure, but the limited PCIe bandwidth necessitates recalling only a sparse subset of KV entries. In block dLLMs, the relevant KV entries remain consistent across denoising steps within a block, enabling high-accuracy selection by identifying the top-k entries once and reusing them throughout all denoising steps. This property appears attractive for offloading as it amortizes the selection overhead across the entire block, but it requires exact attention over the full KV cache, which is too expensive under offloading. We present HERALD, a KV offloading system for block dLLMs that resolves this through two opportunities that reduce the required selection compute by a factor of the block size and enable selection to be overlapped with denoising. Across three block dLLMs and five long context tasks, HERALD achieves near-lossless accuracy at 5-10% KV budget and up to 1.59x lower per block latency and 2.47x higher throughput over GPU-only inference, with speedups growing with context length.

</details>


### [273] [A new classification method based on Minimum Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21639)
*Julio González-Díaz,Beatriz Pateiro-López,Iria Rodríguez-Acevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小生成树的监督学习分类算法，并引入鲁棒且计算高效的改进版本，通过仿真和真实飞机轨迹数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小生成树在无监督学习（尤其是聚类）中已得到广泛应用，但其在监督学习中的潜力尚未被充分探索。本文旨在研究MST在监督学习中的应用，提出一种分类算法。

Method: 提出基于最小生成树的分类算法，并引入鲁棒版本以提高性能和计算效率。

Result: 通过广泛的仿真研究和涉及飞机轨迹的真实案例研究评估了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于最小生成树的监督学习方法在分类任务中有效，且鲁棒版本在性能和计算效率上均有提升。

Abstract: Minimum Spanning Trees have been used in unsupervised learning, particularly in clustering tasks, due to their ability to recognize clusters by removing edges that are considered inconsistent in defining those clusters. This paper aims to study the use of Minimum Spanning Trees in supervised learning. Specifically, we propose a classification algorithm based on Minimum Spanning Trees. To improve its performance, we introduce a robust version of the method that is also computationally more efficient. We evaluate the effectiveness of our proposed method through an extensive simulation study. We also apply the proposed methodology to a real-world case study involving aircraft trajectories.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Towards Fewer Control Laws via Continuous-Time Multiparametric Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21031)
*Lida Lamakani,Efstratios N. Pistikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续时间的多参数规划框架，直接应用庞特里亚金极大值原理（PMP）求解最优控制问题，无需离散化模型。与离散时间方法相比，该方法产生的关键区域数量大幅减少且固定不变，且能显式给出切换时间作为初始状态的函数，从而降低在线计算成本。PAROC框架案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间多参数规划方法在时间离散化时面临两难：区间过少则模型精度不足，区间过多则问题规模、决策变量和关键区域数量急剧增长，导致离线预处理和在线查询成本高昂。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 直接对连续时间最优控制问题应用庞特里亚金极大值原理（PMP），无需时间离散化，将最优控制律恢复为初始状态的显式闭式函数，同时推导出切换时间的显式表达式。

Result: 连续时间方法相比离散时间方法产生更少的关键区域，且该数量不随精度要求变化而增长，仅取决于问题本身的结构。切换时间作为初始状态的显式函数可直接用于在线控制，跳过不必要的计算。

Conclusion: 连续时间多参数规划是传统离散时间方法的一种严谨替代方案，能够降低离线与在线计算负担，PAROC框架案例验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Multiparametric programming offers a powerful solution to the computational burden of solving optimal control problems repeatedly online. By solving the problem once offline, it yields the optimal control laws as explicit closed-form functions of the initial system state, reducing online execution to a direct evaluation with no iterations required. Most existing works build this idea on a discrete-time foundation, slicing the time horizon into intervals and applying KKT conditions to the resulting algebraic system. This discretization forces a tradeoff: too few intervals and the model fails to capture the true system dynamics, while too many cause the problem size, the number of decision variables, and the number of critical regions to grow rapidly, making both offline preparation and online lookup increasingly expensive. This work develops a multiparametric framework that works directly with the continuous-time problem. Pontryagin's Maximum Principle (PMP) is applied without any model discretization, and the optimal control is recovered as an explicit function of the initial state. Compared to the discrete-time formulation, the continuous-time approach produces substantially fewer critical regions, and this number remains fixed regardless of accuracy requirements, since it reflects the structure of the problem itself rather than a discretization grid. The framework also yields the switching times as explicit functions of the initial state, directly exposing when and how the optimal control structure changes over the horizon. Knowing these switching times in advance allows the real-time controller to skip unnecessary computations between them, further reducing the online execution cost. Results from a PAROC framework case study demonstrate that the continuous-time multiparametric approach is a rigorous alternative to the conventional discrete-time formulations.

</details>


### [275] [DUET: Decentralized Bilevel Optimization without Lower-Level Strong Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21153)
*Zhen Qin,Zhuqing Liu,Songtao Lu,Yingbin Liang,Jia Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET算法，通过引入递减二次正则化消除了双层优化中对下层强凸性的依赖，实现了去中心化环境下的双层优化，并证明了其在放松假设下的KKT驻点收敛复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化双层优化(DBO)方法大多依赖下层强凸性(LLSC)来保证唯一解和良定义的超梯度，这限制了其在许多不满足LLSC的实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出DUET算法，在下层目标函数中引入递减二次正则化项以消除对LLSC的依赖；采用单循环结构；引入梯度跟踪机制以处理数据异质性。

Result: DUET在放松假设下实现了$O(1/T^{1-5p-\frac{11}{4}τ})$的近似KKT驻点收敛迭代复杂度，其中$p$和$τ$分别为下层学习率和平均化的控制参数。

Conclusion: DUET是首个在去中心化设置下无需LLSC且处理数据异质性的DBO方法，数值实验验证了理论结果和实际有效性。

Abstract: Decentralized bilevel optimization (DBO) provides a powerful framework for multi-agent systems to solve local bilevel tasks in a decentralized fashion without the need for a central server. However, most existing DBO methods rely on lower-level strong convexity (LLSC) to guarantee unique solutions and a well-defined hypergradient for stationarity measure, hindering their applicability in many practical scenarios not satisfying LLSC. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a new single-loop DBO algorithm called diminishing quadratically-regularized bilevel decentralized optimization (DUET), which eliminates the need for LLSC by introducing a diminishing quadratic regularization to the lower-level (LL) objective. We show that DUET achieves an iteration complexity of $O(1/T^{1-5p-\frac{11}{4}τ})$ for approximate KKT-stationary point convergence under relaxed assumptions, where $p$ and $τ$ are control parameters for LL learning rate and averaging, respectively. In addition, our DUET algorithm incorporates gradient tracking to address data heterogeneity, a key challenge in DBO settings. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tackle DBO without LLSC under decentralized settings with data heterogeneity. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of our proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [276] [A Multi-Objective Approach to Curriculum-Based Course Timetabling with Continuity Across Semesters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21176)
*Florian Meier,Thomas Stidsen,Clemens Thielen,Andreas Wiese*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多目标混合整数规划方法，用于解决课程表编排问题，平衡讲师与学生的偏好，同时维持学期间的课表连续性，并通过lexicographic ε-constraint方法探索目标间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目标或加权求和方法会掩盖利益相关者偏好之间的内在冲突，需要一种能明确分离讲师和学生目标并保持课表连续性的多目标方法。

Method: 采用多目标混合整数规划（MOMIP）建模，通过限制课程时间分配变动（扰动）数量来保持连续性，并基于lexicographic ε-constraint方法求解，生成覆盖不同目标空间区域的代表性解集。

Result: 在慕尼黑工业大学施特劳宾校区的真实数据上，发现讲师与学生目标之间存在明确且一致的权衡；允许的扰动数量是关键参数，放宽约束可显著提升双方课表质量，但存在边际递减效应。

Conclusion: 该方法能生成多样化的优化课表，为实际排课提供决策支持，支持利益相关者权衡分析和课表连续性的透明分析。

Abstract: We study a curriculum-based university course timetabling problem in which the preferences of two key stakeholder groups - lecturers and students - must be balanced while maintaining continuity across semesters in a weekly repeating timetable. While existing approaches typically rely on single-objective formulations or aggregate multiple objectives into a weighted sum, this can obscure the underlying trade-offs between conflicting stakeholder preferences. We therefore propose a multi-objective mixed-integer programming approach that explicitly separates lecturer and student objectives and incorporates timetable continuity by limiting the number of changes, called perturbations, in the time period assignments of selected courses relative to the corresponding semester of the previous academic year. To explore the resulting trade-offs, we develop a multi-objective solution approach based on the lexicographic $\varepsilon$-constraint method, enabling the computation of a representative set of solutions whose images, i.e., their vectors of objective values, cover different regions of the objective space.
  The approach is evaluated on real-world instances from the Straubing Campus of the Technical University of Munich. The computational results reveal a clear and consistent trade-off between lecturers' and students' objectives across all instances. Moreover, the number of allowed perturbations is identified as a key decision parameter: relaxing this constraint significantly improves timetable quality for both stakeholder groups, although diminishing returns are observed beyond certain thresholds.
  Overall, the proposed approach provides decision support by generating a diverse set of optimized timetables and enabling a transparent analysis of stakeholder trade-offs and continuity for the practical timetable planning process.

</details>


### [277] [Restart and Adaptive Acceleration in Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21354)
*Ali Elhishi,Chistophe Roux,Alexandre d'Aspremont*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑弱凸随机优化问题中的重启策略，利用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz不等式实现加速收敛，并证明重启方案对KŁ指数误指定具有鲁棒性，在玩具问题和大语言模型训练上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非光滑弱凸随机优化中，现有方法收敛速度有限。作者希望利用KŁ不等式，通过重启策略来加速收敛，并解决实际中KŁ指数未知的问题，实现自适应优化。

Method: 在满足KŁ不等式的非光滑弱凸随机优化问题中引入重启机制；分析重启调度对收敛率的影响；推导最优重启策略与Polyak步长的等价关系；理论证明重启方案对KŁ指数误指定的鲁棒性。

Result: 重启策略可显式利用KŁ指数实现加速收敛，最优重启调度可得到类似于Polyak步长的学习率；重启方案对KŁ指数的误指定具有近似自适应的鲁棒性；在可控KŁ指数的玩具问题和LLM训练中均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 重启策略能有效利用KŁ结构加速非光滑弱凸随机优化，且在实际中无需精确知道KŁ指数即可达到近自适应效果，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We study restart schemes in stochastic optimization problems for non-smooth and weakly convex that satisfy a Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz inequality. We show that using restarts allows us to leverage the KŁ inequalities to achieve improved rates of convergence, with acceleration depending explicitly on the KŁ exponent. Furthermore, optimal restart schedules lead to learning-rates akin to Polyak steps for SGD. While regularity constants such as the KŁ exponent are typically unknown in practice, we prove that restart schemes are robust to a significant misspecification of these constants, hence nearly adaptive. We detail numerical experiments on both toy problems, where the KŁ exponent is controlled, and training of Large Language Models (LLMs).

</details>


### [278] [Finite-Horizon Portfolio Choice, Labor Supply, and Early Retirement under Borrowing Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21382)
*Gugyum Ha,Junkee Jeon,Jihoon Ok*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在借贷约束下，具有劳动供给灵活性和不可逆提前退休选项的有限期最优消费与投资问题，通过对偶鞅方法转化为零和停时-奇异控制博弈，并用变分不等式求解最优策略，数值分析揭示了劳动供给灵活性对消费、投资和退休时机的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实经济中，个体面临借贷约束、劳动供给灵活性以及不可逆退休决策的多重约束，现有模型未能同时纳入这些因素。本文旨在建立统一框架，刻画这些因素如何共同影响最优消费、投资、劳动供给和退休时机。

Method: 采用对偶鞅方法将原始问题转化为零和停时-奇异控制博弈；通过带障碍和梯度约束的min-max抛物型变分不等式刻画对偶值；证明变分不等式的最大强解是唯一允许的强解，其梯度约束自由边界（绑定边界）随时间单调递增；通过验证论证识别强解与博弈值，并利用对偶恢复最优策略。

Result: 得到了变分不等式的最大强解存在的唯一性及其自由边界的单调性；通过验证论证建立了对偶博弈值与变分不等式解之间的等价关系；数值分析恢复了值函数和最优策略，并展示了劳动供给灵活性对消费、投资组合和退休时机在借贷约束下的影响。

Conclusion: 劳动供给灵活性显著影响个体的消费、投资决策和退休时机选择，尤其在借贷约束环境下；所提出的对偶方法框架有效解决了含不可逆退休期权和劳动供给灵活性的多维最优控制问题。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon optimal consumption and portfolio problem with labor supply flexibility and an irreversible early retirement option under a borrowing constraint. The agent chooses consumption, risky investment, and leisure before retirement, while after retirement labor income disappears and leisure is fixed at its maximal level. Preferences are described by a Cobb--Douglas utility, and wealth must remain nonnegative.
  {Using a dual martingale method, we transform the primal problem into a zero-sum stopper--singular-controller game. The associated dual value is characterized by a min--max parabolic variational inequality with obstacle and gradient constraints. We show that the maximal strong solution of the resulting variational inequality is the unique admissible strong solution whose gradient-constrained free boundary, namely the binding boundary, is monotone increasing in calendar time. A verification argument then identifies this strong solution with the value of the stopper--singular-controller game, and duality recovers the optimal retirement, consumption, leisure, and portfolio policies.}
  The numerical analysis recovers the value function and optimal policies, and illustrates how labor supply flexibility affects consumption, portfolio choice, and retirement timing under borrowing constraints.

</details>


### [279] [Unified Ergodic Primal-Dual Gap Rates with Unhalved Primal Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21482)
*Sirong Dai,Ming Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一阶原始-对偶方法，引入残差-对偶间隙转移原理，在未减半的原始步长τ<2/L下证明了O(1/K)遍历原始-对偶间隙界，适用于Condat-Vũ、PD3O、AFBA/PDDY和PAPC/PDFP²O等方法，并给出了一个二维反例说明完全分离的矩形步长条件无法成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准间隙率证明通常施加减半的光滑步长条件τ≤1/L，而收敛理论允许更大的范围τ<2/L。本文旨在弥合这一差距，证明在未减半步长下仍可获得O(1/K)的遍历原始-对偶间隙界。

Method: 引入残差-对偶间隙转移原理：通过Lyapunov函数的下降来控制单步间隙不等式中的正残差项，从而在算法依赖的乘积条件下，为Condat-Vũ、PD3O、AFBA/PDDY和PAPC/PDFP²O等方法建立未减半原始步长τ<2/L下的遍历原始-对偶间隙界。

Result: 在τ<2/L及各自算法依赖的乘积条件下，证明了O(1/K)的遍历原始-对偶间隙界。同时构造了二维反例，表明完全分离的矩形条件τ<2/L且τη‖A‖²<4/3在一般三函数设置下无法成立。

Conclusion: 残差-对偶间隙转移原理统一地扩展了多种一阶原始-对偶方法的步长范围至未减半情形，同时揭示了完全分离步长条件的固有限制。

Abstract: We study ergodic primal-dual gap rates for first-order primal-dual methods applied to \[
  \min_x f(x)+g(x)+h(Ax), \] where $f$ is smooth and convex, $g$ and $h$ are proper, closed, convex functions, and $A$ is linear. Standard gap-rate proofs often impose the halved smooth-stepsize condition $τ\le 1/L$, even though the corresponding convergence theory allows the larger range $τ<2/L$. We introduce a residual-to-gap transfer principle: positive residual terms in the one-step gap inequality are controlled by the decrease of a Lyapunov function. This yields $O(1/K)$ ergodic primal-dual gap bounds with the unhalved primal stepsize $τ<2/L$ for Condat--Vũ, PD3O, AFBA/PDDY, and PAPC/PDFP$^2$O, under their algorithm-dependent product conditions. We also give a two-dimensional counterexample showing that the fully separated rectangle $τ<2/L$, $τη\|A\|^2<4/3$ cannot hold in the general three-function setting.

</details>


### [280] [A note on the convergence guarantees of RLT-based algorithms for polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21483)
*Alejandro Barros-González,Julio González-Díaz,Brais González-Rodríguez,Iria Rodríguez Acevedo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文指出了多项式优化中RLT（重构-线性化技术）基础结果中的一个数学疏忽，并论证了虽然通过添加一个微小且自然的假设即可轻松恢复原结果的正确性，但忽视这一细微差别可能导致基于RLT的算法失去收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并纠正RLT基础结果中的一个数学疏忽，该疏忽若未被注意可能导致RLT算法失去收敛保证。

Method: 通过对RLT基础结果进行数学分析，指出错误所在，并论证添加一个微小且自然的假设即可恢复正确性。

Result: 发现原结果存在数学疏忽，但可通过添加一个微小且自然的假设轻松修正。

Conclusion: 修正后的结果保持了RLT算法的收敛保证；但对疏忽的不了解可能导致算法失效。

Abstract: This paper identifies and addresses a mathematical oversight in one of the foundational results on the Reformulation-Linearization Technique (RLT) for polynomial optimization. We then argue that, although correctness of the original result can be easily recovered by adding a minor and natural assumption, not being aware of this nuance may lead to the loss of convergence guarantees in RLT-based algorithms.

</details>


### [281] [Reference-Free, Long-Horizon Trajectory Optimization for Aggressive Autonomous Driving in Milliseconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21486)
*Prayag Sharma,Jonathan Y. M. Goh,Franck Djeumou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无参考轨迹和热启动条件下，利用全车辆动力学实时求解长时域、无参考最优控制问题的建模与求解器设计选择。通过分析车辆刚度特性、积分方法选择、非线性求解器策略及初始猜测生成方法，实现了在260米时域上低至55毫秒的求解时间，并能在高速避障场景中自动生成包含漂移的可行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作很少量化无参考、无热启动条件下生成长时域动态可行轨迹的计算需求，且常使用低保真模型，牺牲了精度和控制能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索在极限驾驶条件下实时求解全车辆动力学最优控制问题的可行方案。

Method: 1) 分析车辆刚度特性，论证OCP积分方案的选择；2) 比较不同积分方法，发现低阶A-稳定方法性能最优，求解时间差异可达两个数量级；3) 研究障碍参数更新策略及Hessian矩阵不定性的保护技术对非线性求解器性能的影响；4) 提出基于动态平衡的初始猜测生成方法，将初始不可行性降低四个数量级，实现实时性能。

Result: 在260米时域上实现了低至55毫秒的计算时间，包括高速避障场景，其中漂移作为可行轨迹生成的必要组成部分自然出现。高保真BeamNG模拟验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过合理的模型和求解器设计选择，可以在无参考轨迹和热启动条件下，利用全车辆动力学实时求解长时域最优控制问题，为自主车辆在极限条件下的安全运行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must generate long-horizon and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time-even when operating at the limits of vehicle handling-to ensure safe operation in adverse conditions. However, existing work rarely quantifies the computational demands of generating such trajectories without prior references, warm starts and often defaults to low-fidelity models, compromising accuracy and control authority. We investigate the modeling and solver design choices that enable real-time solution of long-horizon, reference-free optimal control problems (OCPs) using full vehicle dynamics. To this end, we analyze vehicle stiffness properties to justify the OCP's integration scheme and show that lower-order A-stable methods consistently outperform alternatives, with solve time differences reaching two orders of magnitude. We show that robust nonlinear solver performance hinges on understanding barrier parameter update strategies and safeguarding techniques for Hessian indefiniteness, inherent in some interior point methods. Lastly, we propose a computationally efficient method for generating initial guesses using dynamic equilibrium, unlocking real-time performance and reducing initial infeasibility by up to four orders of magnitude. Extensive benchmarking and high-fidelity BeamNG simulation demonstrate compute times as low as 55 ms over a 260 m horizon, including high-speed obstacle avoidance scenarios where drifting emerges as a necessary component of feasible trajectory generation.

</details>


### [282] [Accelerated and Stable Convergence with Anchored Optimistic Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21528)
*Motahareh Sohrabi,Jianxin You,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Eduard Gorbunov,Gauthier Gidel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出GOMA（广义乐观锚定方法）家族，用于求解单调变分不等式。在确定性设置下，该方法在平方梯度范数上实现了最优加速的末位迭代率$O(1/k^2)$；在随机设置下（方差无界），其单次查询变体实现了$O(1/\sqrt{k})$的末位迭代率，这是首个无需方差缩减或增大批量的无约束随机单调Lipschitz变分不等式保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法（如外梯度法）每次迭代需要两次梯度查询，限制了其在在线和随机场景下的分析与适用性。现有方法在随机单调变分不等式上的末位迭代收敛保证不足，特别是在无约束、方差无界的设定下。

Method: 提出广义乐观锚定方法（GOMA），将双时间尺度的乐观更新与Halpern迭代启发的锚定项相结合。在确定性设置中采用完整算法，在随机设置中提出简化单次查询变体。

Result: 确定性：平方梯度范数上末位迭代率$O(1/k^2)$，达到最优加速。随机性（无界方差，无方差缩减/增大批量）：平方梯度范数上末位迭代率$O(1/\sqrt{k})$，为首个此类保证。

Conclusion: GOMA在单调Lipschitz变分不等式上取得了显著的末位迭代收敛加速，并首次在具有挑战性的随机无约束设定中提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study first-order methods for solving monotone variational inequalities arising in min-max optimization. Classical approaches such as the extragradient method rely on two gradient queries per iteration, which limits their analysis and applicability in the online and stochastic settings. We propose a family of Generalized Optimistic Methods with Anchoring (GOMA), which combine two-time-scale optimistic updates with an anchoring term inspired by Halpern iteration. In the deterministic setting, GOMA achieves the optimal accelerated last-iterate rate $O(1/k^2)$ on the squared gradient norm for monotone Lipschitz operators. In the stochastic setting with unbounded variance, a simplified single-call variant of GOMA achieves a last-iterate convergence rate of $O(1/\sqrt{k})$ on the squared gradient norm. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first such guarantee for stochastic monotone Lipschitz variational inequalities in the unconstrained setting without variance reduction or growing batches.

</details>


### [283] [On Fixed-Time Stability of Continuous Dynamics for Non-Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21573)
*Sina Arefizadeh,Kunal Garg,Angelia Nedić*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非单调变分不等式（NMVI）问题，提出了两种新的动力学系统，分别实现解集的指数稳定性和固定时间稳定性，并针对有约束和无约束两种情况给出了理论保证与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于NMVI的算法研究主要集中在渐近收敛性分析，缺乏对指数稳定性和固定时间稳定性的理论保障。本文旨在填补这一空白，为NMVI问题提供更快的收敛保证。

Method: 1）针对无约束NMVI，在非单调映射梯度的温和假设下，构造了第一个动力学系统实现指数稳定性，并构造了第二个动力学系统实现固定时间稳定性（收敛时间与初始条件无关）。2）针对有约束NMVI，采用连续时间版本的Korpelevich方法实现指数稳定性，并引入新的缩放因子实现固定时间稳定性。

Result: 所提出的两类动力学系统分别在无约束和有约束NMVI问题上实现了指数稳定性和固定时间稳定性，数值仿真验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为NMVI问题的快速收敛提供了新的动力学框架，并简要讨论了离散化变体的行为及未来工作方向。

Abstract: Non-monotone variational inequalities (NMVI) are an important class of problems that generalize non-convex optimization and have various applications in optimization theory, machine learning, game theory, and economics, among others. Most existing work on NMVIs focuses on the asymptotic convergence of algorithms proposed to solve these problems. In this paper, we tackle the problems of exponential and fixed-time stability of the solution set of a class of NMVIs for both unconstrained and constrained problems. We first present novel conditions guaranteeing exponential stability of solutions to unconstrained NMVIs for a uniquely constructed dynamical system under mild assumptions on the gradient of the non-monotone map. Then, under similar assumptions, we construct another novel dynamical system whose equilibrium point is fixed-time stable, i.e., the trajectories reach the equilibrium within a fixed time, independent of the initial conditions. For the case of constrained NMVIs, we employ a continuous-time variant of the Korpelevich method for exponential stability of the solution set, and provide a novel scaling factor in the dynamics to achieve fixed-time stability. We illustrate the efficacy of the proposed modified dynamical systems through numerical simulations and conclude the paper with a brief note on the behavior of the discretized variant of the proposed dynamics and on further work that remains to be done.

</details>


### [284] [Nonsmooth Obstacles and Killed Resolvents in Reflected Stochastic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21575)
*Louis Shuo Wang,Jiguang Yu,Ye Liang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究象限中具有非光滑最大型收益的两维反射扩散的最优停时问题，提出测度值变分公式化，证明价值函数具有 killed-预解式表示，并在显式单调性假设下刻画了自由边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常使用无限制预解公式，但对于具有非光滑收益（存在kink对角线）的反射扩散问题，该公式一般无效。本文旨在建立正确的价值函数表示并刻画自由边界。

Method: 采用测度值变分公式化方法，将停时增益Γ表示为带符号的Radon测度，其中奇异分量支撑在kink对角线上并显式计算。利用 killed-预解算子给出价值函数表示，并在单调性假设下通过killed势迹条件刻画自由边界。

Result: 证明了价值函数具有V=G-R_r^C Γ的 killed-预解式表示，纠正了无限制预解公式的错误；在显式单调性假设下，停集具有外延形式，自由边界由killed势迹条件表征；给出了验证定理以确认局部Lipschitz候选边界的最优性。

Conclusion: 本文为具有非光滑收益的反射扩散最优停时问题提供了严格的变分框架，所建立的 killed-预解表示和自由边界刻画具有理论和应用价值。

Abstract: We study an infinite-horizon optimal stopping problem for a normally reflected two-dimensional diffusion in the quadrant with nonsmooth max-type payoff \(G(x_1,x_2)=x_1\veeαx_2\). The main novelty is a measure-valued variational formulation: the stopping gain \(Γ=c+rG-\mathcal LG\) is shown to be a signed Radon measure whose singular component is supported on the kink diagonal \(\{x_1=αx_2\}\), and this component is computed explicitly. We prove that the value admits the killed-resolvent representation \[
  V=G-R_r^{\mathcal C}Γ, \] where the reflected diffusion is killed upon entry into the stopping set. This corrects the generally invalid unrestricted-resolvent formula. Under explicit monotonicity hypotheses, the stopping set has epigraph form, and the free boundary is characterized by a killed-potential trace condition. A verification theorem certifies locally Lipschitz candidate boundaries as optimal.

</details>


### [285] [Convergence Analysis of Muon-type Methods with Inexact LMO in the Degenerate Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21581)
*Xun Qian,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon型优化方法在不精确LMO下的退化情况（动量最小奇异值可趋近于零），提出了新的假设，并在逐层(L0,L1)-平滑假设下建立了非凸和星凸（含权重衰减）两种情形的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Muon方法分析通常假设非退化情况（动量最小奇异值有正下界），而实际中退化情况更常见；Shulgin等人的分析揭示了不精确性与最优步长、动量之间存在耦合，但仅处理了非退化情形。本文旨在填补退化情形下的理论空白。

Method: 针对退化情况下不精确LMO带来的挑战提出新的假设，在逐层(L0,L1)-平滑假设下，分别对一般非凸情形和带权重衰减的星凸情形建立收敛分析。

Result: 在退化情形下，为Muon类方法建立了非凸和星凸（含权重衰减）两种情形下的收敛率保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次在理论层面处理了Muon方法在退化情形下的不精确LMO问题，扩展了其收敛性分析范围，为实际应用提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Muon-type methods have demonstrated potentially superior performance over Adam and its variants, and have shown hyperparameter transferability across model sizes when specific norms are chosen for the LMO in deep architectures. However, while the LMO is solved approximately via iterative algorithms in practice, most convergence analyses consider the ideal case where the search direction is the exact solution to the LMO. Recently, the inexact Muon update was analyzed by Shulgin et al. [2025], which reveals a fundamental coupling between the inexactness and the optimal step size and momentum. However, the convergence is guaranteed for the non-degenerate case only, i.e., the smallest positive singular value of the rescaled momentum is assumed to be bounded below by some positive constant when the spectral norm is used. In this work, we investigate Muon-type methods with inexact LMO in the degenerate case, where the smallest positive singular value of the rescaled momentum can approach zero, for the general non-convex case and the star-convex case with weight decay. Novel assumptions are proposed to address the challenges posed by inexact LMO in such degenerate scenarios, and convergence rates are established under the layer-wise $(L^0, L^1)$-smooth assumption for both cases.

</details>


### [286] [Nonlinear Guidance for Arrival Time and Arrival Angle Control Using Trajectory Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21643)
*Kun Wang,Andrés Infante Adrián*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于常速固定翼无人机到达时间和角度控制的非线性轨迹塑形制导策略，通过四阶多项式参数化视线角，并开发了两阶段求解方法：首先通过近似建立解析暖启动，然后精化求解一维非线性方程，最终作为原始二维非线性系统的初始猜测。数值仿真表明该方法在高度非线性场景下接近开环最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在高度非线性场景下难以生成可行解，而直接求解强耦合的非线性积分方程具有挑战性，因此需要一种高效的求解策略。

Method: 采用四阶多项式参数化视线角，将非线性制导问题转化为求解两个耦合的非线性积分方程。开发两阶段求解过程：第一阶段通过近似建立两个制导参数的线性关系，推导标量二次方程获得第一个参数的初始猜测；第二阶段求解一维非线性方程精化该猜测，最后将其作为原始二维非线性系统的初始猜测。

Result: 数值仿真表明，在测试案例中，即使在一些现有方法无法生成可行解的高度非线性场景下，所获得的解也非常接近开环最优解。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段求解策略能够有效解决强耦合非线性积分方程，在高度非线性场景下仍能获得接近最优的解，优于现有方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a nonlinear trajectory shaping guidance strategy for arrival time and angle control of a constant-speed fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle. The look angle is parameterized by a fourth-order polynomial. The nonlinear guidance problem is transformed into solving two coupled nonlinear integral equations with respect to two unknown guidance parameters. Directly solving these equations is challenging due to the strong coupling between the parameters. To address this, a two-stage solution procedure is developed. In the first stage, an analytical warm start is constructed. Specifically, by applying approximations, a linear relationship between the two guidance parameters is obtained. With this relationship, a scalar quadratic equation in the first guidance parameter is derived, leading to a good initial guess for the first guidance parameter. In the second stage, this initial guess is refined by solving a one-dimensional nonlinear equation. The obtained solution is finally used as the initial guess for solving the original two-dimensional nonlinear system of equations. Numerical simulations demonstrate that for the test cases, the obtained solution is very close to the open-loop optimal solution, even in highly nonlinear scenarios where some existing methods fail to generate a feasible solution.

</details>


### [287] [Optimal Control Problem with Mixed Control and State Constraints for Cancer Chemotherapy and Treatment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21765)
*David Lassounon,Aziz Belmiloudi,Mounir Haddou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究通过优化化疗药物浓度控制侵入性肿瘤细胞密度的问题，构建了带有混合控制-状态约束的最优控制模型，利用非线性反应扩散方程描述药物对肿瘤进展的影响，通过数学分析、最优性条件推导及肺部恶性肿瘤消除的数值模拟验证了策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化疗的成功取决于药物递送策略的有效性及其在杀伤癌细胞的同时最小化对健康组织损伤的能力，因此需要优化化疗药物的控制策略以最小化侵入性肿瘤细胞的密度。

Method: 将化疗药物浓度作为控制变量，建立带有混合控制与状态约束的最优控制问题；使用非线性反应扩散方程建模药物对肿瘤进展的影响；先进行初边值问题的数学分析，再推导最优性的一阶必要条件，最后通过数值模拟验证。

Result: 推导出了最优控制问题的一阶必要条件，并通过恶性肺肿瘤消除的数值仿真展示了所提控制策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优控制策略能够有效实现化疗药物的优化递送，在控制侵入性肿瘤细胞密度方面具有良好效果。

Abstract: The success of chemotherapy depends on the effectiveness of the drug delivery strategy and its ability to destroy cancer cells while minimizing damage to healthy tissues. The main objective of this work is to minimize the density of invasive tumour cells by controlling the chemotherapeutic agents. For this, we address an optimal control problem with mixed control and state constraints. The concentration of chemotherapeutic drugs is represented as a control variable. We use a nonlinear reaction-diffusion equation to describe the effect of drugs on the progression of invasive tumours. We start with the mathematical analysis of this initial boundary value problem. Then, we formulate the optimal control problem, explain the role of different constraints, and derive first-order necessary conditions of optimality. Finally, in order to demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed strategy, numerical simulations in the case of the eradication of malignant lung tumours, are presented and analysed.

</details>


### [288] [Local-to-Global Exactness of SDP Relaxations for Sparse QCQPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21823)
*Masakazu Kojima,Sunyoung Kim,Naohiko Arima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏二次约束二次规划（QCQP）问题，研究了其半定规划（SDP）松弛的精确性判定问题。通过利用数据矩阵的聚合稀疏性模式图的弦扩展的最大团，建立了基于团变量的SDP松弛公式，并提出了一种局部到全局的精确性认证框架。主要定理表明，若由全局最优解确定的局部子SDP是精确的，则原始SDP松弛也是精确的。在任意两个不同团至多交于一个节点的假设下，给出了三类可融入该框架的局部QCQP：凸局部QCQP、符号模式条件刻画的局部QCQP以及约束数量有限的可分离局部QCQP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏QCQP问题广泛存在于信号处理、通信、机器学习等领域，其SDP松弛的精确性（即能恢复原问题的最优解）是理论分析与算法设计的核心问题。现有研究多基于全局结构，缺乏利用问题稀疏性进行局部化分析的统一框架。本文旨在开发一种基于团分解的局部到全局方法，为验证SDP松弛精确性提供系统化工具。

Method: 1) 利用弦扩展图的极大团对稀疏QCQP进行SDP松弛的团变量重构；2) 引入局部子SDP概念，包含两个参数：局部右端向量和一致性矩阵（指定重叠团变量共享条目的值）；3) 提出局部到全局精确性认证定理：全局最优解确定的局部子SDP若精确，则全局SDP松弛精确；4) 在任意两团至多交于一个节点的图假设下，识别三类可融入框架的局部QCQP（凸、符号模式、可分离带有限约束）。

Result: 建立了基于团分解的SDP松弛精确性的局部到全局判定定理；给出了三类满足框架条件的局部QCQP结构；通过示例展示了不同局部QCQP类在稀疏问题中的组合应用。

Conclusion: 本文提出的局部到全局框架为稀疏QCQP的SDP松弛精确性提供了系统的认证工具，将复杂全局问题分解为可独立验证的局部子问题。未来工作可探索更一般的图结构条件以及更多类型的局部QCQP类。

Abstract: We study exact semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation for a given sparse quadratically constrained quadratic program (QCQP). The SDP relaxation is exact if, whenever it has an optimal solution, it admits a rank-at-most-one optimal solution that corresponds to an optimal solution of the QCQP. Using the maximal cliques of a chordal extension of the aggregate sparsity pattern graph of the data matrices, we formulate the SDP relaxation in terms of clique-wise matrix variables and develop a local-to-global framework for certifying exactness. For each clique-wise matrix variable, we introduce a local sub-SDP with two parameters: a local right-hand-side vector and a consistency matrix specifying the values of entries shared by overlapping clique-wise matrix variables. In the main theorem, these parameters are determined by an optimal solution of the global clique-wise SDP. The theorem shows that if the resulting local sub-SDPs are exact, then the original SDP relaxation is exact. Under the additional assumption that any two distinct cliques intersect in at most one node, we present three classes of local QCQPs that can be incorporated into this framework: convex local QCQPs, local QCQPs characterized by sign-pattern conditions, and separable local QCQPs with a limited number of constraints. Examples illustrate how these different local QCQP classes can be combined in sparse QCQPs.

</details>


### [289] [PhiBE-Q-Learning: Bridging Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning and Continuous-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21925)
*Yuhua Zhu,Yutong Ren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间强化学习（CTRL）中离线策略方法缺失的问题，提出了新的状态-动作价值函数定义及其控制方程，并基于PhiBE近似设计了模型已知和模型未知两种场景下的迭代算法，在线性函数逼近下证明了收敛性并给出了显式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典离散时间RL中的状态-动作价值函数Q(s,a)在连续时间强化学习中不存在，导致离线策略学习无法直接应用；现有的连续时间控制方法虽提供瞬时优势函数q(s,a)，但依赖状态价值函数V(s)，限制了离线策略方法的开发。

Method: (1) 提出连续时间RL中状态-动作价值函数的新定义并推导其控制方程；(2) 基于PhiBE近似，在模型已知（model-based）和模型未知（model-free）两种设定下设计迭代算法，仅利用离散时间离线策略数据进行Q函数近似；(3) 在线性函数逼近框架下进行理论分析。

Result: 在线性函数逼近条件下，建立了所提算法的收敛性保证，并推导出了显式的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过重新定义连续时间RL中的Q函数并设计相应的迭代算法，成功实现了仅基于离散时间离线策略数据的连续时间强化学习，为CTRL中的离线策略方法提供了理论基础和算法框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an off-policy method for continuous-time reinforcement learning (CTRL), where the system dynamics are governed by an unknown stochastic differential equation (SDE) and only discrete-time trajectory data are available. A central challenge is that the classical state-action value function $Q(s,a)$, which enables off-policy learning in discrete-time RL, does not exist in CTRL (Baird, 1994; Jia and Zhou, 2023; Tallec et al., 2019). On the other hand, continuous-time control provides local notions such as the instantaneous advantage function $q(s,a)$, but these typically rely on state value function $V(s)$. To address this, we introduce a new definition of the state-action value function in CTRL and derive its governing equation.
  Building on the PhiBE approximation (Zhu, 2024; Zhu et al., 2025), we propose iterative algorithms to approximate the optimal $Q$-function in both model-based and model-free settings using only discrete-time off-policy data. Under linear function approximation, we establish convergence guarantees and derive explicit convergence rates for the proposed method.

</details>


### [290] [Active learning-based Bayesian optimization in the realm of copper slag-blended cement systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21948)
*Debadri Som,Rayna Maheshwari,Mathijs Schuurmans,Panagiotis Patrinos,Yiannis Pontikes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用贝叶斯优化（BO）在极少实验数据条件下加速低碳辅助胶凝材料（SCMs）的配合比优化，仅需10组初始实验和2-6次迭代即可识别出满足强度要求且CO₂排放低于500 kg/吨的帕累托最优配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验方法需要大量测试才能优化低碳辅助胶凝材料的配合比，周期长、成本高，亟需一种高效的数据驱动方法以加速低碳水泥配方的研发部署。

Method: 采用高斯过程代理模型，结合Expected Improvement和Upper Confidence Bound采集函数进行贝叶斯优化；以铜渣、石灰石和波特兰水泥体系为对象，优化2天抗压强度、成本和CO₂排放三个目标；采用保守更新策略（利用不确定性边界估算28天强度）避免等待28天实验周期。

Result: 在2-6次迭代内识别出满足强度要求（2天>10 MPa，28天>32.5 MPa）且CO₂排放低于500 kg/吨的帕累托非支配解；线性核在预测精度上优于非线性核，但径向基函数（RBF）核在主动学习的不确定性量化方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化在严重数据约束条件下是一种实用的水泥研究决策支持工具，能够显著加速低碳水泥配合比优化过程。

Abstract: Accelerated mix design optimization is critical for deploying low-carbon supplementary cementitious materials (SCMs) because traditional experimental approaches require extensive testing campaigns. The authors demonstrate that Bayesian optimization (BO) can identify near-optimal blended cement formulations using an AI-driven approach with minimal experimental data. Starting with only 10 initial experiments and a 2:1 data-to-variable ratio reflecting realistic laboratory constraints, the authors optimized copper slag, limestone, Portland cement systems for 32.5N strength class with respect to 2-day compressive strength, cost, and CO2 emissions. Gaussian process surrogate models guided sequential experimentation through Expected Improvement and Upper Confidence Bound acquisition functions. Within 2-6 iterations, BO identified Pareto non-dominated solutions meeting strength requirements (greater than 10 MPa at 2 days, greater than 32.5 MPa at 28 days) while achieving CO2 emissions below 500 kg CO2/ton; consistent with industry decarbonization targets. A conservative update strategy incorporating uncertainty bounds for 28-day strength enabled rapid iteration without waiting 28 days per cycle. Comparative analysis revealed that linear kernels outperformed nonlinear alternatives in predictive accuracy, though radial basis function kernels were preferred for active learning due to superior uncertainty quantification. This work demonstrates BO as a practical decision-support tool for cement research under severe data constraints.

</details>


### [291] [Optimal Macroitem Sequences in the Precedence Constrained Knapsack Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22018)
*Valerio Dose,Fabio Furini,Marco Locatelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了带优先约束的背包问题（PCKP）的线性规划松弛结构，提出了宏项（macroitem）和可行序列的概念，证明了最优LP解可由宏项的最优序列完全刻画，并针对优先图为森林的特殊情况设计了O(n²)和O(n log n)的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解PCKP的线性规划松弛的最优解结构，为设计更高效的精确算法或近似算法提供理论基础。

Method: 引入宏项（macroitem）和宏项可行序列的概念，将物品集按优先关系划分；利用参数化拉格朗日函数刻画容量约束的断点，并通过宏项内可行流对最优对偶解进行组合刻画。

Result: 证明了最优LP解由宏项的最优序列完全刻画：物品按宏项利润权重比的非增顺序打包，最多一个宏项分数包含；拉格朗日函数的断点与宏项的最优序列中的利润权重比一致；针对森林优先图设计了O(n²)算法，对内向树或外向树改进至O(n log n)。

Conclusion: 揭示了PCKP线性规划松弛的优美组合结构，为后续算法设计提供了新视角，并在特殊图结构上取得了计算效率的显著提升。

Abstract: The Precedence Constrained Knapsack Problem (PCKP) asks for a maximum-profit subset of items, subject to a knapsack capacity constraint and precedence constraints encoded by a directed acyclic graph. We study the structure of optimal solutions of the Linear Programming (LP) relaxation of the natural Integer Linear Programming formulation of the PCKP. We introduce the notion of macroitem and of feasible sequence of macroitems, which partitions the item set while respecting the precedence structure. We establish that an optimal LP solution is fully characterized by the optimal sequence of macroitems: items are packed in nonincreasing order of the profit-to-weight ratio of their macroitem, with at most one macroitem fractionally included. We further show that the breakpoints of the parametric Lagrangian function of the capacity constraint coincide with the profit-to-weight ratios of the macroitems in the optimal sequence, and provide a complete combinatorial characterization of optimal dual solutions in terms of a feasible flow within each macroitem. Finally, for the special case in which the precedence graph is a forest, we devise an O(n^2) algorithm to compute the optimal sequence, which improves to O(n log n) for in-trees or out-trees, where n denotes the number of items.

</details>


### [292] [Information Design under Uncertain Utilities: Probabilistic and CVaR Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22157)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究当设计者不了解代理人支付系数时的信息设计问题，提出了校准贝叶斯相关均衡（Cal-BCE）概念，在LQG结构下将其转化为凸二阶锥和半定规划问题，并通过15个行业ETF实验验证了概率约束设计与CVaR约束设计之间 welfare 与尾部风险的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCE假设设计者完全知晓代理人支付系数，但在实际中设计者常面临结构性不确定性（对支付系数缺乏精确知识），需要建立能在此类不确定下保持激励相容的信息设计框架。

Method: 1）提出Cal-BCE，引入校正策略（corrector policy）以维持设计者结构不确定性下的激励相容性，并推广启示原则；2）在LQG结构下，将非凸设计问题分别转化为带概率约束和CVaR约束的凸二阶锥及半定规划；3）证明Hadamard可逆性条件保证可行性；4）建立联合去中心化定理，分析两类约束对动作协方差的约束差异。

Result: 概率设计与CVaR设计均在各自可行性阈值下运行，CVaR设计对跨代理人动作协方差约束更紧；15个行业ETF实验表明概率设计实现更高的平均福利，CVaR设计提供更好的尾部保护，两者互不支配。

Conclusion: 在设计者面临结构性不确定时，Cal-BCE提供了可行的信息设计框架，概率约束与CVaR约束代表了福利与稳健性之间的基本权衡，选择取决于校准的具体标准和决策者对尾部风险的偏好。

Abstract: This paper studies information design when the designer lacks precise knowledge of agents' payoff coefficients. The Calibrated Bayes Correlated Equilibrium (Cal-BCE) is introduced as a solution concept that augments the Bayes correlated equilibrium with a corrector policy preserving incentive compatibility under the designer's structural uncertainty, adapting its revelation principle to this setting. The design problem is nonconvex in general, but under a linear-quadratic-Gaussian structure it admits convex second-order cone and semidefinite reformulations under two-sided probabilistic and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) constraints, with feasibility guaranteed by a Hadamard invertibility condition. A joint decentralization theorem shows that both designs cap cross-agent action covariances, the CVaR design more tightly at a common tolerance; but because the formulations operate at design-specific feasibility thresholds, the realized ordering is calibration-dependent. Experiments on fifteen sector ETFs confirm the trade-off: the probabilistic design attains higher mean welfare and the CVaR design better tail protection, with neither dominating outright.

</details>


### [293] [Ensemble Feedback Methods for Families of Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22192)
*Michael Schönlein,Fabian Wirth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致参数集上一族有限维线性系统的反馈镇定问题，提出乘法反馈算子解决经典系综反馈无法修改本质谱从而无法镇定无穷维系统的问题，证明了常Hermite指标下的Heymann引理、极点配置定理和镇定结果，并研究了点态可控性与系综可控性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系综反馈诱导紧控制算子，无法修改描述自由运动的相关乘法算子的本质谱，这在无穷维设定下阻碍了系统的镇定。为此需要引入新的反馈机制。

Method: 提出乘法反馈算子替代经典系综反馈；针对常Hermite指标系统证明Heymann引理的类似形式、极点配置定理；分析点态可控性与系综可控性的关系；对非常Hermite指标系统在参数集结构附加假设下推导相应结果。

Result: 建立了常Hermite指标系统的Heymann引理类似物、极点配置定理和镇定结果；阐明了点态可控性与系综可控性之间的联系；在额外假设下推广至非常Hermite指标情形。

Conclusion: 乘法反馈算子有效克服了经典系综反馈在无穷维镇定中的局限性，为参数化线性系统族的系综镇定提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We consider feedback stabilization for one-parameter families of finite-dimensional linear systems over compact parameter sets in the complex field. Classical ensemble feedback induces compact control operators and therefore cannot modify the essential spectrum of the associated multiplication operators describing the free motion of the system. This precludes stabilization in many infinite-dimensional settings. To address this issue, multiplication feedback operators are introduced.
